Q. No. Question SubjectAnswer, Difficulty and Nature Your Answer and MarksExplanation
1

Consider the following statements about the Harappan script:

I. Harappan inscriptions are generally short and occur on objects such as seals, sealings and other artefacts.

II. The script remains undeciphered, and no long inscription comparable to contemporary Egyptian or Mesopotamian texts has been discovered.

III. Some Harappan signs occur repeatedly and appear in recurring combinations.

Which of the following inferences can be drawn from the above statements?

1. Statement I suggests that the script was used in more than one material or functional context.

2. Statement II suggests that the absence of lengthy texts and a deciphered bilingual inscription has made reconstruction of the Harappan language difficult.

3. Statement III conclusively establishes that the Harappan script represented an alphabetic writing system.

Select the answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 2 and 3 only

(c) 1 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

हड़प्पाई लिपि के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. हड़प्पाई अभिलेख सामान्यतः संक्षिप्त होते हैं तथा मुहरों, मुहरों की छापों और अन्य पुरावस्तुओं पर प्राप्त होते हैं।

II. हड़प्पाई लिपि को अभी तक पढ़ा नहीं जा सका है। इसके अतिरिक्त, समकालीन मिस्री अथवा मेसोपोटामियाई अभिलेखों की भाँति कोई विस्तृत अभिलेख भी प्राप्त नहीं हुआ है।

III. हड़प्पाई लिपि में कुछ चिह्नों का बार-बार प्रयोग हुआ है तथा वे आवर्ती चिह्न-समूहों के रूप में दिखाई देते हैं।

उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

1. कथन-I से संकेत मिलता है कि इस लिपि का प्रयोग एक से अधिक प्रकार की सामग्री पर अथवा एक से अधिक प्रयोजनों के लिए किया जाता था।

2. कथन-II से संकेत मिलता है कि विस्तृत लेखों तथा पढ़े जा चुके किसी द्विभाषी अभिलेख के अभाव ने हड़प्पाई भाषा के पुनर्निर्माण को कठिन बना दिया है।

3. कथन-III निर्णायक रूप से स्थापित करता है कि हड़प्पाई लिपि एक वर्णमालात्मक लेखन प्रणाली थी।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

(a)  केवल 1 और 2

(b)  केवल 2 और 3

(c)  केवल 1 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 History (Ancient India) a

Medium
Applied(A)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:92 Sec.
  • Statement 1 is correct: Harappan inscriptions are generally short and occur on seals, sealings and other artefacts. Most surviving inscriptions are brief. Writing occurs on diverse objects, including seals, pottery, copper and terracotta tablets, tools and jewellery. Its occurrence was therefore not confined to a single category of object.
  • Statement 2 is correct: The script remains undeciphered, and no long inscription comparable to contemporary Egyptian or Mesopotamian texts has been discovered. No decipherment has gained general scholarly acceptance. The surviving texts are short and do not provide the extended textual material available for Egyptian and Mesopotamian civilisations. Tthe longest inscription as containing about 26 signs. Researchers have identified repeated signs and patterned combinations. Such patterns permit analysis of sign order and distribution even though their meanings remain uncertain.
  • Statement 3 is not correct: The Harappan script is logo-syllabic and not alphabetical—i.e., each symbol stands for a word or syllable. It was generally written and meant to be read from right to left (this is reversed on the seals). Longer inscriptions with more than one line were sometimes written in the boustrophedon style—with consecutive lines starting in opposite directions.
2

In the context of Buddhist traditions, the term “Manushi Buddhas” refers to:

(a) Bodhisattvas who postpone their own Nirvana for the liberation of humanity

(b) Buddhas believed to have appeared in the human world in successive ages, including predecessors of Gautama Buddha

(c) disciples of Gautama Buddha who attained the status of Arhat

(d) anthropomorphic representations of the Buddha that emerged with Mahayana Buddhism

बौद्ध परंपराओं के संदर्भ में, ‘मानुषी बुद्ध (Manushi Buddhas)’ पद से आशय है:

(a) ऐसे बोधिसत्त्व, जो मानवता की मुक्ति के लिए अपने निर्वाण को स्थगित रखते हैं।

(b)  ऐसे बुद्ध, जिनके बारे में माना जाता है कि वे विभिन्न युगों में क्रमशः मानव जगत में प्रकट हुए थे और जिनमें गौतम बुद्ध के पूर्ववर्ती बुद्ध भी शामिल हैं।

(c) गौतम बुद्ध के ऐसे शिष्य, जिन्होंने अर्हत की अवस्था प्राप्त कर ली थी।

(d) बुद्ध की ऐसी मानवाकृति प्रतिमाएँ, जिनका उद्भव महायान बौद्ध धर्म के साथ हुआ था।


 History (Ancient India) b

Difficult
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:19 Sec.
  • Manushi Buddhas (or Manushya-Buddhas) are earthly, human Buddhas who appear in human form to attain enlightenment and teach the Dharma during specific human world-ages.
  • According to early Pali Buddhist texts, there are seven historical/human Buddhas in this lineage:

    1. Vipasyin – "He who sees clearly"

    2. Sikhi – Embodiment of universal truth and compassion

    3. Vishvabhu – "Whose light is universal"

    4. Krakucchanda – "He who is adorned with patience"

    5. Kanakamuni – "He whose compassion shines like gold"

    6. Kashyapa – Beacon of consciousness beyond lifetimes

    7. Shakyamuni (Gautama Buddha) – The fourth or fifth mortal Buddha of the current age, regarded as the historical founder of Buddhism.

  • Hence option (b) is the correct answer.
3

With reference to ports and maritime trade during the Gupta period, consider the following pairs:

Ancient town/portPresent/Regional identification
1. GhantasalaAndhra coast
2. KaduraKerala
3. ChaulWestern coast
4. TamraliptiBengal Coast

How many of the above pairs are correctly matched?

(a) Only one

(b) Only two 

(c) Only three

(d) All four

गुप्त काल के पत्तनों और समुद्री व्यापार के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित युग्मों पर विचार कीजिए:

प्राचीन नगर/पत्तनवर्तमान/क्षेत्रीय पहचान
1. घंटशालाआंध्र तट
2. कदुराकेरल
3. चौलपश्चिमी तट
4. ताम्रलिप्ति

बंगाल तट

उपर्युक्त युग्मों में से कितने सही सुमेलित हैं?

(a) केवल एक

(b) केवल दो

(c) केवल तीन

(d) सभी चारों


 History (Ancient India) c

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:30 Sec.
  • The Ghantasala Stupa (or Maha Chaitya) is an ancient Buddhist archaeological site located in Ghantasala village, Krishna district, Andhra Pradesh, India. Situated about 21 km west of Machilipatnam and 11 km east of the Krishna River. Hence pair 1 is correctly matched.
    • Excavated by Alexander Rea, the ruined stupa measured roughly 112 feet in circumference and 23 feet in height.
    • Design & Features: It features a unique design with a solid brick cube at the center inscribed with the 12 constellations of the zodiac, concentric inner wheels, and fine lime mortar plastering characteristic of the Satavahana era.
    • Historical Significance: Known historically as Kantakasola (and mentioned by Ptolemy as Kantakossyla), Ghantasala was a thriving center for Indo-Roman maritime trade and a prominent Buddhist religious hub between the 2nd century BC and 3rd century CE.
  • Kadura was an ancient seaport handling foreign trade during the Gupta dynasty (c. 320–550 CE).
    • Location: Situated on the eastern coast of India in the Andhra region, near Ghantasala. Hence pair 2 is not correctly matched.
    • Role: Actively involved in overseas commerce and maritime trade routes across the Bay of Bengal.
    • Trade Goods: Handled the export and import of commodities such as textiles, spices, ivory, and precious goods.
  • Chaul is a historic port town located on the western Konkan coast of Maharashtra, India (near modern Revdanda and Alibag), situated along the Roha Creek and the Kundalika River (Raigad district, Maharashtra). Hence pair 3 is correctly matched.
    • Known as Champavati in early traditions and referred to as Symulla or Timulla by the ancient Greek geographer Ptolemy, Chaul served as a major seaport during the Gupta dynasty and earlier periods, facilitating trade with the Roman Empire, Western Asia, and the Arabian Peninsula.
    • Medieval & Sultanate Era: By the late 15th century, Chaul came under the Ahmednagar Sultanate (Nizamshahi rulers), functioning as a prominent regional port famous for cotton manufactured goods, horses, and maritime commerce.
    • The Portuguese arrived in the early 16th century, establishing a foothold in Lower Chaul (oriented toward sea trade) alongside the neighboring Korlai Fort and engaging in several notable conflicts (such as the Battle of Chaul and subsequent sieges).
  • Tamralipti - Tamralipti (also known as Tamralipta) was a major ancient port city and capital of the Suhma Kingdom, located along the Bay of Bengal in present-day Tamluk in the Purba Medinipur district of West Bengal, India﻿. Hence pair 4 is correctly matched.
    • Origin of Name: Derives from the Sanskrit word "Tamra" (meaning copper), as it served as a primary trade and export hub for copper mined nearby at Ghatsila in the Chota Nagpur Plateau.

    • Situated on the banks of the Rupnarayan River near the Bay of Bengal.

    • Flourished from roughly the 3rd century BCE to the 8th century CE, serving as a prominent maritime center during the Mauryan, Gupta, and post-Gupta periods.

    • Global Trade & Travelers: Acted as a vital departure point for trade with Sri Lanka (Ceylon), Southeast Asia (Java, Sumatra, Malaya), China, and the West. Famous Chinese Buddhist pilgrims such as Fa-Hien (Faxian), Xuanzang, and Yijing visited and documented the port.

    • Served as an important hub for the spread of Buddhism in eastern India and was visited by monks, traders, and missionaries.

4

Which of the following statements regarding the Badami Caves is/are correct?

1. The caves represent a rock-cut architectural tradition associated with the Early Chalukyas of Badami.

2. The Badami cave complex contains monuments associated with Hindu as well as Jaina religious traditions.

3. The paintings preserved in Cave 3 belong to the Gupta period, when Badami formed part of the Gupta Empire.

Select the answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 only

(b) 1 and 2 only

(c) 2 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

बादामी की गुफाओं के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

1. ये गुफाएँ बादामी के प्रारंभिक चालुक्यों से संबद्ध शैलकृत स्थापत्य परंपरा का प्रतिनिधित्व करती हैं।

2. बादामी गुफा परिसर में हिंदू तथा जैन, दोनों धार्मिक परंपराओं से संबद्ध स्मारक हैं।

3. गुफा संख्या 3 में संरक्षित चित्र गुप्त काल के हैं, जब बादामी गुप्त साम्राज्य का भाग था।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए। 

(a) केवल 1

(b) केवल 1 और 2

(c) केवल 2 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 Art And Culture b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:32 Sec.
  • Badami was the capital of the early Chalukyan dynasty (543 to 598 CE).  Hence statement 1 is correct.
  • These cave paintings are dedicated to Lord Shiva and have the earliest Brahmanical paintings (belonging to the 6th century A.D.) known so far.
  • Four rock-cut caves adorned with ancient carved pillars and bracket figures cut out of red sandstone form the Badami cave complex.
    • Cave 1: The first Badami cave temple is dedicated to Nataraja (Lord Shiva). Lifesize 18 handed Nataraja image striking 81 dance poses is present here. Contains a relief of Harihara (half Shiva, half Vishnu).
    • Cave 2: This Badami cave temple is flanked by celestial guardians and dwarf statues. This cave shows several of Lord Vishnu’s avatars. Four ornate pillars with carvings of the mythical lion Yali support this temple. Features sculptures of Vishnu as Trivikrama (conqueror of worlds), Varaha (boar), and Vamana (dwarf).
    • Cave 3: This is the finest as well as the largest of the group. It has giant-sized images of Lord Vishnu seated on a coiled serpent along with other images of the Vaishnava pantheon. As in other caves, every inch of the roof and pillars are carved.
      • It contains detailed carvings of Narasimha, Anantasayana, and Trivikrama. Completed in 578 CE (Saka 500) during the reign of King Kirtivarma I by his stepbrother, Chalukya Mangalesha. 
      • It features surviving Chalukya-period ceiling paintings that display classic modeling techniques and stylistic refinement directly inspired by the classical Gupta and Ajanta traditions but not painted during the times of Guptas. The Gupta Empire fell by the middle of the 6th century CE—around 550 CE—due to a combination of foreign invasions, internal political weakness, and economic decline. Hence statement 3 is not correct.
    • Cave 4: This cave temple is dedicated to Jainism. A small cave with images of Jain Tirthankaras, the sanctum holds a large sculpture of Lord Mahavira. It also features image of Parshvanatha.  Hence statement 2 is correct.
5

Consider the following assertion about the Sangam polity:

Political authority in the Sangam age was characterised by the coexistence of powerful crowned kings with subordinate or local centres of authority, while warfare remained an important basis of political power.

Which of the following statements support/supports the above assertion?

1. The Cheras, Cholas and Pandyas were collectively represented by the category of Ventar, while Velir were chieftains associated with the hill tracts.

2. The title Kilar was associated with village headmen, whereas Enadi was conferred on military commanders or captains.

3. The Pandyan ruler Nedunjeliyan defeated the Chera and Pandya kings along with eleven minor chieftains at the Battle of Venni.

Select the answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 only

(b) 1 and 2 only

(c) 2 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

संगम काल की राजव्यवस्था के संदर्भ में निम्नलिखित अभिकथन पर विचार कीजिए:

संगम काल में राजनीतिक सत्ता की विशेषता शक्तिशाली मुकुटधारी राजाओं के साथ अधीनस्थ अथवा स्थानीय सत्ता-केंद्रों का सह-अस्तित्व था, जबकि युद्ध राजनीतिक शक्ति का एक महत्वपूर्ण आधार बना रहा।

निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा/से उपर्युक्त अभिकथन का समर्थन करता है/करते हैं?

1. चेर, चोल और पांड्य सामूहिक रूप से ‘वेंतर’ श्रेणी के अंतर्गत आते थे, जबकि ‘वेलिर’ पहाड़ी क्षेत्रों से संबद्ध सरदार थे।

2. ‘किलार’ उपाधि ग्राम प्रधानों से संबद्ध थी, जबकि ‘एनादि’ उपाधि सैन्य सेनापतियों अथवा नायकों को प्रदान की जाती थी।

3. पांड्य शासक नेडुंजेलियन ने वेण्णि के युद्ध में चेर और पांड्य राजाओं के साथ ग्यारह छोटे सरदारों को पराजित किया था।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

(a) केवल 1

(b) केवल 1 और 2

(c) केवल 2 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 History (Ancient India) b

Difficult
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		d
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:65 Sec.
  • Statements 1 and 2 only support the assertion. Statement 3 is factually incorrect because it confuses Karikala’s victory at Venni with Nedunjeliyan’s victory at Talaiyalanganam.
  • The Cheras, Cholas and Pandyas were collectively represented by the category of Ventar, while Velir were chieftains associated with the hill tracts.
  • Meaning of the terms:
    • Ventar, also written Vendar/Vendhar, refers to the major crowned rulers. The expression Muventar denotes the three crowned ruling houses: Cheras, Cholas and Pandyas. Hence statement 1 is correct.
    • Velir refers to lesser or regional chieftains. Many were associated with hilly and forested territories. Hill tracts means stretches of hilly country, rather than a single hill or settlement.
    • The distinction between Ventar and Velir shows that political power was exercised by both major kings and regional chiefs. It supports the assertion’s claim that crowned monarchy coexisted with other centres of authority.
  • The title Kilar was associated with village headmen, whereas Enadi was conferred on military commanders or captains. Hence statement 2 is correct.
  • Meaning of the terms:
    • Kilar, also written Kizhar/Kilar - Village-level headmen or local landed leaders, particularly associated with agrarian settlements. Authority existed at the local level alongside kings and regional chiefs.
    • Enadi, also written Enati -  An honorific title bestowed on military captains or commanders. Military leadership received recognition and status. Conferred formally awarded or bestowed Enadi was an honour or title, rather than the name of a ruling dynasty.
  • The Chola ruler Karikala defeated the Chera and Pandya rulers, supported by eleven minor chieftains, at the Battle of Venni. Whereas, Nedunjeliyan/Neduncheliyan, Pandyan ruler defeated Chera and Chola rulers, supported by five chieftains at the Battle of Talaiyalanganam. Hence statement 3 is not correct.
6

With reference to society during the Rig Vedic period, consider the following statements:

I. Social differentiation existed, but occupational and class divisions had not yet become rigidly hereditary.

II. Women could participate in assemblies such as the Vidatha, and some women were composers of Vedic hymns.

III. The four-fold Varna order was already a rigid and fully developed feature throughout the Rig Vedic period.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) I only

(b) I and II only

(c) II and III only

(d) I, II and III

ऋग्वैदिक काल के समाज के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. सामाजिक विभेदीकरण विद्यमान था, किंतु व्यवसायगत और वर्गगत विभाजन अभी कठोर रूप से वंशानुगत नहीं हुए थे।

II. महिलाएँ विदथ जैसी सभाओं में भाग ले सकती थीं तथा कुछ महिलाएँ वैदिक सूक्तों की रचयिता थीं।

III. संपूर्ण ऋग्वैदिक काल में चतुर्वर्ण व्यवस्था पहले से ही एक कठोर तथा पूर्णतः विकसित विशेषता थी।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल I

(b) केवल I और II

(c) केवल II और III

(d) I, II और III


 History (Ancient India) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:33 Sec.
  • Rig Vedic society had differences of wealth, occupation, status and gender, but these had not yet crystallised into a fully developed, rigidly hereditary four-fold social order. Women’s participation in certain collective activities and the tradition of women hymn-composers also distinguish this period.
  • Statement I is correct: Social differentiation existed, but occupational and class divisions had not yet become rigidly hereditary.
    • Social differentiation means differences between people or groups based on factors such as wealth, occupation, political authority or ritual status. Rig Vedic society included chiefs, priests, warriors, pastoralists and craftspeople. Some possessed greater wealth—particularly cattle—and influence than others. However, the existence of different occupations does not automatically imply a rigid hereditary caste system
    • Occupational divisions refer to people performing different kinds of work.
    • Hereditary divisions mean that a person’s position or occupation is determined by birth and passed from parents to children.
    • Rigidly hereditary implies that movement between such positions is strongly restricted.
    • A well-known illustration is Rigveda 9.112.3, which describes a poet, his physician father and his mother grinding grain. The reference to different occupations within one family supports the argument that occupational roles were not invariably fixed by birth.
    • Thus, the statement correctly distinguishes existing inequality and differentiation from a later, more entrenched hereditary hierarchy. This does not mean that Rig Vedic society was completely equal or that everyone enjoyed unrestricted social mobility.
  • Statement II is correct: Women could participate in assemblies such as the Vidatha, and some women were composers of Vedic hymns.
  • First, women could participate in the Vidatha. The Vidatha was a collective gathering associated with religious, social and other community activities. Its precise functions are debated; it should not be equated with a modern elected legislature.
    • References to women speaking in the Vidatha support their participation in at least some collective gatherings.
    • Second, some hymns are traditionally attributed to women seers. A hymn, or sukta, is a composition of verses, commonly addressed to a deity. A rishika is a woman seer.
    • Examples traditionally associated with Rig Vedic hymns include: Woman seer Hymn traditionally associated with her Lopamudra (Rigveda 1.179, a dialogue involving Agastya); Pala (Rigveda 8.91, addressed to Indra); Ghosha (Rigveda 10.39–40, addressed to the Ashvins).
    •  Evidence of women seers and assembly participation does not establish universal literacy, complete gender equality or the absence of patriarchy.
  • Statement III is not correct: The Purusha Sukta—Rigveda 10.90—mentions the four categories through the imagery of their emergence from the cosmic being, Purusha. This hymn belongs to a relatively late layer of the Rigveda. Therefore, its presence demonstrates that a four-fold classification was being articulated; it does not establish that a rigid hereditary system operated uniformly throughout the entire Rig Vedic period. 
    • The four-fold Varna order refers to the broad classification into: Brahmana: Priestly and ritual functions; Rajanya/Kshatriya: ruling and warrior functions; Vaishya: productive activities; Shudra: service functions.

Consider the following properties:

1. Bhimbetka Cave Paintings

2. Charaideo Moidams

3. Kalka-Shimla Railway

4. Ashokan Edict Sites Along the Mauryan Routes

How many of the above properties were included in the World Heritage List released by UNESCO?

(a) Only one 

(b) Only two 

(c) Only three

(d) All four

 निम्नलिखित धरोहरों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. भीमबेटका के गुफा चित्र

2. चराइदेव के मोइदम

3. कालका-शिमला रेलवे

4. मौर्यकालीन मार्गों के किनारे स्थित अशोक के अभिलेख स्थल

उपर्युक्त धरोहरों में से कितनी यूनेस्को द्वारा जारी विश्व धरोहर सूची में शामिल की गई थीं?

(a) केवल एक

(b) केवल दो

(c) केवल तीन

(d) सभी चारों


 Art And Culture c

Difficult
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:13 Sec.
  • Bhimbetka cave paintings - Included in the World Heritage List in 2003 - Bhimbetka is a major prehistoric site with evidence spanning multiple Stone Age phases; It was discovered by V. S. Wakankar; It contains evidence from Paleolithic, Mesolithic, and Neolithic periods; It is especially noted for Mesolithic finds and prehistoric art (petroglyphs/cave paintings).
  • Charaideo Moidam - Included in the World Heritage List in 2024: The Charaideo Moidams, a unique 700-year old mound-burial system of the Ahom dynasty from Assam, They are located in the foothills of the Patkai Ranges, eastern Assam; Charaideo is home to the royal necropolis of the Tai-Ahom dynasty (13th–19th century CE) and are comparable the Egyptian Pyramids; Protection Officers: During Ahom's reign, Moidams were protected by special officers called Moidam Phukans and a guard group known as Moidamiya; Moidams have grave goods such as food, horses, elephants, and sometimes even servants (items they would require in their afterlife).
  • UNESCO accorded Darjeeling Himalayan Railway (1999), Nilgiri Mountain Railway (2005), Kalka Shimla Railway (2008) and Chhatrapati Shivaji Terminus, Mumbai (2004) - World Heritage Site status.
  • Ashokan Edict Sites Along the Mauryan Routes: This property was only added to India's UNESCO Tentative List in 2025 and has not yet been inscribed on the main World Heritage List.
  • Hence option (c) is the correct answer.

With reference to Mughal court culture, the term “Naubat Khana” referred to a:

(a) royal workshop where musical instruments were manufactured

(b) hall where the emperor received foreign ambassadors

(c) drum house from where the imperial band played

(d) department responsible for maintaining the imperial cavalry

मुगल दरबारी संस्कृति के संदर्भ में, ‘नौबतखाना’ से आशय था:

(a) एक शाही कार्यशाला, जहाँ वाद्ययंत्रों का निर्माण किया जाता था।

(b) एक सभागार, जहाँ सम्राट विदेशी राजदूतों से भेंट करता था।

(c) एक नक्कारखाना, जहाँ से शाही वाद्यवृंद वादन करता था।

(d) शाही घुड़सवार सेना के रखरखाव के लिए उत्तरदायी एक विभाग।


 History (Medieval India) c

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		Not Attempted
  • In the Mughal court culture, the term “Naubat Khana” (also known as Naqqar Khana) referred to a drum house or a royal music pavilion.
  • Key Characteristics & Functions:
    • Purpose: It was a designated structure where court musicians played instruments—primarily drums (naqqar or naubat), shehnais, and trumpets—to announce the arrival of the Emperor or important dignitaries at the royal court.
    • Daily Rituals: The musicians also played traditional ensembles at appointed hours throughout the day (usually five times daily) to mark the time or signal special imperial ceremonies.
    •  These pavilions were historically constructed as ceremonial gateways situated between the outer public areas and the inner private palace complex (such as the famous Naubat Khana at the Red Fort in Delhi or at Fatehpur Sikri).
    • Protocol: It served a distinct ceremonial function where all visitors—except royal princes of the blood—were required to dismount from their elephants or horses before entering further into the inner courts.
  • Hence option (c) is the correct answer.

Consider the following statements regarding Rajendra I of the Imperial Cholas:

1. He completed the Chola conquest of Sri Lanka and defeated King Mahinda V.

2. His northern expedition reached Bengal, where he defeated the Pala ruler Mahipala I.

3. Following his Ganga expedition, he assumed the title Gangaikondachola and founded Gangaikondacholapuram.

4. His naval expedition against Srivijaya resulted in the permanent annexation of the entire Srivijayan Empire into the Chola Empire.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 1, 2 and 3 only

(c) 2, 3 and 4 only

(d) 1, 3 and 4 only

साम्राज्यवादी चोल वंश के राजेंद्र प्रथम के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. उसने श्रीलंका पर चोल विजय को पूर्ण किया तथा राजा महिंद पंचम को पराजित किया।

2. उसका उत्तरी अभियान बंगाल तक पहुँचा, जहाँ उसने पाल शासक महीपाल प्रथम को पराजित किया।

3. अपने गंगा अभियान के पश्चात उसने ‘गंगैकोंडचोल’ की उपाधि धारण की और गंगैकोंडचोलपुरम की स्थापना की।

4. श्रीविजय के विरुद्ध उसके नौसैनिक अभियान के परिणामस्वरूप संपूर्ण श्रीविजय साम्राज्य का चोल साम्राज्य में स्थायी विलय हो गया।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 1, 2 और 3

(c) केवल 2, 3 और 4

(d) केवल 1, 3 और 4


 History (Medieval India) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		Not Attempted
  • Rajendra I, the son and successor of Rajaraja I, expanded Chola power through campaigns in Sri Lanka, eastern India and Southeast Asia.
  • Statement 1 is correct: Rajendra I (son of Rajaraja-I) extended the conquest around 1017–1018 CE, defeated and captured Mahinda V (Srilanka ruler), and carried him to the Chola country as a prisoner. He completed the Chola conquest of Sri Lanka and defeated King Mahinda V.
    • Rajaraja I Invaded Sri Lanka around 993 CE and established Chola control over its northern part.
  • Statement 2 is correct: Rajendra-I's northern expedition reached Bengal, where he defeated the Pala ruler Mahipala I.
    • In the early 1020s, an army dispatched by Rajendra marched through eastern India towards the Ganga. Chola accounts include Mahipala I of the Pala dynasty among the defeated rulers.
    • The Palas were a major ruling dynasty associated with Bengal and Bihar. Rajendra’s campaign demonstrated the ability of a southern power to project military force far beyond its core territory.
  • Statement 3 is correct: Following his Ganga expedition, he assumed the title Gangaikondachola and founded Gangaikondacholapuram.
    • Gangaikondachola - Conventionally translated as “the Chola who conquered the Ganga.” It commemorated the successful expedition to the Gangetic region.
    • Gangaikondacholapuram - “The city of the Chola who conquered the Ganga.” Rajendra founded it as his new capital.
    • Chola Gangam lake - A large reservoir associated with the commemoration of the victory.
    • He shifted the capital from Thanjavur to Gangaikondacholapuram, now in Tamil Nadu’s Ariyalur district, and built its great Shiva temple. The Gangetic expedition connected the city, temple and reservoir.
  • Statement 4 is not correct: Rajendra launched a major naval expedition against Srivijaya around 1025 CE.
    • Srivijaya was a maritime power centred in Sumatra, with influence over ports and trading networks across parts of the Malay world. Its position was important for commerce linking the Indian Ocean with Southeast Asia and China.
    • The Chola expedition attacked important centres, including Kadaram, generally identified with Kedah, and captured the Srivijayan ruler Sangrama Vijayottungavarman. However, the campaign did not permanently incorporate the entire Srivijayan political network into the Chola Empire.
    • The expedition demonstrated Chola maritime strength and is commonly interpreted in connection with commercial and strategic interests.

With reference to the rock-cut caves at Junnar, which one of the following statements is correct?

(a) Junnar has more than 130 rock-cut excavations, including groups such as Tulija Lena and Ganesh Lena, and the Tulija Lena group contains a rare circular Chaitya.

(b) Junnar is principally known for a group of 23 caves, most of which are Viharas, including the famous Nahapana Vihara.

(c) Junnar is best known for the Avalokitesvara Litany and an elaborate system of rock-cut water cisterns.

(d) Junnar contains a Chaitya hall whose façade is concealed behind a screen of rock and whose pillars have bell capitals surmounted by animals.

जुन्नर की शैलकृत गुफाओं के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

(a) जुन्नर में 130 से अधिक शैलकृत गुफाएँ हैं, जिनमें तुलजा लेणा और गणेश लेणा जैसे समूह शामिल हैं तथा तुलजा लेणा समूह में एक दुर्लभ वृत्ताकार चैत्य है।

(b) जुन्नर मुख्यतः 23 गुफाओं के एक समूह के लिए प्रसिद्ध है, जिनमें से अधिकांश विहार हैं और जिनमें प्रसिद्ध नहपान विहार भी शामिल है।

(c) जुन्नर अवलोकितेश्वर की स्तुति तथा शैलकृत जलकुंडों की विस्तृत प्रणाली के लिए सर्वाधिक प्रसिद्ध है।

(d) जुन्नर में एक चैत्यगृह है, जिसका अग्रभाग चट्टान के एक आवरण के पीछे छिपा हुआ है तथा जिसके स्तंभों के घंटाकार शीर्षों पर पशु आकृतियाँ बनी हैं।


 Art And Culture a

Difficult
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		Not Attempted
  • Statement (a) is correct: The Junnar Caves complex contains the largest concentration of rock-cut excavations in India, consisting of more than 200 individual caves across four main hills. These groups include Tulja Lena, Ganesh Lena (also known as Lenyadri), Shivneri, and Manmodi. Crucially, Cave 3 of the Tulja Caves group houses an exceptionally rare, circular Chaitya hall (vritta-chaitya) featuring twelve octagonal basalt pillars encircling a central monolithic stupa.
  • Statement (b) is incorrect: The famous Nahapana Vihara (Cave 10) is located at the Nasik Caves (Pandavleni Caves), not Junnar.
  • Statement (c) is incorrect: The Avalokitesvara Litany (depictions of Avalokitesvara saving devotees from various dangers) is famously found at sites like the Kanheri Caves and Ajanta Caves, rather than being the defining characteristic of Junnar.
  • Statement (d) is incorrect: A magnificent Chaitya hall whose pillars have bell capitals surmounted by animal figures and whose wooden-ribbed screen/façade is iconic is characteristic of the Karla Caves or Bedse Caves.

Consider the following statements regarding Mahatma Gandhi's Phoenix Settlement:

I. Gandhi's reading of John Ruskin's Unto This Last influenced his belief in the dignity of labour and a life based on simplicity and collective work.

II. The Phoenix Settlement became an early practical experiment in these ideas, combining communal living and manual labour with the publication of Indian Opinion.

III. The experience at Phoenix preceded Gandhi's establishment of Tolstoy Farm in South Africa.

Which of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

1. Phoenix Settlement can be regarded as an early laboratory for some ideas that later became important to Gandhi's ashram life.

2. Gandhi's experiments with communal living began only after he developed the technique of Satyagraha during the Transvaal struggle.

3. Gandhi's engagement with Ruskin went beyond intellectual appreciation and influenced the organisation of his everyday social and economic life.

Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 only

(b) 1 and 3 only

(c) 2 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

महात्मा गांधी की फीनिक्स बस्ती के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. जॉन रस्किन की ‘अन्टू दिस लास्ट (Unto This Last)’ के अध्ययन ने श्रम की गरिमा तथा सादगी और सामूहिक श्रम पर आधारित जीवन में गांधी के विश्वास को प्रभावित किया।

II. फीनिक्स बस्ती इन विचारों का एक प्रारंभिक व्यावहारिक प्रयोग बनी, जिसमें सामुदायिक जीवन और शारीरिक श्रम को ‘इंडियन ओपिनियन’ के प्रकाशन के साथ जोड़ा गया।

III. फीनिक्स का अनुभव दक्षिण अफ्रीका में गांधी द्वारा टॉल्सटॉय फार्म की स्थापना से पहले का था।

उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

1. फीनिक्स बस्ती को ऐसे कुछ विचारों की प्रारंभिक प्रयोगशाला माना जा सकता है, जो आगे चलकर गांधी के आश्रम जीवन के लिए महत्वपूर्ण बने।

2. सामुदायिक जीवन के संबंध में गांधी के प्रयोग ट्रांसवाल संघर्ष के दौरान सत्याग्रह की तकनीक विकसित करने के बाद ही आरंभ हुए।

3. रस्किन के विचारों के साथ गांधी का जुड़ाव केवल बौद्धिक सराहना तक सीमित नहीं था, बल्कि उसने उनके दैनिक सामाजिक और आर्थिक जीवन के संगठन को भी प्रभावित किया।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

(a) केवल 1

(b) केवल 1 और 3

(c) केवल 2 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3

Difficult
Current Affairs(CA)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:103 Sec.
  • Conclusion 1 is correct: The Phoenix Settlement (established in 1904) served as the foundational blueprint and practical laboratory for communal living, manual labor, and self-reliance that Gandhi later expanded into his ashrams in South Africa (Tolstoy Farm) and India (Sabarmati).
  • Conclusion 2 is not correct: Gandhi's experiment with communal living at the Phoenix Settlement began in 1904, which predates the formal emergence and application of Satyagraha during the Transvaal Indian struggle that started around 1906–1907.
  • Conclusion 3 is correct: Gandhi’s reading of John Ruskin's Unto This Last immediately transformed his outlook, prompting him to reorganize his daily life, move to a rural commune, and establish the Phoenix Settlement to practice manual work and economic equality

With reference to the constitutional developments in British India, consider the following statements:

Assertion (A): The Round Table Conferences constituted an important stage in the constitutional process that eventually culminated in the Government of India Act, 1935.

Reason (R): A White Paper on constitutional reforms, drawing upon the discussions and proposals of the three Round Table Conferences, was published in 1933 and subsequently became a basis for the Government of India Act, 1935.

Which one of the following statements is correct?

(a) Both A and R are correct, and R is the correct explanation of A.

(b) Both A and R are correct, but R is not the correct explanation of A.

(c) A is correct, but R is incorrect.

(d) A is incorrect, but R is correct.

ब्रिटिश भारत में संवैधानिक विकास के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

अभिकथन (A): गोलमेज सम्मेलन उस संवैधानिक प्रक्रिया का एक महत्वपूर्ण चरण थे, जिसकी परिणति अंततः भारत शासन अधिनियम, 1935 के रूप में हुई।

कारण (R): तीनों गोलमेज सम्मेलनों की चर्चाओं और प्रस्तावों पर आधारित संवैधानिक सुधारों संबंधी एक श्वेत पत्र 1933 में प्रकाशित किया गया, जो बाद में भारत शासन अधिनियम, 1935 का एक आधार बना।

उपर्युक्त कथनों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

(a) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या है।

(b) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या नहीं है।

(c) A सही है, किंतु R गलत है।

(d) A गलत है, किंतु R सही है।


 History (Modern India) a

Easy
Applied(A)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:25 Sec.
  • Assertion (A) is correct: The three Round Table Conferences (held between 1930 and 1932) were organized by the British Government to deliberate upon constitutional reforms in India following the Simon Commission report. These discussions formed the baseline for drafting the next phase of governance.
  • Reason (R) is correct: Following the conclusion of the Third Round Table Conference, the British Government published a White Paper on Constitutional Reforms in March 1933. This document contained the blueprint for a new constitution. It was subsequently analyzed by a Joint Select Committee of the British Parliament, and its recommendations directly resulted in the enactment of the Government of India Act, 1935.
  • Because the White Paper directly processed the proposals of the Round Table Conferences to lay the groundwork for the 1935 Act, Reason (R) perfectly explains why Assertion (A) is correct.
  • Hence option (a) is the correct answer.

The Sanskrit drama Svapnavasavadatta, centred on King Udayana, Queen Vasavadatta and the political strategy of minister Yaugandharayana, is attributed to:

(a) Kalidasa

(b) Bhasa

(c) Vishakhadatta

(d) Shudraka

राजा उदयन, रानी वासवदत्ता तथा मंत्री यौगंधरायण की राजनीतिक रणनीति पर केंद्रित संस्कृत नाटक ‘स्वप्नवासवदत्तम्’ के रचयिता हैं:

(a) कालिदास

(b) भास

(c) विशाखदत्त

(d) शूद्रक


 Art And Culture b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:74 Sec.
  • Svapnavasavadatta (translated as "The Dream of Vasavadatta") is a celebrated six-act classical Sanskrit drama written by the ancient Indian playwright Bhasa.
    • The plot highlights the political stratagems of the chief minister, Yaugandharayana, who orchestrates a plan to fake Queen Vasavadatta's death so King Udayana can marry Princess Padmavati, thereby securing a vital military alliance to reclaim his lost kingdom.
  •  Kalidasa is famous for masterpieces like Abhijnanasakuntalam and Meghaduta.
  • Vishakhadatta authored the political drama Mudrarakshasa.
  • Shudraka wrote the well-known play Mrichhakatikam (The Little Clay Cart)
  • Hence option (b) is the correct answer.

Consider the following statements regarding the Mansabdari and Jagirdari arrangements under the Mughals:

I. Mansab denoted the rank of an imperial officer under Akbar.

II. A Jagir was essentially a revenue assignment that could be used for remunerating an imperial officer and was ordinarily not a hereditary proprietary right over the land.

Which of the statements given above is/are correct?

(a) I only

(b) II only

(c) Both I and II

(d) Neither I nor II

मुगलों के अधीन मनसबदारी और जागीरदारी व्यवस्थाओं के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. अकबर के शासनकाल में 'मनसब' शाही अधिकारी के पद को दर्शाता था।

II. जागीर मूल रूप से राजस्व का एक ऐसा आवंटन था जिसका उपयोग शाही अधिकारी को पारिश्रमिक देने के लिए किया जा सकता था, और यह आमतौर पर भूमि पर कोई वंशानुगत मालिकाना हक नहीं होता था।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

(a) केवल I

(b) केवल II

(c) I और II दोनों

(d) न तो I, न ही II


 History (Medieval India) c

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		d
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:142 Sec.
  • Statement I is correct: The term Mansab is of Arabic origin and denotes a position, status, or rank. The Mansabdari system was formally introduced and institutionalized by the Mughal Emperor Akbar in 1571 to organize both civil and military administrative officials into a single hierarchical structure.
  • Statement II is correct: Under the Mughal administration, a Jagir was a revenue assignment given to a Mansabdar in lieu of a cash salary (naqdi). Critically, the Jagirdar did not have hereditary proprietary ownership of the land, but merely held the right to collect and enjoy the state's share of land revenue equivalent to their sanctioned pay. These assignments were highly transferable and strictly controlled by the emperor.

I. बौद्ध ग्रंथ ‘अंगुत्तर निकाय’ छठी शताब्दी ईसा पूर्व के सोलह महाजनपदों की मानक सूची प्रदान करता है।

II. जैन ग्रंथ ‘भगवती सूत्र’ सोलह महाजनपदों की वही सूची प्रदान करता है, जो ‘अंगुत्तर निकाय’ में दी गई है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?


(a) केवल I

(b) केवल II

 Art And Culture a

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:19 Sec.
  • Statement I is correct: The Anguttara Nikaya, an ancient Buddhist scripture (part of the Sutta Pitaka), provides the definitive and most widely accepted standard canonical list of the sixteen Mahajanapadas that flourished in northern ancient India during the 6th century BCE.
  • Statement II is incorrect: While the Jaina text Bhagavati Sutra does mention a list of sixteen Mahajanapadas, its list is not identical to the one found in the Anguttara Nikaya. The Jaina tradition incorporates different regional names (such as Malaya, Malava, Kochchha, and Padha) and focuses more heavily on countries in Madhyadesha, the far east, and the south, rather than the exact names given in the Buddhist text.

Consider the following pairs:

Invader/Group Nature/Outcome of intervention in India
1. Alexander Campaign followed by withdrawal
2. Kushanas Migration/conquest followed by settlement and cultural synthesis
3. Mahmud of Ghazni Raids combined with control of Punjab, but without creation of a large permanent Gangetic empire

How many of the pairs given above are correctly matched?

(a) Only one 

(b) Only two 

(c) Only three

(d) All four

आक्रमणकारी/समूहभारत में हस्तक्षेप की प्रकृति/परिणाम
1. सिकंदरसैन्य अभियान के पश्चात वापसी
2. कुषाणप्रवासन/विजय के पश्चात बस जाना और सांस्कृतिक समन्वय
3. महमूद गजनवीपंजाब पर नियंत्रण के साथ छापामार आक्रमण, किंतु गंगा क्षेत्र में किसी बड़े स्थायी साम्राज्य की स्थापना नहीं
4. मुहम्मद गोरीस्थायी राजनीतिक सत्ता स्थापित किए बिना मुख्यतः धन प्राप्त करने के उद्देश्य से छापामार आक्रमण
उपर्युक्त युग्मों में से कितने सही सुमेलित हैं?

(a) केवल एक

(b) केवल दो

(c) केवल तीन

(d) सभी चारों


 History (Ancient India) c

Difficult
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		d
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:218 Sec.
  • Pair 1 is correctly matched: Alexander (Campaign followed by withdrawal): Alexander the Great invaded northwestern India in 326 BCE, defeated King Porus, but his weary army mutinied at the Beas River, forcing him to turn back and withdraw his forces.
  • Pair 2 is correctly matched: Kushanas (Migration/conquest followed by settlement and cultural synthesis): The Kushanas (originally part of the Yuezhi confederation) migrated from Central Asia, established a massive empire in northern India, settled down, and famously patronized Buddhism and Indian art (like the Gandhara and Mathura schools).
  • Pair 3 is correctly matched: Mahmud of Ghazni (Raids combined with control of Punjab, but without creation of a large permanent Gangetic empire). Mahmud of Ghazni launched 17 major raids into India primarily to plunder wealth (such as the temple of Somnath), annexed the Punjab region to secure his borders, but never attempted to build a permanent empire deep into the Gangetic plains.
  • Pair 4 is not correctly matched: Muhammad Ghori (Raids primarily aimed at extracting wealth without establishing enduring political authority): Unlike Mahmud of Ghazni, Muhammad Ghori's interventions were explicitly aimed at territorial conquest and establishing political authority. His victories laid the actual foundation of the Delhi Sultanate through his general Qutb-ud-din Aibak.

Consider the following statements regarding certain terms associated with the English East India Company in the eighteenth century:

I. Gomasthas were paid agents employed by the Company to supervise weavers, procure textiles and enforce contracts with producers.

II. Nabobs was a term used in Britain for Company officials and merchants who returned from India with large fortunes, often attracting criticism for their wealth and influence.

III. Both Gomasthas and Nabobs referred to categories of Indian revenue officials incorporated into the Company's land-revenue administration.

Which one of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

(a) Statements I and II are correct, and they illustrate two different dimensions of the Company's expanding economic influence.

(b) Statements I and III are correct, as both terms were associated primarily with land-revenue collection.

(c) Statements II and III are correct, as both referred to officials who accumulated wealth in India and returned to Britain.

(d) All three statements are correct.

अठारहवीं शताब्दी में अंग्रेजी ईस्ट इंडिया कंपनी से संबद्ध कुछ पदों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. गुमाश्ते कंपनी द्वारा नियुक्त वेतनभोगी अभिकर्ता थे, जो बुनकरों की निगरानी करते थे, वस्त्र प्राप्त करते थे तथा उत्पादकों के साथ किए गए अनुबंधों को लागू करवाते थे।

II. ‘नवाब’ पद का प्रयोग ब्रिटेन में कंपनी के उन अधिकारियों और व्यापारियों के लिए किया जाता था, जो भारत से अपार धन-संपत्ति लेकर लौटते थे और प्रायः अपनी संपत्ति तथा प्रभाव के कारण आलोचना का विषय बनते थे।

III. गुमाश्ते और नवाब, दोनों कंपनी के भू-राजस्व प्रशासन में सम्मिलित भारतीय राजस्व अधिकारियों की श्रेणियाँ थीं।

उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक निष्कर्ष निकाला जा सकता है?

(a) कथन-I और कथन-II सही हैं तथा वे कंपनी के बढ़ते आर्थिक प्रभाव के दो भिन्न आयामों को दर्शाते हैं।

(b) कथन-I और कथन-III सही हैं, क्योंकि दोनों पद मुख्यतः भू-राजस्व संग्रह से संबद्ध थे।

(c) कथन-II और कथन-III सही हैं, क्योंकि दोनों ऐसे अधिकारियों को दर्शाते थे, जिन्होंने भारत में धन अर्जित किया और ब्रिटेन लौट गए।

(d) सभी तीनों कथन सही हैं।


 History (Modern India) a

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:48 Sec.
  • The correct conclusion is (a) Statements I and II are correct, and they illustrate two different dimensions of the Company's expanding economic influence.
  • Statement I is correct: Gomasthas were indeed paid Indian agents appointed by the English East India Company to establish direct control over local textile producers. Their job was to supervise weavers, collect supplies, enforce contracts via advance loan systems, and inspect the quality of the cloth.
  • Statement II is correct: Nabobs was a derogatory, anglicized pun on the Indian word Nawab. It was widely used in Britain to describe Company officials who amassed massive private fortunes through corruption and trade in India and returned home to buy political influence and estates, often drawing immense public criticism and mockery.
  • Statement III is incorrect: Neither Gomasthas nor Nabobs were categories of Indian land-revenue officials. Gomasthas belonged to the commercial and textile procurement sphere, while Nabobs referred specifically to wealthy British individuals rather than indigenous revenue administrators.

जैन धर्म की दिगंबर और श्वेतांबर परंपराओं के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

कथन-I: दिगंबरों का मानना है कि मुनियों के लिए पूर्ण नग्नता अनिवार्य है तथा वे यह स्वीकार नहीं करते कि कोई महिला अपने वर्तमान स्त्री जन्म में मोक्ष प्राप्त कर सकती है।

कथन-II: श्वेतांबर मुनियों को श्वेत वस्त्र धारण करने की अनुमति देते हैं तथा यह स्वीकार करते हैं कि महिलाएँ अपने वर्तमान जीवन में मोक्ष प्राप्त कर सकती हैं।

    Svetambaras are “white-clad” and described as more liberal/adaptive.

    Digambaras are “sky-clad” (nudity) and described as more orthodox/strict.

Consider the following statements regarding the Charter Act of 1833:

I. The Governors of Bombay and Madras were deprived of their legislative powers.

II. The Governor-General-in-Council was empowered to legislate for the whole of the British territories in India.

III. A fourth member was added to the Governor-General's Council, but his participation was confined to legislative business.

Which of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

1. The Act contributed to the centralisation of legislative authority in the Government of India.

2. The addition of the fourth member reflected an emerging distinction between the legislative and executive functions of the Governor-General's Council.

3. The Act established fully separate legislative and executive councils at the central level.

Select the answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 1 and 3 only

(c) 2 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

चार्टर अधिनियम, 1833 के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

I. बंबई और मद्रास के गवर्नरों को उनकी विधायी शक्तियों से वंचित कर दिया गया।

II. गवर्नर-जनरल-इन-काउंसिल को भारत में संपूर्ण ब्रिटिश राज्यक्षेत्रों के लिए कानून बनाने का अधिकार प्रदान किया गया।

III. गवर्नर-जनरल की परिषद में एक चौथा सदस्य जोड़ा गया, किंतु उसकी भागीदारी केवल विधायी कार्यों तक सीमित थी।

उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

1. इस अधिनियम ने भारत सरकार में विधायी प्राधिकार के केंद्रीकरण में योगदान दिया।

2. चौथे सदस्य को शामिल किया जाना गवर्नर-जनरल की परिषद के विधायी और कार्यकारी कार्यों के बीच उभरते भेद को दर्शाता था।

3. इस अधिनियम ने केंद्रीय स्तर पर पूर्णतः पृथक विधायी और कार्यकारी परिषदों की स्थापना की।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।


(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 1 और 3

(c) केवल 2 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 History (Modern India) a

Medium
Applied(A)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:85 Sec.
  • Conclusion 1 is correct: Statements I and II directly show the shift of legislative authority from the provincial presidencies (Bombay and Madras) to the central government (the Governor-General-in-Council). This was the definitive step toward the centralisation of legislative authority in British India.
  • Conclusion 2 is correct: Statement III indicates that a fourth member (the Law Member, Lord Macaulay) was added to the Council, but his role was strictly restricted to legislative affairs. By separating his participation from regular executive duties, the Act introduced an emerging distinction between the legislative and executive functions of the Council.
  • Conclusion 3 is incorrect: The Charter Act of 1833 did not establish fully separate legislative and executive councils. The expansion into a distinct 6-member Indian (Central) Legislative Council, separate from the Executive Council, did not occur until the Charter Act of 1853
21

The Government substantially increases its borrowing from the domestic financial market. At the same time, the supply of loanable funds does not increase proportionately, leading to upward pressure on interest rates and reduced private investment. Which one of the following best describes the situation?

(a) Fiscal consolidation

(b) Crowding-out effect

(c) Quantitative easing

(d) Disinflation

सरकार घरेलू वित्तीय बाजार से अपने उधार में पर्याप्त वृद्धि करती है। इसी समय, उधार देने योग्य निधियों की आपूर्ति में आनुपातिक वृद्धि नहीं होती है, जिससे ब्याज दरों पर बढ़ने का दबाव पड़ता है और निजी निवेश घटता है। निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक इस स्थिति का सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त वर्णन करता है?

(a) राजकोषीय समेकन

(b) क्राउडिंग-आउट प्रभाव

(c) मात्रात्मक सहजता

(d) अवस्फीति


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:17 Sec.
  • The crowding-out effect refers to a situation in which increased government borrowing reduces the availability of funds for private-sector investment by exerting upward pressure on interest rates. It generally occurs when the government finances its fiscal deficit through borrowing from the domestic financial market, thereby competing with private businesses for a limited pool of loanable funds.
  • When government borrowing increases without a corresponding rise in the supply of savings or loanable funds, the demand for funds increases, leading to higher interest rates. As borrowing becomes more expensive, private firms may postpone or reduce their investment in machinery, infrastructure, business expansion and other productive activities. Consequently, increased public borrowing may partially offset private investment, potentially affecting capital formation and long-term economic growth.
  • The magnitude of the crowding-out effect depends on prevailing economic conditions. It is generally more pronounced when the economy is operating close to full capacity and financial resources are relatively constrained. However, during periods of economic slowdown, when private investment demand is weak and productive resources remain underutilised, increased government expenditure may stimulate aggregate demand without significantly displacing private investment. Moreover, public investment in infrastructure may encourage private investment over the longer term, resulting in a crowding-in effect.
  • Fiscal consolidation refers to measures aimed at reducing fiscal deficits and public debt, while quantitative easing involves the purchase of financial assets by a central bank to inject liquidity into the financial system. Disinflation refers to a reduction in the rate of inflation, rather than an absolute decline in the general price level. Therefore, these concepts do not describe the situation presented in the question.
  • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.
22

Which one of the following statements regarding the monetary policy instruments of the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) is correct?

(a) An increase in the Cash Reserve Ratio (CRR) increases the lending capacity of commercial banks by releasing additional liquidity into the banking system.

(b) Under the Statutory Liquidity Ratio (SLR), commercial banks are required to maintain a prescribed proportion of their deposits exclusively as cash balances with the RBI.

(c) An increase in the repo rate generally raises the cost of borrowing for commercial banks and may lead to higher lending rates in the economy.

(d) The reverse repo rate is the rate at which commercial banks borrow short-term funds from the RBI against government securities.

भारतीय रिज़र्व बैंक (RBI) के मौद्रिक नीति उपकरणों के संबंध में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

(a) नकद आरक्षित अनुपात (CRR) में वृद्धि से बैंकिंग प्रणाली में अतिरिक्त तरलता उपलब्ध होती है, जिससे वाणिज्यिक बैंकों की ऋण देने की क्षमता बढ़ती है।

(b) सांविधिक तरलता अनुपात (SLR) के अंतर्गत वाणिज्यिक बैंकों को अपनी जमाराशियों का एक निर्धारित अनुपात अनन्य रूप से RBI के पास नकद शेष के रूप में बनाए रखना आवश्यक होता है।

(c) रेपो दर में वृद्धि से सामान्यतः वाणिज्यिक बैंकों की उधार लेने की लागत बढ़ती है और अर्थव्यवस्था में ऋण दरें बढ़ सकती हैं।

(d) रिवर्स रेपो दर वह दर है, जिस पर वाणिज्यिक बैंक सरकारी प्रतिभूतियों के बदले RBI से अल्पकालिक निधियाँ उधार लेते हैं।


 Economics (Macroeconomics) c

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:135 Sec.
  • Option (A) is not correct: CRR refers to the proportion of a bank's Net Demand and Time Liabilities (NDTL) that must be maintained as cash balances with the RBI. An increase in CRR reduces the funds available with commercial banks for lending, thereby absorbing liquidity from the banking system. Conversely, a reduction in CRR releases liquidity and may encourage credit expansion.
  • Option (B) is not correct: SLR requires banks to maintain a prescribed proportion of their NDTL in specified liquid assets, such as cash, gold and approved government securities. Unlike CRR, SLR does not require banks to maintain these assets exclusively as cash balances with the RBI. These assets are generally maintained by banks themselves.
  • Option (C) is correct: The repo rate is the interest rate at which the RBI provides short-term liquidity to eligible banks against eligible securities under repurchase agreements. An increase in the repo rate makes borrowing more expensive for banks and may result in higher lending rates for households and businesses. This can reduce credit demand, consumption and investment, thereby moderating aggregate demand and helping contain inflationary pressures.
  • Option (D) is not correct: The reverse repo rate refers to the interest rate at which the RBI borrows funds from eligible banks through reverse repurchase agreements. It is used to absorb surplus liquidity from the banking system. The statement incorrectly describes a repo transaction rather than a reverse repo transaction. Since April 2022, the Standing Deposit Facility (SDF) rate has served as the floor of the Liquidity Adjustment Facility (LAF) corridor, replacing the fixed reverse repo rate in that role.
  • Hence, option (C) is the correct answer.
23

In an economy experiencing persistently high inflation, the central bank decides to increase the policy interest rate as part of its monetary policy response.

With reference to the likely implications of such a decision, consider the following statements:

1. An increase in the policy interest rate may result in higher Equated Monthly Instalments (EMIs) or an extension of repayment tenure for borrowers with floating-rate home loans.

2. An increase in the policy interest rate encourages borrowing and private investment by reducing the cost of credit, thereby helping to control inflation.

3. Higher interest rates may discourage household consumption and business investment, thereby moderating aggregate demand and inflationary pressures.

4. An increase in the policy interest rate directly reduces the prices of essential commodities by improving their physical supply in the market.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 1 and 3 only

(c) 2, 3 and 4 only

(d) 1, 3 and 4 only

. निरंतर उच्च मुद्रास्फीति का सामना कर रही एक अर्थव्यवस्था में केंद्रीय बैंक अपनी मौद्रिक नीति संबंधी प्रतिक्रिया के अंतर्गत नीतिगत ब्याज दर बढ़ाने का निर्णय लेता है। ऐसे निर्णय के संभावित प्रभावों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. नीतिगत ब्याज दर में वृद्धि से परिवर्ती दर वाले आवास ऋण लेने वालों की समान मासिक किस्तें (EMIs) बढ़ सकती हैं अथवा उनकी पुनर्भुगतान अवधि का विस्तार हो सकता है।

2. नीतिगत ब्याज दर में वृद्धि से ऋण की लागत घटती है, जिससे उधार लेने और निजी निवेश को प्रोत्साहन मिलता है तथा मुद्रास्फीति को नियंत्रित करने में सहायता मिलती है।

3. उच्च ब्याज दरें घरेलू उपभोग और व्यावसायिक निवेश को हतोत्साहित कर सकती हैं, जिससे समग्र माँग और मुद्रास्फीतिक दबावों में कमी आती है।

4. नीतिगत ब्याज दर में वृद्धि बाजार में आवश्यक वस्तुओं की भौतिक आपूर्ति में सुधार करके सीधे उनके मूल्यों को कम करती है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 1 और 3

(c) केवल 2, 3 और 4

(d) केवल 1, 3 और 4


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:39 Sec.
  • Statement 1 is correct: An increase in the policy interest rate is a contractionary monetary policy measure adopted by a central bank to contain inflationary pressures. In India, an increase in the repo rate can raise the marginal cost of borrowing for banks, which may subsequently be transmitted to the lending rates charged to households and businesses. Borrowers with floating-rate home loans linked to external benchmarks, such as the RBI's policy repo rate, may experience an increase in their Equated Monthly Instalments (EMIs) or an extension of their loan repayment tenure, depending on their loan agreements and the applicable regulatory framework. However, fixed-rate loans are generally insulated from immediate changes in the policy rate during their fixed-rate period.
  • Statement 2 is not correct: An increase in the policy interest rate generally raises rather than reduces the cost of borrowing. Higher borrowing costs discourage households from taking loans for housing, automobiles and other consumption expenditure. Similarly, firms may postpone capital investments and expansion plans because of increased financing costs. The objective of such a monetary policy measure is to moderate aggregate demand rather than stimulate additional borrowing and spending. Consequently, contractionary monetary policy can help contain demand-pull inflation by reducing excessive spending pressures in the economy.
  • Statement 3 is correct: Monetary policy influences inflation through several transmission channels, including interest rates, bank lending, asset prices, exchange rates and inflation expectations. When the central bank raises policy interest rates, borrowing becomes relatively expensive, while saving may become more attractive. This can reduce consumption expenditure and private investment, thereby moderating aggregate demand. As demand pressures ease, businesses may find it more difficult to increase prices, helping inflation gradually move towards the central bank's target. However, the transmission of monetary policy occurs with a time lag, and prolonged monetary tightening may also adversely affect economic growth and employment.
  • Statement 4 is not correct: An increase in the policy interest rate does not directly improve the physical availability of essential commodities such as food grains, vegetables or petroleum products. Supply-side inflation may arise from factors such as crop failures, adverse weather conditions, geopolitical disruptions, supply-chain bottlenecks or increases in international commodity prices. Monetary tightening cannot directly address these structural and supply-related constraints. Nevertheless, it may prevent temporary supply shocks from generating persistent inflation by moderating aggregate demand and anchoring inflation expectations. Measures such as improved agricultural productivity, infrastructure development, better logistics and supply-chain management are more directly relevant to addressing supply-side inflation.
  • Hence, statements 1 and 3 are correct, while statements 2 and 4 are incorrect. Therefore, option (B) is the correct answer.
24

Consider the following statements regarding tokenisation of real-world financial assets:

1.Tokenisation can involve representing rights relating to an underlying asset through digital tokens.

2.Tokenisation automatically removes all legal, custodial and settlement risks associated with an asset.

3. A token representing an asset is not necessarily equivalent to a direct legal ownership claim over that asset in every jurisdiction.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 only

(b) 1 and 2 only

(c) 1 and 3 only

(d) 2 and 3 only

वास्तविक जगत की वित्तीय परिसंपत्तियों के टोकनीकरण के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. टोकनीकरण में किसी अंतर्निहित परिसंपत्ति से संबंधित अधिकारों को डिजिटल टोकनों के माध्यम से निरूपित करना शामिल हो सकता है।

2. टोकनीकरण किसी परिसंपत्ति से संबद्ध सभी विधिक, अभिरक्षा-संबंधी और निपटान-संबंधी जोखिमों को स्वतः समाप्त कर देता है।

3. किसी परिसंपत्ति का प्रतिनिधित्व करने वाला टोकन प्रत्येक अधिकार क्षेत्र में उस परिसंपत्ति पर प्रत्यक्ष विधिक स्वामित्व के दावे के समतुल्य हो, यह आवश्यक नहीं है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1

(b) केवल 1 और 2

(c) केवल 1 और 3

(d) केवल 2 और 3


 Economics (Macroeconomics) c

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:21 Sec.
  • Tokenisation of real-world financial assets refers to the process of representing financial assets or rights associated with them in the form of digital tokens, generally using Distributed Ledger Technology (DLT), such as blockchain. These underlying assets may include equities, bonds, government securities and other financial instruments. Tokenisation has the potential to improve the efficiency, transparency and accessibility of financial markets by facilitating faster transactions, automated settlement and fractional ownership of assets.
  • Statement 1 is correct: Tokenisation can involve the creation of digital tokens representing ownership interests, contractual claims or other economic rights associated with an underlying asset. For instance, a bond may be tokenised to represent an investor's entitlement to interest payments and repayment of principal. Depending on the legal and technological framework, such tokens may be transferred or traded through digital platforms, potentially reducing transaction costs and improving market liquidity.
  • Statement 2 is incorrect: Tokenisation does not automatically eliminate the legal, custodial or settlement risks associated with financial assets. Although blockchain-based systems may improve record-keeping, transparency and settlement efficiency, risks relating to legal enforceability, cybersecurity, asset custody, counterparty defaults and technological failures may continue to exist. The Bank for International Settlements (BIS) has highlighted that tokenisation may transform existing financial risks rather than eliminate them entirely.
  • Statement 3 is correct: Possession of a digital token does not necessarily confer direct legal ownership of the underlying asset in every jurisdiction. The legal rights associated with a token depend on the applicable regulatory framework, contractual arrangements and structure of the tokenisation mechanism. In certain arrangements, token holders may possess only a contractual claim against the issuer or intermediary rather than direct ownership rights over the underlying asset. Therefore, the legal status and enforceability of tokenised assets may differ across jurisdictions.
  • Tokenisation is emerging as an important development in financial market infrastructure, with institutions such as the BIS and International Monetary Fund (IMF) examining its implications for financial stability, settlement systems and regulatory oversight. While it offers opportunities for greater efficiency and financial innovation, appropriate legal safeguards, governance mechanisms and risk management frameworks remain essential.
  • Hence, statements 1 and 3 are correct, and option (c) is the correct answer.

Consider the following statements about the Open Network for Digital Commerce (ONDC):

1.ONDC enables different buyer-side and seller-side applications to participate through an interoperable network.

2. A seller participating in ONDC must necessarily operate an exclusive application owned by ONDC.

3.ONDC is intended to reduce dependence on closed, platform-centric digital commerce ecosystems.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 only

(b) 1 and 3 only

(c) 2 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

ओपन नेटवर्क फॉर डिजिटल कॉमर्स (ONDC) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. ONDC विभिन्न क्रेता-पक्षीय और विक्रेता-पक्षीय अनुप्रयोगों को एक अंतर-संचालनीय नेटवर्क के माध्यम से भागीदारी करने में सक्षम बनाता है।

2. ONDC में भाग लेने वाले किसी विक्रेता को अनिवार्यतः ONDC के स्वामित्व वाले एक विशिष्ट अनुप्रयोग का संचालन करना होता है।

3. ONDC का उद्देश्य बंद, प्लेटफॉर्म-केंद्रित डिजिटल वाणिज्य पारितंत्रों पर निर्भरता कम करना है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1

(b) केवल 1 और 3

(c) केवल 2 और 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:29 Sec.
  • The Open Network for Digital Commerce (ONDC) is an initiative launched by the Department for Promotion of Industry and Internal Trade (DPIIT), Ministry of Commerce and Industry, to promote an open, interoperable and inclusive digital commerce ecosystem in India. It seeks to democratise digital commerce by enabling buyers and sellers to transact through different applications connected to a common network, thereby reducing dependence on a few dominant e-commerce platforms.
  • Statement 1 is correct: ONDC operates on an open network architecture that enables interoperability between different buyer-side and seller-side applications. Unlike conventional e-commerce platforms, where buyers and sellers generally interact within the same platform, ONDC allows a buyer using one participating application to discover and purchase products from sellers registered through another participating application. This interoperability is enabled through open protocols and network specifications.
  • Statement 2 is not correct: Sellers participating in ONDC are not required to operate an exclusive application owned by ONDC. They can join the network through participating seller-side applications or other authorised network participants that facilitate catalogue management, order processing and related services. ONDC functions as an enabling network rather than a centralised marketplace that directly owns and operates all buyer and seller applications.
  • Statement 3 is correct: ONDC aims to reduce dependence on closed, platform-centric digital commerce ecosystems by separating the network infrastructure from individual applications. It promotes wider market access for small businesses, MSMEs and local retailers, encourages competition among service providers and allows greater choice for consumers. Its open-network approach seeks to lower entry barriers and foster innovation in India's digital commerce sector.
  • Hence, statements 1 and 3 are correct, and option (b) is the correct answer.
26

A financing arrangement allows an MSME supplier to obtain funds against invoices raised on a corporate buyer. A financier discounts the accepted receivable, enabling the supplier to receive payment before the invoice maturity date. Which one of the following best describes this arrangement?

(a) Venture capital financing

(b) Trade receivables discounting

(c) Sovereign bond underwriting

(d) Foreign portfolio investment

एक वित्तपोषण व्यवस्था किसी सूक्ष्म, लघु एवं मध्यम उद्यम (MSME) आपूर्तिकर्ता को कॉरपोरेट क्रेता के नाम जारी बीजकों के बदले निधियाँ प्राप्त करने की अनुमति देती है। एक वित्तपोषक स्वीकृत प्राप्य राशि का बट्टाकरण करता है, जिससे आपूर्तिकर्ता बीजक की परिपक्वता तिथि से पहले भुगतान प्राप्त कर सकता है। निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक इस व्यवस्था का सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त वर्णन करता है?

(a) उद्यम पूँजी वित्तपोषण (Venture capital financing)

(b) व्यापार प्राप्यों का बट्टाकरण (Trade receivables discounting)

(c) संप्रभु बॉण्ड का हामीदारीकरण (Sovereign bond underwriting)

(d) विदेशी पोर्टफोलियो निवेश (Foreign portfolio investment)


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:115 Sec.
  • Trade Receivables Discounting is a financing mechanism that enables Micro, Small and Medium Enterprises (MSMEs) to obtain funds against payments due from their buyers before the actual maturity date of the invoices. It helps MSMEs address short-term liquidity constraints and meet their working capital requirements without waiting for buyers to settle their outstanding dues.
  • Under this arrangement, an MSME supplies goods or services to a corporate buyer and raises an invoice for the transaction. Once the invoice is accepted by the buyer, a financier, such as a bank or eligible Non-Banking Financial Company (NBFC), provides funds to the MSME after deducting a discounting charge. On the invoice maturity date, the buyer makes the full payment to the financier.
  • In India, this mechanism is facilitated through the Trade Receivables Discounting System (TReDS), an electronic platform regulated by the Reserve Bank of India (RBI). TReDS enables MSMEs to obtain financing against their trade receivables through a transparent and competitive process involving multiple financiers. An important feature of TReDS is that financing is provided without recourse to the MSME seller, meaning that the financier bears the buyer's payment default risk.
  • Unlike venture capital financing, which involves investment in businesses with high growth potential, or foreign portfolio investment, which involves investment in financial securities, trade receivables discounting is specifically designed to release funds tied up in unpaid commercial invoices. It thereby improves cash flow, reduces dependence on conventional working capital borrowing and promotes financial inclusion among MSMEs.
  • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer. 
27

With reference to Priority Sector Lending (PSL) in India, consider the following statements:

1. The Reserve Bank of India prescribes Priority Sector Lending targets for banks to ensure adequate credit flow to specified sectors of the economy.

2. Agriculture, Micro, Small and Medium Enterprises (MSMEs), and renewable energy are among the sectors eligible for Priority Sector Lending.

3. All scheduled commercial banks in India are required to allocate exactly 40% of their total deposits towards Priority Sector Lending.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 2 and 3 only

(c) 1 and 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

 भारत में प्राथमिकता प्राप्त क्षेत्र को ऋण (PSL) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. भारतीय रिज़र्व बैंक अर्थव्यवस्था के निर्दिष्ट क्षेत्रों में पर्याप्त ऋण प्रवाह सुनिश्चित करने के लिए बैंकों हेतु प्राथमिकता प्राप्त क्षेत्र को ऋण देने के लक्ष्य निर्धारित करता है।

2. कृषि, सूक्ष्म, लघु एवं मध्यम उद्यम (MSMEs) तथा नवीकरणीय ऊर्जा प्राथमिकता प्राप्त क्षेत्र के ऋण हेतु पात्र क्षेत्रों में शामिल हैं।

3. भारत में सभी अनुसूचित वाणिज्यिक बैंकों को अपनी कुल जमाराशियों का ठीक 40% प्राथमिकता प्राप्त क्षेत्र को ऋण देने के लिए आवंटित करना आवश्यक है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 2 और 3

(c) केवल 1 और 3

(d)  1, 2 और 3


 Economics (Macroeconomics) a

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:21 Sec.
  • Statement 1 is correct: Priority Sector Lending is a regulatory mechanism through which the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) directs banks to ensure adequate credit flow to sectors considered important for socioeconomic development. These sectors may otherwise face difficulties accessing institutional finance despite being creditworthy. PSL promotes financial inclusion, balanced economic development and improved access to formal credit.
  • Statement 2 is correct: Under the RBI's Priority Sector Lending Directions, 2025, eight broad categories are recognised: agriculture; MSMEs; export credit; education; housing; social infrastructure; renewable energy; and others. However, not every loan within these sectors automatically qualifies for PSL. Eligibility depends on RBI-prescribed conditions, including applicable loan limits and borrower characteristics.
  • Statement 3 is not correct: The Priority Sector Lending target is not calculated as a percentage of total deposits. For domestic commercial banks, excluding RRBs and Small Finance Banks, the overall target is generally 40% of Adjusted Net Bank Credit (ANBC) or the Credit Equivalent of Off-Balance Sheet Exposures (CEOBSE), whichever is higher. Targets also vary by bank category. For example, the RBI reduced the overall PSL target for Small Finance Banks to 60% with effect from financial year 2025–26.
  • Hence, statements 1 and 2 are correct, while statement 3 is not correct. Therefore, option (A) is the correct answer.


28

Ms. Sharma owns a retail electronics store in Delhi. A customer purchases electronic accessories worth ₹5,000 and makes the payment by scanning the store's UPI QR code. Assume that India's recently announced Merchant Discount Rate (MDR) framework for UPI transactions has been implemented and Ms. Sharma's store does not qualify for the small-merchant exemption.

With reference to the above transaction, consider the following statements:

1. Since the customer initiates the UPI payment, the applicable MDR would be borne by the customer rather than Ms. Sharma.

2. GST at 18% would be levied on the entire transaction value of ₹5,000, irrespective of the GST applicable to the goods purchased.

3. If the customer were a relative of Ms. Sharma and transferred ₹5,000 to her through UPI as a personal gift rather than as payment for goods purchased, the transaction would not attract MDR.

Which of the statements given above are correct?

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 2 and 3 only

(c) 3 only

(d) 1, 2 and 3

सुश्री शर्मा दिल्ली में इलेक्ट्रॉनिक वस्तुओं के एक खुदरा प्रतिष्ठान की स्वामिनी हैं। एक ग्राहक 5,000 रुपये मूल्य के इलेक्ट्रॉनिक सहायक उपकरण खरीदता है और प्रतिष्ठान का UPI QR कोड स्कैन करके भुगतान करता है। मान लीजिए कि UPI लेन-देन के लिए भारत की हाल ही में घोषित मर्चेंट डिस्काउंट रेट (MDR) रूपरेखा लागू हो गई है तथा सुश्री शर्मा का प्रतिष्ठान छोटे व्यापारियों को मिलने वाली छूट के लिए पात्र नहीं है।

उपर्युक्त लेन-देन के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

1. चूँकि UPI भुगतान की पहल ग्राहक करता है, इसलिए लागू MDR का वहन सुश्री शर्मा के बजाय ग्राहक करेगा।

2. खरीदी गई वस्तुओं पर लागू GST की परवाह किए बिना, 5,000 रुपये के संपूर्ण लेन-देन मूल्य पर 18% की दर से GST लगाया जाएगा।

3. यदि ग्राहक सुश्री शर्मा का संबंधी होता और खरीदी गई वस्तुओं के भुगतान के बजाय व्यक्तिगत उपहार के रूप में UPI के माध्यम से उन्हें 5,000 रुपये हस्तांतरित करता, तो उस लेन-देन पर MDR लागू नहीं होता।

उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 2 और 3

(c) केवल 3

(d) 1, 2 और 3


 Economics (Macroeconomics) c

Medium
Current Affairs(CA)
Detailed Analysis 		c
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:87 Sec.
  • Recent Context: In September 2026, the Government of India announced a revised framework for levying Merchant Discount Rate (MDR) on specified person-to-merchant (P2M) UPI transactions exceeding ₹2,000. The proposed framework provides for an MDR of 0.4% of the transaction value, subject to a maximum of ₹300, while exempting person-to-person (P2P) transactions and specified small-merchant payments. Originally scheduled for implementation on October 15, 2026, its rollout was deferred until after Diwali.
  • Statement 1 is not correct: Merchant Discount Rate (MDR) is a transaction-processing fee borne by merchants for accepting digital payments. The fact that the customer initiates the UPI transaction does not make the customer liable to pay MDR. Under the proposed framework, Ms. Sharma, being the merchant, would bear the applicable MDR. For a transaction worth ₹5,000, an MDR of 0.4% would amount to ₹20. The amount is distributed among participants in the payment ecosystem, including banks and payment application providers. Thus, MDR is neither a tax nor a charge collected by the government.
  • Statement 2 is not correct: GST on payment-processing services is levied on the value of the service provided, represented by the MDR, rather than on the entire underlying transaction value. In the given example, the MDR would amount to ₹20, and GST at 18% on this fee would be ₹3.60. Therefore, the total processing charge, including GST, would be ₹23.60. This should not be confused with the GST applicable to the electronic accessories purchased, which is determined independently according to the classification of those goods.
  • Statement 3 is correct: Under the revised framework, genuine person-to-person (P2P) UPI transactions remain exempt from MDR, irrespective of the amount transferred. Therefore, if the customer were a relative of Ms. Sharma and transferred ₹5,000 as a personal gift, rather than as consideration for goods or services, the transaction would not attract MDR. The important distinction is between a commercial payment and a genuine personal transfer. Merely transferring a commercial payment through a personal UPI account would not automatically convert it into an exempt P2P transaction.
