Consider the following pairs regarding important price indices and their measurement:

Price Index What it measures
1. Consumer Price Index (CPI) Changes in the prices paid by households for consumption goods and services
2. Wholesale Price Index (WPI)Changes in prices received by producers for all goods and services
3. Output Producer Price Index (OPPI)Changes in prices received by domestic producers for their output

How many of the above pairs are correctly matched?

(a) Only one

(b) Only two

(c) All three

(d) None 

 प्रमुख मूल्य सूचकांकों और उनके मापन के संबंध में, निम्नलिखित युग्मों पर विचार कीजिए:

मूल्य सूचकांकमापन का विषय
1. उपभोक्ता मूल्य सूचकांक (CPI)उपभोग की वस्तुओं और सेवाओं के लिए परिवारों द्वारा भुगतान किए जाने वाले मूल्यों में परिवर्तन
2. थोक मूल्य सूचकांक (WPI)

सभी वस्तुओं और सेवाओं के लिए उत्पादकों को प्राप्त होने वाले मूल्यों में परिवर्तन

3. निर्गत उत्पादक मूल्य सूचकांक (OPPI)

उपर्युक्त युग्मों में से कितने सही सुमेलित हैं?

(a) केवल एक

(b) केवल दो

(c) सभी तीनों

(d) कोई नहीं


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:44 Sec.
  • Pair 1 is correctly matched: The Consumer Price Index (CPI) measures changes in the prices paid by households for a representative basket of consumption goods and services. It reflects inflation from the consumer's perspective and includes applicable indirect taxes and distribution margins in the prices paid.
  • Pair 2 is incorrectly matched: The Wholesale Price Index (WPI) measures changes in prices of goods at the wholesale stage of transactions. In India, it covers primary articles, fuel and power, and manufactured products, but does not cover services. Therefore, it cannot be described as measuring prices received by producers for all goods and services.
  • Pair 3 is correctly matched: The Output Producer Price Index (OPPI) measures changes in prices received by domestic producers for their output. Unlike WPI, PPI is conceptually aligned with producer prices and excludes indirect taxes on products. India introduced the new OPPI series with base year 2022–23 in June 2026.

Which of the following transactions are normally recorded in the current account of a country's Balance of Payments?

1. Export of software services

2. Remittances received from workers abroad

3. Foreign direct investment in an automobile factory

4. Interest paid to foreign holders of domestic bonds

Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) 1 and 2 only

(b) 1, 2 and 4 only

(c) 2, 3 and 4 only

(d) 1, 2, 3 and 4

निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से लेन-देन सामान्यतः किसी देश के भुगतान संतुलन के चालू खाते में दर्ज किए जाते हैं?

1. सॉफ्टवेयर सेवाओं का निर्यात

2. विदेश में कार्यरत श्रमिकों से प्राप्त विप्रेषण

3. किसी ऑटोमोबाइल कारखाने में प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश

4. घरेलू बॉण्डों के विदेशी धारकों को भुगतान किया गया ब्याज

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

(a) केवल 1 और 2

(b) केवल 1, 2 और 4

(c) केवल 2, 3 और 4

(d) 1, 2, 3 और 4


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:19 Sec.
  • The Balance of Payments records economic transactions between residents of a country and the rest of the world. Its current account includes transactions involving goods, services, primary income and secondary income. Export earnings from software services are classified as service exports and therefore form part of the current account. Hence, Item 1 is correct.
  • Personal remittances received from workers abroad are generally recorded under secondary income when they constitute transfers between residents and non-residents. Interest payments made to foreign holders of domestic bonds are recorded under primary income, as they represent income arising from financial investments. Both are current-account transactions, although they are recorded under different components. Hence, options 2 and 4 are correct.
  • Foreign direct investment in a domestic automobile factory represents a cross-border investment transaction that generally creates an ownership or financial claim. Such investment is recorded in the financial account rather than the current account. This distinction is important because the current account primarily records trade and income flows, whereas the financial account records transactions in financial assets and liabilities. Hence, option 3 is incorrect. 
  • Hence, Option (B) is the correct answer.

Consider the following statements:

Assertion (A): A rise in government bond yields may reduce the market value of previously issued fixed-rate bonds.

Reason (R): The fixed coupon payments of existing bonds become relatively less attractive when newly issued bonds offer higher yields.

Select the correct answer using the code given below.

(a) Both A and R are true, and R correctly explains A.

(b) Both A and R are true, but R does not explain A.

(c) A is true, but R is false.

(d) A is false, but R is true.

निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

अभिकथन (A): सरकारी बॉण्डों के प्रतिफल में वृद्धि से पूर्व में जारी स्थिर दर वाले बॉण्डों का बाजार मूल्य घट सकता है।

कारण (R): जब नए जारी बॉण्ड अधिक प्रतिफल प्रदान करते हैं, तो मौजूदा बॉण्डों के नियत कूपन भुगतान तुलनात्मक रूप से कम आकर्षक हो जाते हैं।

नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

(a) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या है।

(b) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या नहीं है।

(c) A सही है, किंतु R गलत है।

(d) A गलत है, किंतु R सही है।


 Economics (Macroeconomics) a

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		a
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:77 Sec.
  • Government bonds are debt instruments through which governments borrow money from investors. Many government securities carry fixed coupon payments, while their market prices fluctuate according to prevailing interest rates, demand, supply and expectations. An increase in market yields generally causes the prices of previously issued fixed-rate bonds to decline. Hence, the Assertion is correct.
  • Consider an existing bond offering a fixed annual coupon of ₹60 on a face value of ₹1,000. If comparable newly issued bonds offer higher market yields, investors would be less willing to purchase the older bond at its original price. Its market price would therefore need to fall to provide a competitive yield to new buyers. Hence, the Reason is correct.
  • The yield-to-maturity of a bond is influenced by its market price, remaining coupon payments, principal repayment and time to maturity. Bond prices and yields therefore generally move inversely for fixed-rate securities. This relationship also explains why rising interest rates can produce mark-to-market losses on banks' and investors' existing bond portfolios. The Reason directly explains the Assertion.
  • Hence, Option (A) is the correct answer.

Which one of the following best explains double deflation in the estimation of real value added?

(a) Deflating nominal GDP using both CPI and WPI and then averaging the results.

(b) Deflating output and intermediate consumption separately using appropriate price indices before calculating real value added.

(c) Adjusting nominal GDP twice for changes in the exchange rate.

(d) Subtracting both direct and indirect taxes from national income.

वास्तविक मूल्यवर्धन के आकलन में ‘दोहरे अपस्फीतन’ का निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त स्पष्टीकरण है?

(a) CPI और WPI, दोनों का उपयोग करके नाममात्र GDP का अपस्फीतन करना तथा फिर प्राप्त परिणामों का औसत निकालना।

(b) वास्तविक मूल्यवर्धन की गणना करने से पहले उपयुक्त मूल्य सूचकांकों का उपयोग करके उत्पादन और मध्यवर्ती उपभोग का अलग-अलग अपस्फीतन करना।

(c)  विनिमय दर में परिवर्तनों के लिए नाममात्र GDP को दो बार समायोजित करना।

(d) राष्ट्रीय आय से प्रत्यक्ष और अप्रत्यक्ष, दोनों करों को घटाना।


 Economics (Macroeconomics) b

Medium
Fundamental(F)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:19 Sec.
  • Double deflation is a method used in national income accounting to estimate real value added by separately adjusting the value of output and intermediate consumption for price changes. Unlike single deflation, which may use one price index for nominal value added, double deflation seeks to account for differences in price movements between final output and production inputs.
  • Under this method, gross output is deflated using an appropriate output price index, while intermediate consumption is deflated using an appropriate input price index. The volume measure of value added is then derived by subtracting real intermediate consumption from real gross output. This approach can provide a more accurate measurement of real production when output prices and input prices change at different rates.
  • Double deflation is especially relevant in sectors where input costs and product prices experience divergent movements. For example, a manufacturer may face rising prices for imported raw materials while the prices of finished products increase only marginally. Using a single deflator may distort the measurement of real value added. The other options incorrectly associate double deflation with consumer indices, exchange-rate adjustment or tax subtraction.

Consider the following statements:

Statement I: A reduction in the policy repo rate by the RBI may not immediately translate into an equivalent reduction in lending rates charged by all commercial banks.

Statement II: Monetary policy transmission is influenced by banks' funding costs, liquidity conditions, risk assessments and the structure of lending contracts.

Which one of the following statements is correct?

(a) Both statements are correct, and Statement II explains Statement I.

(b) Both statements are correct, but Statement II does not explain Statement I.

(c) Statement I is correct, but Statement II is incorrect.

(d) Statement I is incorrect, but Statement II is correct.

 निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

कथन-I: RBI द्वारा नीतिगत रेपो दर में कमी किए जाने से सभी वाणिज्यिक बैंकों द्वारा वसूल की जाने वाली ऋण दरों में तुरंत समान मात्रा में कमी हो जाए, यह आवश्यक नहीं है।

कथन-II: मौद्रिक नीति का संचरण बैंकों की निधि जुटाने की लागत, तरलता की स्थिति, जोखिम आकलनों तथा ऋण अनुबंधों की संरचना से प्रभावित होता है।

उपर्युक्त कथनों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

(a) कथन-I और कथन-II दोनों सही हैं तथा कथन-II, कथन-I की सही व्याख्या है।

(b) कथन-I और कथन-II दोनों सही हैं तथा कथन-II, कथन-I की सही व्याख्या नहीं है।

(c) कथन-I सही है, किंतु कथन-II गलत है।

(d) कथन-I गलत है, किंतु कथन-II सही है।


 Economics (Macroeconomics) a

Medium
Current Affairs(CA)
Detailed Analysis 		d
Incorrect
:-0.66 Marks
Time:87 Sec.
  • The policy repo rate is an important instrument through which the Reserve Bank of India influences monetary and financial conditions. A reduction in the repo rate generally aims to reduce borrowing costs and support economic activity. However, commercial banks may not immediately or fully pass on this reduction to borrowers. Hence, Statement I is correct.
  • Banks determine lending rates using several factors, including the cost of deposits, market liquidity, credit risk, operating expenses and the terms of existing loans. Loans linked to external benchmarks may reprice differently from loans linked to internal benchmarks. Deposit rates and banks' funding costs may also adjust more slowly than the policy repo rate. Hence, Statement II is correct.
  • Monetary transmission refers to the process through which central bank policy actions influence interest rates, credit, investment, consumption and ultimately inflation and economic output. Transmission can be delayed or incomplete because of structural characteristics of the financial system and prevailing economic conditions. The factors mentioned in Statement II explain why a reduction in the policy rate need not produce an equivalent and immediate reduction in all lending rates.
  • Hence, Option (A) is the correct answer.

An economy experiences a reduction in its annual inflation rate from 7% to 4%, while the overall price level continues to rise. Which one of the following terms best describes this situation?

(a) Deflation

(b) Disinflation

(c) Reflation

(d) Stagflation

किसी अर्थव्यवस्था की वार्षिक मुद्रास्फीति दर 7% से घटकर 4% हो जाती है, जबकि समग्र मूल्य स्तर में वृद्धि जारी रहती है। निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक पद इस स्थिति का सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त वर्णन करता है?

(a) अपस्फीति

(b) अवस्फीति

(c) पुनःस्फीति

(d) मुद्रास्फीतिजनित मंदी


 Economics (Indian Economy) b

Easy
Current Affairs(CA)
Detailed Analysis 		b
Correct
:2 Marks
Time:25 Sec.
  • Disinflation refers to a situation in which the rate of inflation declines over time, while the general price level in an economy continues to increase. In other words, prices are still rising, but at a slower pace than before. It is important to distinguish disinflation from deflation, as the former indicates a reduction in the rate of price increases, whereas the latter refers to an actual decline in the overall price level.
  • In the given situation, the annual inflation rate decreases from 7% to 4%. This indicates that goods and services, which were previously becoming more expensive at an annual rate of 7%, are now becoming more expensive at a rate of 4%. Since the inflation rate remains positive, the general price level continues to rise, although at a reduced pace. Therefore, the situation represents disinflation rather than deflation.
  • Disinflation may occur due to several factors, including contractionary monetary policy, moderation in aggregate demand, improvements in supply conditions, declining commodity prices or the easing of supply-chain disruptions. For instance, when the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) raises policy interest rates to contain inflationary pressures, borrowing and spending may moderate, potentially leading to a decline in the inflation rate. However, disinflation does not necessarily imply an economic slowdown, as it may also result from favourable supply-side developments.
  • Disinflation must also be distinguished from other related economic concepts. Deflation refers to a sustained decline in the general price level, generally reflected in a negative inflation rate. Reflation refers to policies or developments aimed at raising aggregate demand and restoring prices or inflation towards desired levels, particularly following a period of economic weakness. Stagflation refers to the simultaneous occurrence of high inflation, stagnant economic growth and elevated unemployment.
  • Hence, option (B) is the correct answer.

Consider the following pairs:

Regulatory Body

Role


1. SEBIRegulation of the securities market
2. PFRDARegulation of the National Pension System
3. DICGCInsurance of deposits in eligible banks
4. IRDAIRegulation of the government securities market

How many of the above pairs are correctly matched?

    (a) Only one

    (b) Only two

    (c) Only three

    (d) All four

    निम्नलिखित युग्मों पर विचार कीजिए:

    नियामक निकायभूमिका
    1.  SEBIप्रतिभूति बाजार का विनियमन
    2. PFRDAराष्ट्रीय पेंशन प्रणाली का विनियमन
    3. DICGCपात्र बैंकों में जमाराशियों का बीमा
    4.  IRDAIसरकारी प्रतिभूति बाजार का विनियमन

    उपर्युक्त युग्मों में से कितने सही सुमेलित हैं?

    (a) केवल एक

    (b) केवल दो

    (c)  केवल तीन

    (d) सभी चारों


    		 Economics (Indian Economy) c

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		c
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:18 Sec.
    • India's financial sector is regulated by various statutory and specialised institutions responsible for ensuring financial stability, investor protection, transparency and orderly functioning of financial markets. These institutions exercise regulatory or supervisory functions over specific segments of the financial system, including securities, pensions, insurance and banking.
    • Pair 1 is correctly matched: The Securities and Exchange Board of India (SEBI) is the statutory regulatory authority responsible for regulating India's securities market. Established in 1988 and granted statutory status under the SEBI Act, 1992, it aims to protect investors, promote the development of the securities market and regulate intermediaries such as stockbrokers, mutual funds and merchant bankers.
    • Pair 2 is correctly matched: The Pension Fund Regulatory and Development Authority (PFRDA) regulates and promotes the development of the pension sector in India, particularly the National Pension System (NPS). It was established under the PFRDA Act, 2013, and is responsible for protecting subscribers' interests, regulating pension fund managers and ensuring transparency in pension-related activities.
    • Pair 3 is correctly matched: The Deposit Insurance and Credit Guarantee Corporation (DICGC) is a wholly owned subsidiary of the Reserve Bank of India (RBI), responsible for providing deposit insurance to depositors of eligible banks. Under the existing framework, eligible deposits, including savings, fixed, current and recurring deposits, are insured up to ₹5 lakh per depositor per bank, covering both principal and interest, subject to applicable conditions.
    • Pair 4 is incorrectly matched: The Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority of India (IRDAI) is responsible for regulating and developing India's insurance and reinsurance sectors, rather than the government securities market. The government securities market is primarily regulated by the Reserve Bank of India (RBI), under the applicable statutory framework, including the Government Securities Act, 2006.
    • Thus, SEBI, PFRDA and DICGC are correctly matched with their respective functions, whereas IRDAI is incorrectly associated with the government securities market.
    • Hence, three pairs are correctly matched, and option (c) is the correct answer.

    Consider the following wetlands in India:

    1. Sukhna Wetland

    2. Sauj Jheel

    3. Shekha Jheel

    4. Surajpur Wetland

    5. Sultanpur National Park

    Which of the above are designated Ramsar Sites under the Ramsar Convention?

    (a) 1, 2 and 3 only

    (b) 1, 2, 3 and 5 only

    (c) 2, 3, 4 and 5 only

    (d) 1, 2, 3, 4 and 5

     भारत की निम्नलिखित आर्द्रभूमियों पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. सुखना आर्द्रभूमि

    2. सौज झील

    3. शेखा झील

    4. सूरजपुर आर्द्रभूमि

    5. सुल्तानपुर राष्ट्रीय उद्यान

    उपर्युक्त में से किन्हें रामसर अभिसमय के अंतर्गत रामसर स्थलों के रूप में अभिहित किया गया है?

    (a) केवल 1, 2 और 3

    (b) केवल 1, 2, 3 और 5

    (c) केवल 2, 3, 4 और 5

    (d) 1, 2, 3, 4 और 5


    		 Environment b

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		d
    Incorrect
    :-0.66 Marks
    Time:38 Sec.
    • Sukhna Wetland, located in Chandigarh at the foothills of the Shivalik Hills, was designated a Ramsar Site in October 2026. It is a human-made, rain-fed reservoir constructed in 1958 and receives water from seasonal streams originating in the Shivalik catchment. The wetland performs important ecological functions, including sediment retention, water-flow regulation and supporting biodiversity in an urban landscape. It provides feeding, breeding, roosting and wintering habitats for migratory birds along the Central Asian Flyway. Important species associated with the wetland include the vulnerable common pochard, endangered Pallas's fish eagle and endangered black pond turtle. It has been assigned Ramsar Site Number 2602. Hence, statement 1 is correct.
    • Sauj Jheel, located in Uttar Pradesh, was designated a Ramsar Site in October 2026, along with Sukhna Wetland. According to the Ministry of Environment, Forest and Climate Change, these two additions increased India's reported number of Ramsar Sites to 103 as of 15 October 2026. Its inclusion highlights the ecological significance of freshwater wetlands in supporting biodiversity, maintaining ecological processes and providing habitat for wetland-dependent organisms. The designation also reinforces the importance of conserving wetlands beyond formally notified national parks and wildlife sanctuaries. Hence, statement 2 is correct.
    • Shekha Jheel Bird Sanctuary is located in Aligarh district of Uttar Pradesh and was announced as India's 99th Ramsar Site on 22 April 2026, becoming the 12th Ramsar Site of Uttar Pradesh at that time. It is a freshwater wetland associated with the Upper Ganga Canal system and serves as an important stopover for migratory birds travelling along the Central Asian Flyway. Important bird species include the bar-headed goose, painted stork and several migratory ducks. The wetland also supports aquatic biodiversity and provides ecological services such as habitat conservation and water regulation. Hence, statement 3 is correct.
    • Surajpur Wetland is located in Gautam Buddha Nagar district, near Greater Noida, Uttar Pradesh. It is an important freshwater wetland situated within the Yamuna River basin and supports several resident and migratory bird species, including the spot-billed duck, lesser whistling duck, bar-headed goose and sarus crane. The wetland has been the focus of conservation and habitat-restoration initiatives involving the Uttar Pradesh Forest Department, WWF-India and the Greater Noida Industrial Development Authority. However, despite its ecological significance, Surajpur Wetland is not included in the official Ramsar List as of October 2026. Its recognition as an important bird habitat does not automatically confer Ramsar status. Hence, statement 4 is incorrect.
    • Sultanpur National Park is located in Gurugram district of Haryana and was designated a Ramsar Site in 2021. It is an important wetland ecosystem supporting more than 220 species of resident and migratory waterbirds. Its shallow freshwater habitat provides feeding, breeding and wintering grounds for several threatened species, including the critically endangered sociable lapwing and the endangered Egyptian vulture and Pallas's fish eagle. The wetland receives water from neighbouring canals and agricultural fields, along with contributions from groundwater. Its designation illustrates that a protected area can simultaneously possess national park status under domestic legislation and Ramsar recognition under an international environmental convention. Hence, statement 5 is correct.

    With reference to the Nairobi Convention, recently seen in the news in connection with its 12th Conference of the Parties (COP12), which one of the following statements best describes its primary objective?

    (a) It is an international agreement aimed at regulating maritime trade and ensuring freedom of navigation across the Indian Ocean.

    (b) It is a regional environmental agreement aimed at protecting, managing and developing the marine and coastal environment of the Western Indian Ocean.

    (c) It is a global treaty aimed at preventing marine pollution caused exclusively by shipping activities and offshore oil exploration.

    (d) It is a regional agreement aimed at promoting sustainable fisheries and regulating deep-sea mining activities across the entire Indian Ocean.

    हाल ही में अपने पक्षकारों के 12वें सम्मेलन (COP12) के संबंध में समाचारों में रहे नैरोबी अभिसमय के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा एक उसके प्रमुख उद्देश्य का सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त वर्णन करता है?

    (a) यह हिंद महासागर में समुद्री व्यापार का विनियमन करने और नौवहन की स्वतंत्रता सुनिश्चित करने के उद्देश्य वाला एक अंतर्राष्ट्रीय समझौता है।

    (b) यह पश्चिमी हिंद महासागर के समुद्री और तटीय पर्यावरण के संरक्षण, प्रबंधन एवं विकास के उद्देश्य वाला एक क्षेत्रीय पर्यावरणीय समझौता है।

    (c) यह अनन्य रूप से पोत परिवहन गतिविधियों और अपतटीय तेल अन्वेषण से होने वाले समुद्री प्रदूषण को रोकने के उद्देश्य वाली एक वैश्विक संधि है।

    (d) यह संपूर्ण हिंद महासागर में सतत मत्स्यन और गहरे समुद्र में खनन गतिविधियों के विनियमन को बढ़ावा देने के उद्देश्य वाला एक क्षेत्रीय समझौता है।


    		 Environment b

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		c
    Incorrect
    :-0.66 Marks
    Time:47 Sec.
    • Recent Context: The 12th Conference of the Parties (COP12) to the Nairobi Convention was held from 6–9 October 2026 in Dar es Salaam, Tanzania, marking 40 years of regional cooperation for marine and coastal environmental protection. The conference was organised under the theme “Towards a Sustainable Blue Economy in the Western Indian Ocean for People and Nature: A Journey of Forty Years.” It concluded with 15 decisions aimed at strengthening regional ocean governance, marine biodiversity conservation, pollution control and sustainable development.
    • The Nairobi Convention, formally known as the Convention for the Protection, Management and Development of the Marine and Coastal Environment of the Western Indian Ocean Region, is a legally binding regional environmental agreement developed under the United Nations Environment Programme (UNEP). It was adopted in 1985 and entered into force in 1996. The Convention forms part of UNEP's Regional Seas Programme, which promotes cooperation among countries sharing marine ecosystems to address common environmental challenges. Its primary objective is to protect marine biodiversity, prevent and control marine pollution, promote sustainable resource utilisation and strengthen integrated coastal and marine ecosystem management.
    • The Convention has 10 Contracting Parties: Comoros, France, Kenya, Madagascar, Mauritius, Mozambique, Seychelles, Somalia, South Africa and Tanzania. It provides a framework for regional cooperation in addressing transboundary environmental challenges, including marine pollution, habitat degradation, coastal erosion, climate change, invasive alien species and unsustainable exploitation of marine resources. Its conservation priorities include ecologically significant coastal and marine ecosystems such as coral reefs, mangroves, seagrass beds and coastal wetlands. It also promotes marine spatial planning, ecosystem-based management, scientific monitoring and sustainable coastal livelihoods.
    • Option (a) is incorrect: The Nairobi Convention does not regulate international maritime trade or guarantee freedom of navigation across the Indian Ocean. Such matters are primarily governed by international maritime law, particularly the United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea (UNCLOS), which establishes the legal framework for maritime zones, navigational rights and the use of ocean resources. The Nairobi Convention is concerned with regional environmental protection rather than commercial navigation or maritime trade regulation.
    • Option (b) is correct: The Nairobi Convention specifically focuses on the protection, management and sustainable development of the marine and coastal environment of the Western Indian Ocean region. It promotes regional cooperation to address marine pollution, conserve biodiversity, restore degraded ecosystems and support sustainable utilisation of marine resources. Its geographical scope and environmental objectives correspond directly to the description given in this option.
    • Option (c) is incorrect: The Nairobi Convention is neither a global treaty nor an agreement restricted exclusively to pollution caused by shipping activities and offshore oil exploration. It addresses a wider range of environmental threats, including land-based pollution, marine litter, habitat destruction, climate change and biodiversity loss. International agreements such as the MARPOL Convention specifically address pollution from ships, whereas the Nairobi Convention adopts a broader regional ecosystem-management approach.
    • Option (d) is incorrect: Although sustainable fisheries and responsible utilisation of marine resources are relevant to the Convention's wider objectives, it is not primarily a fisheries-management or deep-sea mining regulatory agreement. Moreover, its geographical jurisdiction is confined to the Western Indian Ocean region rather than the entire Indian Ocean. Deep-seabed mineral activities in international seabed areas are governed through the legal framework of UNCLOS and the International Seabed Authority (ISA).
    • Important outcomes of COP12 (2026)
    • The conference adopted 15 decisions addressing regional environmental priorities. Major areas of emphasis included strengthening the implementation of the Integrated Regional Programme (2025–2035), advancing the Regional Ocean Governance Strategy, promoting marine spatial planning, improving ecosystem monitoring and facilitating cooperation on the implementation of the Biodiversity Beyond National Jurisdiction (BBNJ) Agreement. These initiatives reflect increasing recognition of the interrelationship between biodiversity conservation, climate resilience and the sustainable blue economy in the Western Indian Ocean.
    • The Nairobi Convention is significant in the context of Sustainable Development Goal 14 (Life Below Water), the Convention on Biological Diversity (CBD) and the Kunming–Montreal Global Biodiversity Framework. It provides an important example of regional environmental governance in which countries cooperate to address shared ecological challenges that cannot be effectively managed through national actions alone.
    • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.

    An environmental monitoring team records the following observations in a freshwater ecosystem:

    • Detectable concentrations of methylmercury in phytoplankton.
    •  Higher concentrations of methylmercury in small fish feeding on plankton.
    •  Still higher concentrations in large predatory fish.
    •  Relatively low concentrations of methylmercury in the surrounding water.

    Which one of the following processes most plausibly explains these observations?

    (a) Bioaccumulation resulting from the progressive absorption of pollutants by primary producers alone.

    (b) Biomagnification resulting from the transfer and increasing concentration of persistent pollutants across successive trophic levels.

    (c) Bioremediation resulting from the microbial transformation of pollutants into more concentrated forms at higher trophic levels.

    (d) Biological amplification resulting from the progressive increase in the total biomass of organisms at successive trophic levels.

    एक पर्यावरण निगरानी दल मीठे जल के एक पारितंत्र में निम्नलिखित प्रेक्षण दर्ज करता है:

    • पादपप्लवकों में मिथाइल मरकरी की मापने योग्य सांद्रता।

    • प्लवकों को खाने वाली छोटी मछलियों में मिथाइल मरकरी की अधिक सांद्रता।

    • बड़ी परभक्षी मछलियों में उससे भी अधिक सांद्रता।

    • आसपास के जल में मिथाइल मरकरी की अपेक्षाकृत कम सांद्रता।

    निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सी एक प्रक्रिया इन प्रेक्षणों की सर्वाधिक संभावित व्याख्या करती है?

    (a) केवल प्राथमिक उत्पादकों द्वारा प्रदूषकों के क्रमिक अवशोषण से उत्पन्न जैवसंचयन।

    (b) क्रमिक पोषण स्तरों के बीच स्थायी प्रदूषकों के स्थानांतरण और उनकी बढ़ती सांद्रता से उत्पन्न जैव आवर्धन।

    (c) उच्चतर पोषण स्तरों पर सूक्ष्मजीवों द्वारा प्रदूषकों को अधिक सांद्र रूपों में परिवर्तित किए जाने से उत्पन्न जैव उपचारण।

    (d) क्रमिक पोषण स्तरों पर जीवों के कुल जैवभार में उत्तरोत्तर वृद्धि से उत्पन्न जैविक प्रवर्धन।


    		 Environment b

    Easy
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:45 Sec.
    • Biomagnification refers to the progressive increase in the concentration of persistent and poorly metabolised pollutants at successive trophic levels of a food chain. Methylmercury is a highly toxic organic form of mercury that can accumulate in living organisms and undergo biomagnification in aquatic ecosystems. Phytoplankton absorb methylmercury from the surrounding water, which is subsequently transferred to zooplankton, small fish and large predatory fish through feeding relationships. Since methylmercury is eliminated relatively slowly, its concentration generally increases at higher trophic levels. Hence, option (b) is correct.
    • Bioaccumulation refers to the gradual accumulation of a substance within an individual organism over time when its rate of absorption exceeds its rate of elimination. It can occur through exposure to contaminated water, food or sediments and is not restricted to primary producers. Although bioaccumulation contributes to the presence of methylmercury within individual organisms, the increasing concentration across successive trophic levels is specifically explained by biomagnification. Hence, option (a) is incorrect.
    • Bioremediation involves the use of microorganisms, plants or other biological agents to remove, degrade, transform or immobilise environmental contaminants. Certain microorganisms can convert inorganic mercury into methylmercury under suitable environmental conditions, particularly in oxygen-deficient sediments. However, this microbial transformation is not itself the process responsible for increasing methylmercury concentrations across successive trophic levels. Hence, option (c) is incorrect.
    • Biological amplification, as described in option (d), incorrectly associates pollutant concentration with an increase in the total biomass of organisms at higher trophic levels. In ecological food chains, the available energy and generally the biomass decrease at successive trophic levels due to energy losses during trophic transfer. Biomagnification concerns increasing pollutant concentration rather than increasing organism biomass. Hence, option (d) is incorrect.
    • Therefore, option (b) is the correct answer.

    Consider the following statements:

    Statement I: Even if the cement industry completely replaces fossil fuels with renewable energy, carbon dioxide emissions may continue during cement manufacturing.

    Statement II: The calcination of limestone during clinker production releases carbon dioxide through the chemical decomposition of calcium carbonate.

    Statement III: Carbon dioxide emissions from cement manufacturing arise exclusively from the combustion of fossil fuels used to generate high temperatures in cement kilns.

    Which one of the following is correct in respect of the above statements?

    (a) Both Statement II and Statement III are correct, and both explain Statement I.

    (b) Both Statement II and Statement III are correct, but only one of them explains Statement I.

    (c) Only one of Statements II and III is correct, and that explains Statement I.

    (d) Neither Statement II nor Statement III is correct.

    निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    कथन-I: यदि सीमेंट उद्योग जीवाश्म ईंधनों को पूर्णतः नवीकरणीय ऊर्जा से प्रतिस्थापित कर दे, तब भी सीमेंट निर्माण के दौरान कार्बन डाइऑक्साइड का उत्सर्जन जारी रह सकता है।

    (b) कथन-II और कथन-III दोनों सही हैं, किंतु उनमें से केवल एक कथन-I की व्याख्या करता है।

    (c) कथन-II और कथन-III में से केवल एक सही है तथा वही कथन-I की व्याख्या करता है।

    (d)  न तो कथन-II सही है, न ही कथन-III।

    • Statement I is correct. Cement manufacturing produces both energy-related and process-related CO₂ emissions. Even if fossil fuels are replaced with renewable energy, emissions arising from chemical transformations of raw materials may continue.
    • Statement II is correct. During clinker production, limestone (calcium carbonate) undergoes calcination, releasing carbon dioxide as a direct consequence of its chemical decomposition:

    Consider the following statements:

    1. Atmospheric aerosols consist of tiny solid particles or liquid droplets suspended in the air.

    2. All atmospheric aerosols are formed exclusively through chemical reactions involving sunlight.

    3. Secondary aerosols may form through atmospheric reactions involving sulphur dioxide, nitrogen oxides, ammonia and volatile organic compounds.

    4. Atmospheric aerosols can influence Earth's radiation balance by scattering and absorbing incoming solar radiation.

    Which of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) 1 and 2 only

    (b) 1, 3 and 4 only

    (c) 2, 3 and 4 only

    (d) 1, 2, 3 and 4

    निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. वायुमंडलीय एरोसोल वायु में निलंबित सूक्ष्म ठोस कणों अथवा द्रव की बूँदों से बने होते हैं।

    2. सभी वायुमंडलीय एरोसोल अनन्य रूप से सूर्य के प्रकाश की भागीदारी वाली रासायनिक अभिक्रियाओं से बनते हैं।

    3. सल्फर डाइऑक्साइड, नाइट्रोजन ऑक्साइडों, अमोनिया और वाष्पशील कार्बनिक यौगिकों की भागीदारी वाली वायुमंडलीय अभिक्रियाओं से द्वितीयक एरोसोल बन सकते हैं।

    4. वायुमंडलीय एरोसोल आगत सौर विकिरण का प्रकीर्णन और अवशोषण करके पृथ्वी के विकिरण संतुलन को प्रभावित कर सकते हैं।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

    (a) केवल 1 और 2

    (b) केवल 1, 3 और 4

    (c) केवल 2, 3 और 4

    (d) 1, 2, 3 और 4


    		 Environment b

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:22 Sec.
    • Atmospheric aerosols are suspensions of microscopic solid particles or liquid droplets in the atmosphere, originating from both natural and anthropogenic sources. They play an important role in atmospheric chemistry, air quality, cloud formation and Earth's climate system. Aerosols may be classified as primary aerosols, which are directly emitted into the atmosphere, or secondary aerosols, which are formed through chemical transformations of gaseous precursors. Common examples include dust, sea salt, soot, sulphate particles, nitrate particles and organic aerosols.
    • Statement 1 is correct: Atmospheric aerosols consist of fine solid particles or liquid droplets suspended in air. They may originate from natural processes such as volcanic eruptions, dust storms, sea spray and wildfires, as well as anthropogenic activities such as fossil fuel combustion, industrial emissions, construction and biomass burning. Their size, chemical composition and atmospheric residence time vary considerably. Aerosols form an important component of atmospheric particulate matter, including PM2.5 and PM10.
    • Statement 2 is not correct: Not all atmospheric aerosols are formed through sunlight-driven chemical reactions. Primary aerosols are directly released into the atmosphere through mechanical or combustion-related processes, such as windblown dust, sea salt particles and soot emissions. Secondary aerosols are produced through atmospheric chemical reactions involving gaseous precursors. Although sunlight-driven photochemical reactions contribute to the formation of certain secondary aerosols, several aerosol-forming processes, including gas-particle partitioning and some oxidation reactions, can occur without sunlight. Therefore, sunlight is not a universal requirement for aerosol formation.
    • Statement 3 is correct: Secondary aerosols can form through atmospheric reactions involving precursor gases such as sulphur dioxide (SO₂), nitrogen oxides (NOₓ), ammonia (NH₃) and volatile organic compounds (VOCs). Sulphur dioxide can undergo atmospheric oxidation to form sulphuric acid, which contributes to sulphate aerosol formation. Similarly, nitrogen oxides can form nitric acid, which may react with ammonia to produce particulate ammonium nitrate. Ammonia also contributes to ammonium sulphate formation, while the atmospheric oxidation of VOCs can generate low-volatility organic compounds that form secondary organic aerosols. These processes contribute significantly to fine particulate pollution, particularly PM2.5.
    • Statement 4 is correct: Atmospheric aerosols influence Earth's radiation balance through their interaction with incoming solar radiation. Certain aerosols, such as sulphates and sea salt, predominantly scatter sunlight back towards space, producing a cooling effect. In contrast, light-absorbing aerosols such as black carbon absorb solar radiation and contribute to atmospheric warming. Aerosols can also indirectly influence climate by acting as cloud condensation nuclei and ice-nucleating particles, thereby affecting cloud properties, precipitation processes and the Earth's energy balance. The overall climatic effect of aerosols depends on their chemical composition, size, concentration, altitude and interactions with clouds.
    • Atmospheric aerosols are therefore significant from both environmental pollution and climate change perspectives. While certain aerosols contribute to atmospheric cooling, others cause warming, and exposure to fine particulate matter poses serious risks to human health. Understanding aerosol formation, transformation and radiative effects is essential for developing effective air pollution control strategies and climate mitigation policies.

    • Recently, Iran linked hezbollah bombardment on Israel was in news. Also,Houthi rebels were in news due to the Red Sea crisis.
    • The Houthis are an armed political and religious group which champions Yemen's Shia Muslim minority, the Zaidis. They declare themselves to be part of the Iranian-led "axis of resistance" against Israel, the US and the wider West - along with armed groups such as Hamas and Lebanon's Hezbollah movement. Formally known as the Ansar Allah (Partisans of God), the group emerged in the 1990s and takes its name from the movement's late founder, Hussein al-Houthi. The current leader is his brother, Abdul Malik al-Houthi. In the early 2000s, the Houthis fought a series of rebellions against Yemen's long-time authoritarian president, Ali Abdullah Saleh. They wanted greater autonomy for the group's homeland in the north of Yemen. Hence, pair 2 is correctly matched.
    • Hostilities between Lebanon's Hezbollah and Israel have rumbled on in parallel to the Gaza war for major part of the last one year, fuelling concern about the risk of a wider conflict between the heavily armed adversaries. Iran's Revolutionary Guards founded Hezbollah in 1982 during Lebanon's 1975-90 civil war, part of Iran's effort to export its 1979 Islamic Revolution and fight Israeli forces which had invaded Lebanon in 1982. The group has risen from a shadowy faction to a heavily armed force with big sway in Lebanon and the region. Western governments including the United States designate it a terrorist group. So do Sunni Muslim Gulf Arab states including Saudi Arabia. Hence, pair 1 is not correctly matched.
    • Al-Shabaab, Somali-based Islamist militant group with links to al-Qaeda. Beginning in 2006, the group waged an insurgency against Somalia’s Transitional Federal Government (TFG). In recent times, it was in news for numerous violent attacks in Somalia including on UAE military personnel, UN Helicopters  and Ethiopian peacemakers. Hence, pair 3 is correctly matched.

    III. विश्व स्वास्थ्य संगठन 2005 से प्रतिवर्ष ‘विश्व स्वास्थ्य सांख्यिकी’ शृंखला का प्रकाशन करता आ रहा है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

    1. यह प्रकाशन केवल संचारी रोगों से संबंधित है।

    2. इसका उपयोग अंतर्राष्ट्रीय स्तर पर सहमत स्वास्थ्य लक्ष्यों की दिशा में हुई प्रगति का आकलन करने के लिए किया जा सकता है।

    3. यह विश्व स्वास्थ्य संगठन की वैश्विक स्वास्थ्य आँकड़ा प्रणाली का एक भाग है।

    नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

    (a) केवल 1

    (b) केवल 1 और 2

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d) 1, 2 और 3


    		 Social Issues c

    Medium
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		c
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:28 Sec.
    • The World Health Organization (WHO) released the World Health Statistics 2026 report on 13 May 2026.
      • The report reveals that while the world has seen major health achievements, global health gains are under serious threat due to pandemic disruptions and global shocks.
      • WHO's 2026 edition covers SDG-related health indicators and topics including healthy life expectancy, premature mortality and inequalities in immunisation. It is not confined to communicable diseases.
      • Progress toward the 2030 Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) remains uneven and slow.
    • Key Metrics, Progress, and Gaps:
      • The report details significant slowdowns in universal health coverage, ongoing financial hardship from medical costs, persistent mortality challenges, and environmental health risks.
      • Additional data on these findings and notable progress in areas like infectious disease reduction and expanded water infrastructure are detailed by Health Systems Facts.
      • A primary concern remains the severe lack of reliable data infrastructure, with only a fraction of global deaths having recorded cause-of-death data
    • Conclusion 1 is not correct: The publication is concerned only with communicable diseases:  The report covers a broad spectrum of health topics beyond communicable diseases, including age- and cause-specific mortality, non-communicable diseases, risk factors, universal health coverage (UHC), life expectancy, and COVID-19 excess mortality.
    • Conclusion 2 is correct: It can be used to assess progress towards internationally agreed health targets: The publication explicitly assesses progress towards the health-related Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) and global targets like the WHO Triple Billion targets.
    • Conclusion 3 is correct: It forms part of WHO's global health-data architecture: It serves as the WHO’s annual flagship statistical compilation and core monitoring tool integrating global health data and country reporting systems.

    During the Indian Prime Minister's visit to the Netherlands in May 2026, one of the outcomes concerned the restitution of Chola Copper Plates.

    Which of the following is the most appropriate inference from this development?

    (a) Restitution of cultural property can form part of contemporary bilateral diplomacy.

    (b) Cultural objects can be returned only under the UNESCO World Heritage Convention.

    (c) Restitution necessarily transfers sovereignty over the territory from which the object originated.

    (d) Cultural-property cooperation falls exclusively within the jurisdiction of the United Nations.

    मई 2026 में भारतीय प्रधानमंत्री की नीदरलैंड की यात्रा के दौरान प्राप्त परिणामों में से एक चोल ताम्रपत्रों की वापसी से संबंधित था।

    इस घटनाक्रम से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा निष्कर्ष निकालना सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त है?

    (a) सांस्कृतिक संपदा की वापसी समकालीन द्विपक्षीय कूटनीति का एक भाग हो सकती है।

    (b) सांस्कृतिक वस्तुओं की वापसी केवल यूनेस्को के विश्व धरोहर अभिसमय के अंतर्गत ही की जा सकती है।

    (c) सांस्कृतिक वस्तु की वापसी से उस राज्यक्षेत्र की संप्रभुता भी अनिवार्यतः हस्तांतरित हो जाती है, जहाँ से वह वस्तु मूलतः आई थी।

    (d) सांस्कृतिक संपदा संबंधी सहयोग अनन्य रूप से संयुक्त राष्ट्र के अधिकार क्षेत्र के अंतर्गत आता है।


    		 International Relations a

    Medium
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		a
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:28 Sec.
    • The official outcomes of the May 2026 visit included a 2026–2030 India–Netherlands Strategic Partnership Roadmap and restitution of Chola Copper Plates.
    •  Option (a) is correct: The return of the 11th-century Chola Copper Plates from the Netherlands to India serves as a primary example of how countries utilize direct state-to-state engagements and cultural diplomacy to resolve long-standing historical and heritage claims.
    • Option (b) is not correct: While the UNESCO frameworks provide legal guidelines, cultural artifacts can also be returned through voluntary repatriation, bilateral agreements, or direct institutional decisions (such as the agreement involving Leiden University and both governments).
    • Option (c) is not correct: Restitution relates strictly to ownership or custody of cultural objects; it has no legal effect on territorial sovereignty or borders.
    • Option (d) is not correct: Cooperation regarding cultural heritage is not exclusive to the United Nations. Sovereign nations frequently negotiate these outcomes independently on a bilateral basis.

    Consider the following:

    1. Political and security cooperation

    2. Economic and financial cooperation

    3. Cultural and people-to-people cooperation

    4. Collective military defence

    Which of the above constitute the three fundamental pillars of BRICS cooperation?

    (a) 1, 2 and 3 only

    (b) 1, 2 and 4 only

    (c) 3 and 4 only

    (d) 1, 2, 3 and 4

    निम्नलिखित पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. राजनीतिक और सुरक्षा सहयोग

    2. आर्थिक और वित्तीय सहयोग

    3. सांस्कृतिक और जन सहयोग

    4. सामूहिक सैन्य रक्षा

    उपर्युक्त में से कौन-से ब्रिक्स सहयोग के तीन मूलभूत स्तंभ हैं?

    (a) केवल 1, 2 और 3

    (b) केवल 1, 2 और 4

    (c) केवल 3 और 4

    (d) 1, 2, 3 और 4


    		 International Relations a

    Medium
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		a
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:14 Sec.
    • BRICS is an intergovernmental organization that promotes economic, political, and cultural cooperation among major emerging economies.
    • Member Countries (11 Full Members)
    • Original Founders (BRIC): Brazil, Russia, India, and China (formed in 2009)
      • First Expansion (BRICS): South Africa (joined in 2011)
      • 2024 Expansion: Egypt, Ethiopia, Iran, and the United Arab Emirates
      • 2025 Expansion: Indonesia
    • The BRICS partnership is built upon three fundamental pillars of cooperation:
      • Political and Security Cooperation: Security dialogues, counter-terrorism cooperation, and working toward a more equitable, representative, and multipolar world order. Hence option 1 is correct.
      • Economic and Financial Cooperation: Enhancing intra-BRICS trade, boosting industrial development, and strengthening financial integration through institutions like the New Development Bank (NDB). Hence option 2 is correct.
      • Cultural and People-to-People Cooperation: Fostering deeper understanding and connections through cultural exchanges, sports, education, youth initiatives, and tourism. Hence option 3 is correct.
      • Collective military defence is not a component of BRICS. BRICS is an informal socio-economic and political bloc, not a mutual military alliance or defence pact (unlike organizations such as NATO). Hence option 4 is not correct.

    • The 20th Asian GamesAichi-Nagoya 2026 took place in Aichi Prefecture and Nagoya, Japan, from September 19 to October 4, 2026.
    • Overall Standing: India finished 4th in the final medal table.

      • Medal Count: 85 total medals, comprising 21 Gold, 27 Silver, and 37 Bronze.

      •  Key Highlights:

        • India secured gold in men's cricket by defeating Pakistan in the final.

        • The men's hockey team won gold and earned qualification.

        • The women's 4x400m relay team won gold in athletics, contributing to India's 24 total medals in the discipline.

        • 17-year-old shooter Kungum Mahod won multiple medals, including gold.

    • Women’s Trap (Neeru Dhanda): Represented a first-ever achievement. Neeru Dhanda won the individual gold medal, making history as the first Indian woman to win any individual medal in trap shooting at the Asian Games.
    • Women’s Individual Golf (Pranavi Sharath Urs): Did not represent a first-ever achievement. While Pranavi Urs scripted history by winning India’s first-ever gold medal in this event, it is not India's first-ever achievement/medal in women's individual golf, as Aditi Ashok had previously won a silver medal in the same event at the 2023 Hangzhou Asian Games.
    • Women’s Recurve Individual Archery (Kumkum Anil Mohod): Represented a first-ever achievement. At just 17 years old, Kumkum Mohod won the gold medal, becoming the first Indian woman ever to win an individual recurve archery medal at the Asian Games.
    • Soft Tennis—Men’s Individual (Jay Meena): Represented a first-ever achievement. Jay Meena clinched a bronze medal in the men's singles event, securing India's first-ever Asian Games medal in the sport of Soft Tennis
    • Hence all the pairs are correctly matched.

    According to UNHCR data released in 2026, consider the following statements:

    I. A majority of the world's refugees and other people in need of international protection are hosted by low- and middle-income countries.

    II. A majority live in countries neighbouring their countries of origin.

    III. Least Developed Countries collectively host no refugees because refugee protection is principally an obligation of developed countries.

    Which of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) I only

    (b) I and II only

    (c) II and III only

    (d) I, II and III

    संयुक्त राष्ट्र शरणार्थी उच्चायुक्त (UNHCR) द्वारा 2026 में जारी आँकड़ों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    I. विश्व के अधिकांश शरणार्थियों तथा अंतर्राष्ट्रीय संरक्षण की आवश्यकता वाले अन्य लोगों को निम्न और मध्यम आय वाले देशों में आश्रय प्राप्त है।

    II. इनमें से अधिकांश अपने मूल देशों के पड़ोसी देशों में रहते हैं।

    III. अल्प विकसित देश सामूहिक रूप से किसी भी शरणार्थी को आश्रय नहीं देते हैं, क्योंकि शरणार्थी संरक्षण मुख्यतः विकसित देशों का दायित्व है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

    (a) केवल I

    (b) केवल I और II

    (c) केवल II और III

    (d) I, II और III


    		 International Relations b

    Difficult
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:33 Sec.
    • The UNHCR (Office of the United Nations High Commissioner for Refugees) is the UN Refugee Agency dedicated to protecting and assisting refugees, forcibly displaced communities, and stateless people worldwide.
    • Key Facts & Functions
      • Mandate: Founded in 1950 by the UN General Assembly, it safeguards rights under the 1951 Refugee Convention and its 1967 Protocol.
      • Headquarters: Geneva, Switzerland.
    • UNHCR releases primary statistical and programmatic data through several key flagship reports and platforms:

      • Global Trends Report: Released annually in June, this report provides official global statistics and analyzes changes in the numbers of refugees, asylum-seekers, internally displaced people (IDPs), and stateless persons.

      • Mid-Year Trends Report: Released each October, this report offers preliminary figures and analysis covering the first six months of the year.

      • Global Report: Released annually in June, this report serves as UNHCR's primary accountability and overview product, detailing activities, achievements, resource use, and operational results.

      • Global Appeal: Released each November, this report outlines UNHCR's future plans, budgets, and funding requirements for the coming year.

      • Thematic & Operational Reports: UNHCR also publishes specialized reports such as the UNHCR Education Report, emergency-focused CORE products via the Operational Data Portal, and interactive datasets through the Refugee Data Finder.

    • Statement I is correct: According to UNHCR's Global Trends data, 68 percent of the world's refugees and other people in need of international protection are hosted in low- and middle-income countries. UNHCR.

    • Statement II is correct: The data shows that proximity remains a major factor, with 65 percent of refugees living in countries neighboring their countries of origin.

    • Statement III is not correct: Least Developed Countries (LDCs) do not host "no refugees." In fact, they collectively host a significant portion (26 percent) of the global refugee population, despite representing a very small fraction of the global population and GDP.

    1. अब्राहम समझौतों के माध्यम से संयुक्त राज्य अमेरिका ने इज़राइल और कई अरब तथा मुस्लिम-बहुल देशों के बीच राजनयिक एवं आर्थिक संबंधों को सामान्य बनाने का प्रयास किया।
    2. अब्राहम समझौते राष्ट्रपति बराक ओबामा के कार्यकाल के दौरान संयुक्त राज्य अमेरिका की मध्यस्थता एवं पहल से संपन्न हुए थे।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

    		 International Relations a

    Medium
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		a
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:12 Sec.
    • Statement 1 is correct: The Abraham Accords are a set of agreements that established Arab–Israeli normalization between Israel and several Arab states, beginning with the United Arab Emirates (UAE) and Bahrain.
    • Statement 2 is not correct: The accords were brokered by the United States in 2020 during the administration of President Donald Trump, not Barack Obama.
      • The UAE and Bahrain became the first two Gulf Arab countries to formally recognize Israel and establish friendly relations.
    • Signatories and Status:
      • Israel: Core signatory.
      • United Arab Emirates (UAE): Signed September 2020 (full normalization).
      • Bahrain: Signed September 2020 (full normalization).
      • Morocco: Signed December 2020 (full normalization).
      • Sudan: Signed the general declaration in January 2021; full bilateral normalization has been delayed due to internal political instability.
      • Kazakhstan: Acceded to the framework in November 2025 (having had prior diplomatic ties since the 1990s).
      • United States: Broker and co-signatory of the framework.
      • Mediator: The United States under the Trump administration.
    • Focus Areas: Direct flights, tourism, trade, cybersecurity, technology, and joint security cooperation.

    With reference to the Sustainable Harnessing and Advancement of Nuclear Energy for Transforming India (SHANTI) Act, 2025, consider the following statements:

    1. It provides statutory status to the Atomic Energy Regulatory Board (AERB).

    2. It permits private-sector participation in nuclear energy, while certain strategic activities such as uranium enrichment and spent-fuel reprocessing remain reserved for the Central Government or its wholly owned institutions.

    3. It completely abolishes the liability of nuclear operators and transfers responsibility for compensation to the Central Government.

    4. It replaced the Civil Liability for Nuclear Damage Act, 2010.

    How many of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) Only one

    (b) Only two

    (c) Only three

    (d) All four

    भारत के रूपांतरण हेतु परमाणु ऊर्जा के सतत उपयोग एवं उन्नयन (SHANTI) अधिनियम, 2025 के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. यह परमाणु ऊर्जा नियामक बोर्ड (AERB) को सांविधिक दर्जा प्रदान करता है।

    2. यह परमाणु ऊर्जा के क्षेत्र में निजी क्षेत्र की भागीदारी की अनुमति देता है, जबकि यूरेनियम संवर्धन तथा प्रयुक्त परमाणु ईंधन के पुनर्प्रसंस्करण जैसी कुछ रणनीतिक गतिविधियाँ केंद्र सरकार या उसके पूर्ण स्वामित्व वाली संस्थाओं के लिए आरक्षित रहती हैं।

    3. यह परमाणु संचालकों के दायित्व को पूर्णतः समाप्त कर देता है तथा प्रतिकर का उत्तरदायित्व केंद्र सरकार को हस्तांतरित कर देता है।

    4. इसने परमाणु नुकसान के लिए सिविल दायित्व अधिनियम, 2010 का स्थान लिया।

    :2 Marks
    Time:18 Sec.
    • The Sustainable Harnessing and Advancement of Nuclear Energy for Transforming India (SHANTI) Bill, 2025 was introduced in Lok Sabha on December 15, 2025. The Bill replaces the Atomic Energy Act, 1962 and the Civil Liability for Nuclear Damage Act, 2010.
      • The 1962 Act provides for the development and use of atomic energy, and the 2010 Act provides a framework for assigning liability and compensation in case of a nuclear incident.
    • Statement 1 is correct: The SHANTI Act modernizes the regulatory framework and grants long-awaited statutory status to the Atomic Energy Regulatory Board (AERB), enhancing its independent enforcement capabilities.
    • Statement 2 is correct: The law breaks the long-standing state monopoly and allows private-sector participation in nuclear power operations and small modular reactors (SMRs). However, the Central Government retains exclusive sovereign control over critical, highly strategic activities such as fuel enrichment, spent-fuel reprocessing, and nuclear waste management.
    • Statement 3 is not correct: The Act does not completely abolish operator liability. Instead, it maintains the core principle of exclusive, no-fault operator liability but establishes a graded or tiered operator liability framework capped at specific amounts (e.g., ₹3,000 crore for large reactors, ₹1,500 crore for medium, and ₹100 crore for SMRs), while removing automatic statutory liability for suppliers.
    • Statement 4 is correct: The SHANTI Act unifies the legal landscape by repealing and replacing both the Atomic Energy Act of 1962 and the Civil Liability for Nuclear Damage (CLND) Act, 2010.

    भारत-ऑस्ट्रेलिया संबंधों में हाल के घटनाक्रमों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    2. साइबर, महत्वपूर्ण प्रौद्योगिकियों और आपूर्ति शृंखलाओं के लिए ऑस्ट्रेलिया-भारत साझेदारी (PACTS) का उद्देश्य डिजिटल आघातसहनीयता और सुरक्षित आपूर्ति शृंखलाओं के क्षेत्र में सहयोग को सुदृढ़ करना है।

    3. भारत और ऑस्ट्रेलिया, दोनों क्वाड तथा आपूर्ति शृंखला लचीलापन पहल (SCRI) के सदस्य हैं।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कितने सही हैं?

    (a) केवल एक

    (b) केवल दो

    (c) सभी तीनों

    (d) कोई नहीं


    		 International Relations c

    Medium
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		c
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:23 Sec.
    • India and Australia maintain a close Comprehensive Strategic Partnership focused on security, trade, and regional stability in the Indo-Pacific.
    • Diplomatic & Strategic Ties
      • Partnership Level: Upgraded to a Comprehensive Strategic Partnership (CSP) in June 2020 during a virtual leaders' summit.
      • Multilateral Alliances: Both nations are key members of the Quad, the Indian Ocean Rim Association (IORA), and the Commonwealth of Nations.
      • Defense Cooperation: Regular joint drills include AUSINDEX (naval), AUSTRAHIND (army), and participation in Exercise Pitch Black (air force), alongside a Mutual Logistics Support Arrangement.
      • High-Level Visits: Leaders hold regular Annual Leaders' Summits, with recent milestone visits taking place in July 2026 in Melbourne.
    • Economic & Trade
      • Bilateral Trade: Total two-way trade reached AUD 50.21 billion in 2025, supported by the Australia-India Economic Cooperation and Trade Agreement (ECTA).
      • Next Steps: Both countries are actively negotiating an upgraded Comprehensive Economic Cooperation Agreement (CECA) to expand services, investments, and digital trade.
      • Key Sectors: Australia supplies vital resources like coal, gold, and uranium, while partnering with India on critical minerals (lithium and rare earths) and clean energy.
    • Education, Culture & People-to-People
      • Diaspora: Over 1 million people of Indian origin live and work in Australia.
      • Education: Australia is a top destination for Indian students, with branch campuses (such as those in GIFT City) and joint research funds like the Maitri Scholarships.
      • Cultural Programs: The Centre for Australia-India Relations (CAIR) coordinates cultural exchanges, business literacy, and policy dialogues.
    • Statement 1 is correct: The India–Australia Civil Nuclear Cooperation Agreement, signed and subsequently operationalized through the finalization of the Administrative Arrangement, enables long-term, safeguarded exports of Australian uranium to India for civilian, peaceful purposes under IAEA monitoring.
    • Statement 2 is correct: The Australia–India Partnership for Cyber, Critical Technologies and Supply Chains (PACTS)—launched and operationalized during high-level bilateral summits—focuses on strengthening cooperation in cybersecurity, digital resilience, emerging tech, and secure supply chains.
    • Statement 3 is correct: Both India and Australia are members of the Quad (along with the United States and Japan) as well as the Supply Chain Resilience Initiative (SCRI), which was jointly launched by India, Australia, and Japan.

    With reference to the Pradhan Mantri Matru Vandana Yojana (PMMVY), consider the following statements:

    I. It provides maternity benefits through Direct Benefit Transfer to eligible pregnant women and lactating mothers, partly to compensate for wage loss and promote health-seeking behaviour.

    II. Under PMMVY 2.0, an eligible beneficiary can receive ₹6,000 for the second child, provided the second child is a girl.

    III. PMMVY is presently implemented as a component of the Samarthya sub-scheme under Mission Shakti.

    Which of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

    1. PMMVY combines maternal-health objectives with income support during maternity.

    2. The scheme incorporates an incentive intended to encourage positive behavioural change towards the girl child.

    3. The maternity benefit under PMMVY is available for every childbirth irrespective of the number or sex of children.

    Select the correct answer using the code given below.

    (a) 1 only

    (b) 1 and 2 only

    (c) 2 and 3 only

    (d) 1, 2 and 3

    प्रधानमंत्री मातृ वंदना योजना (PMMVY) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    I. यह पात्र गर्भवती महिलाओं और स्तनपान कराने वाली माताओं को प्रत्यक्ष लाभ अंतरण के माध्यम से मातृत्व लाभ प्रदान करती है, जिसका उद्देश्य आंशिक रूप से मजदूरी की हानि की भरपाई करना और स्वास्थ्य सेवाएँ प्राप्त करने की प्रवृत्ति को बढ़ावा देना है।

    II. PMMVY 2.0 के अंतर्गत कोई पात्र लाभार्थी दूसरी संतान के लिए 6,000 रुपये प्राप्त कर सकती है, बशर्ते दूसरी संतान बालिका हो।

    III. वर्तमान में PMMVY को मिशन शक्ति की ‘सामर्थ्य’ उप-योजना के एक घटक के रूप में कार्यान्वित किया जा रहा है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

    1. PMMVY मातृ स्वास्थ्य के उद्देश्यों को मातृत्व अवधि के दौरान आय सहायता के साथ जोड़ती है।

    2. इस योजना में बालिकाओं के प्रति सकारात्मक व्यवहारगत परिवर्तन को प्रोत्साहित करने के उद्देश्य से एक प्रोत्साहन सम्मिलित है।

    3. PMMVY के अंतर्गत मातृत्व लाभ बच्चों की संख्या या उनके लिंग की परवाह किए बिना प्रत्येक प्रसव के लिए उपलब्ध है।

    नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

    (a) केवल 1

    (b) केवल 1 और 2

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d) 1, 2 और 3


    		 Ministry-wise Schemes b

    Difficult
    Current Affairs(CA)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:31 Sec.
    •  Pradhan Mantri Matru Vandana Yojana (PMMVY),  is a Centrally Sponsored maternity-benefit scheme implemented under Section 4 of the National Food Security Act, 2013.
      • Its objectives include partial compensation for wage loss and promotion of health-seeking behaviour. 
      • Under the revised PMMVY 2.0, ₹5,000 is provided for the first child in two instalments, while ₹6,000 is provided in a single instalment for the second child if the second child is a girl.
    • Conclusion 1 is correct: PMMVY combines maternal-health and nutrition objectives with partial financial compensation for wage loss during pregnancy and lactation through Direct Benefit Transfer (DBT).
    • Conclusion 2 is correct: Under PMMVY 2.0 (launched under Mission Shakti), the scheme provides an additional cash incentive of ₹6,000 for the second child only if the second child is a girl, promoting a positive behavioral shift toward the girl child.
    • Conclusion 3 is not correct: The benefit is not available for every childbirth; it is restricted to the first live birth (₹5,000) and the second live birth only if it is a girl (₹6,000), rather than irrespective of the number or sex of the children.

    • Statement 1 is correct: India and Romania established diplomatic relations before Romania became a member of the European Union. India and Romania established diplomatic relations in 1948. Romania joined the European Union later, on January 1, 2007.
    • Statement 2 is correct: Romania is both a member of the European Union and NATO. Romania joined NATO in 2004 and the European Union in 2007.

    With reference to the Viksit Bharat–Guarantee for Rozgar and Ajeevika Mission (Gramin) [VB-G RAM G], consider the following statements:

    1. It provides a statutory guarantee of 125 days of wage employment in a financial year to eligible rural households whose adult members volunteer for unskilled manual work.

    2. It is implemented as a Centrally Sponsored Scheme, with the Centre bearing 100% of the expenditure in all States.

    3. Its permissible works emphasise areas such as water security, core rural infrastructure, livelihood-related infrastructure and mitigation of extreme weather events. 

    Which of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) 1 and 2 only

    (b) 1 and 3 only

    (c) 2 and 3 only

    (d) 1, 2 and 3

    विकसित भारत रोजगार और आजीविका गारंटी मिशन (ग्रामीण) [VB-G RAM G] के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. यह ऐसे पात्र ग्रामीण परिवारों को एक वित्तीय वर्ष में 125 दिनों के मजदूरी रोजगार की सांविधिक गारंटी प्रदान करता है, जिनके वयस्क सदस्य स्वेच्छा से अकुशल शारीरिक कार्य करने के इच्छुक हैं।

    2. इसे एक केंद्र प्रायोजित योजना के रूप में कार्यान्वित किया जाता है, जिसमें सभी राज्यों में होने वाले व्यय का 100% केंद्र सरकार वहन करती है।

    3. इसके अंतर्गत अनुमन्य कार्यों में जल सुरक्षा, बुनियादी ग्रामीण अवसंरचना, आजीविका-संबंधी अवसंरचना तथा चरम मौसमी घटनाओं के प्रभावों के शमन जैसे क्षेत्रों पर बल दिया गया है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

    (a) केवल 1 और 2

    (b) केवल 1 और 3

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d) 1, 2 और 3


    		 Ministry-wise Schemes b

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:46 Sec.
    • The Viksit Bharat–Guarantee for Rozgar and Ajeevika Mission (Gramin) (VB-G RAM G), enacted via the VB-G RAM G Act, 2025, replaced MGNREGA on July 1, 2026.
    • Key Features of VB-G RAM G:
      • Employment Guarantee: Increases the statutory wage employment guarantee from 100 to 125 days per rural household per financial year. Includes a mandatory aggregated 60-day no-work period to protect agricultural labor availability during peak sowing and harvesting.
      • Minimum Wage: Raises the minimum daily wage to at least ₹300 (over 10% higher than previous rates).
      • Payment Schedule: Mandates wage disbursements on a weekly basis, or no later than a fortnight (15 days) after work completion.
      • Four Priority Verticals: Focuses asset creation on:
        • Water security (e.g., ponds, check dams, Amrit Sarovar)
        • Core rural infrastructure (e.g., rural roads)
        • Livelihood-related infrastructure
        • Special works to mitigate extreme weather events
      • Funding & Cost Sharing: Operates as a centrally sponsored scheme with a 60:40 ratio for general states and 90:10 for Himalayan and North-Eastern states.
      • Technology & Governance: Integrates AI-enabled analytics, biometric authentication, real-time attendance, geotagging, and spatial planning tied to PM Gati Shakti through Viksit Gram Panchayat Plans (VGPPs). All generated assets merge into the Viksit Bharat National Rural Infrastructure Stack.
    • Statement 1 is correct: It provides a statutory guarantee of 125 days of wage employment in a financial year to eligible rural households whose adult members volunteer for unskilled manual work (replacing the previous 100-day limit under MGNREGA).
    • Statement 2 is not correct: It is implemented as a Centrally Sponsored Scheme (CSS) with cost-sharing (such as a 60:40 or 90:10 Centre-State sharing pattern and 100% for certain Union Territories), not 100% borne by the Centre in all States.
    • Statement 3 is correct: Its permissible works focus on water security, core rural infrastructure, livelihood-related infrastructure, and mitigation of extreme weather events.

    Consider the following statements regarding the First Past the Post (FPTP) electoral system:

    1. A candidate may be declared elected even without securing an absolute majority of the votes polled in the constituency.

    2. The system may result in a political party obtaining a higher proportion of seats in the legislature than its proportion of the total votes polled.

    3. Under this system, the percentage of seats secured by each political party necessarily corresponds to its percentage of votes.

    4. In India, elections to the Lok Sabha are conducted from single-member territorial constituencies using this system.

    Which of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) 1 and 4 only

    (b) 1, 2 and 4 only

    (c) 2 and 3 only

    (d) 1, 2 and 3 only

    सर्वाधिक मत प्राप्त करने वाले प्रत्याशी की विजय वाली निर्वाचन प्रणाली (FPTP) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    1. किसी प्रत्याशी को निर्वाचन क्षेत्र में पड़े मतों का पूर्ण बहुमत प्राप्त किए बिना भी निर्वाचित घोषित किया जा सकता है।

    2. इस प्रणाली के परिणामस्वरूप किसी राजनीतिक दल को विधानमंडल में प्राप्त सीटों का अनुपात, कुल पड़े मतों में उसके मतों के अनुपात से अधिक हो सकता है।

    3. इस प्रणाली के अंतर्गत प्रत्येक राजनीतिक दल द्वारा प्राप्त सीटों का प्रतिशत अनिवार्यतः उसके मतों के प्रतिशत के अनुरूप होता है।

    4. भारत में लोक सभा के निर्वाचन इस प्रणाली का प्रयोग करते हुए एक-सदस्यीय प्रादेशिक निर्वाचन क्षेत्रों से कराए जाते हैं।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

    (a) केवल 1 और 4

    (b) केवल 1, 2 और 4

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d)  केवल 1, 2 और 3


    		 Polity And Governance b

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:25 Sec.
    • First past the post or FPTP, also known as Simple Majority Voting, Winner-takes-all voting, or Plurality voting is the most basic form of the voting system in which the country is divided into small geographical called constituencies or districts. From each constituency, only one representative is elected. Hence such a constituency is known as a single-member constituency. Hence statement 4 is correct.
    • While every constituency elects one representative, the voter votes for the candidate, not for the party.
    • In this voting method, the candidate with the highest number of votes rather than absolute majority (50%+1) in a constituency is declared the winner. Hence statement 1 is correct.
    • There is no minimum threshold limit to cross. Even a margin of one vote results in victory. Thus, the FPTP system allows the candidate to win despite securing less than half the votes in a contest.
    • In FPTP system, a party may get more seats than votes in the legislature. Also, smaller parties representing specific groups have a lower chance of being elected in FPTP. Hence statement 2 is correct.
    • In the FPTP system, there is absolutely no link/correlation between the vote share obtained by the political parties and the concomitant number of seats secured. It is in the Proportional Representation method that every party gets seats in the legislature in proportion to the percentage of votes that it gets. Hence statement 3 is not correct.
    • Examples of FPTP in India include direct elections to the Lok Sabha and State Legislative Assemblies.

    II. Developmental democracy regards political participation as valuable because participation itself can contribute to the development of citizens' capacities and character.

    III. Protective democracy is generally associated with limited government and a sphere of individual liberty protected from excessive state interference.

    Which of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

    1. Statements I and III suggest that protective democracy values democracy primarily for restraining governmental power rather than for maximizing continuous political participation.

    3. Statements I and II suggest that protective and developmental democracy differ principally over whether citizens should possess equal voting rights.

    भारत के संविधान की उद्देशिका के संदर्भ में, निम्नलोकतंत्र के प्रतिमानों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    I. संरक्षणात्मक लोकतंत्र लोकतांत्रिक संस्थाओं को मुख्यतः ऐसे तंत्रों के रूप में देखता है, जिनके माध्यम से नागरिक स्वयं को मनमानी करने वाली अथवा स्वार्थसाधक सरकार से सुरक्षित रख सकते हैं।

    II. विकासात्मक लोकतंत्र राजनीतिक भागीदारी को मूल्यवान मानता है, क्योंकि भागीदारी स्वयं नागरिकों की क्षमताओं और चरित्र के विकास में योगदान दे सकती है।

    III. संरक्षणात्मक लोकतंत्र सामान्यतः सीमित सरकार तथा राज्य के अत्यधिक हस्तक्षेप से सुरक्षित व्यक्तिगत स्वतंत्रता के क्षेत्र से संबद्ध है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

    1. कथन-I और कथन-III से संकेत मिलता है कि संरक्षणात्मक लोकतंत्र निरंतर राजनीतिक भागीदारी को अधिकतम करने की अपेक्षा सरकारी शक्ति को सीमित रखने के लिए लोकतंत्र को मुख्यतः महत्व देता है।

    2. कथन-II से संकेत मिलता है कि विकासात्मक लोकतंत्र राजनीतिक भागीदारी को एक शिक्षाप्रद भूमिका प्रदान करता है।

    3. कथन-I और कथन-II से संकेत मिलता है कि संरक्षणात्मक तथा विकासात्मक लोकतंत्र के बीच मुख्य मतभेद इस विषय में है कि नागरिकों को समान मताधिकार प्राप्त होना चाहिए या नहीं।

    नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

    (a) केवल 1 और 2

    (b) केवल 1

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d) 1, 2 और 3


    		 Polity And Governance a

    Difficult
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		a
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:67 Sec.
    • Protective democracy proposed by political thinkers like Locke, Jeremy Bentham and James Mill. It holds that the purpose of the vote is to protect the governed from those who govern. In this model participation is instrumental, so a limited franchise activity is sufficient.
      • It sits comfortably with laissez-faire capitalism and a minimal state. The protective model treats the state as a necessary evil and the vote as a shield; consent, limited government, the separation of powers and a formal equality of voting rights are its furniture.
      • Protective Democracy → Participation as a means → protects individual liberty against government.
    • Developmental democracy, argued by Rousseau and John Stuart Mill, treats participation as valuable in itself. The developmental model treats participation as educative (people become better citizens by governing themselves) and so wants participation widened rather than economised.
      • Developmental Democracy → Participation as an end/value → develops the capacities of citizens.
    • Conclusion 1 is correct: Protective democracy treats democracy largely as a protective device. Citizens require mechanisms such as voting, consent and limited government to prevent rulers from abusing political power. Participation is therefore primarily instrumental, rather than an end in itself.
    • Conclusion 2 is correct: This captures the central proposition of developmental democracy: participation has intrinsic and educative value because involvement in collective decision-making can develop the capacities, intelligence and character of citizens.
    • Conclusion 3 is correct: The principal distinction presented here is not over equal voting rights. Protective democracy can accept formal political equality in the form of equal voting rights. The distinction concerns the purpose and value of participation.

    Consider the following statements regarding the Preamble to the Constitution of India:

    I. The Preamble describes India as a Sovereign Socialist Secular Democratic Republic.

    II. It resolves to secure to all citizens Justice, Liberty and Equality, and to promote Fraternity.

    III. The expressions Socialist, Secular and Integrity did not form part of the Preamble as originally adopted.

    Which of the following conclusions can be drawn from the above statements?

    1. In the present text of the Preamble, the word ‘Socialist’ precedes ‘Secular’, while ‘Justice’ precedes ‘Liberty’ and ‘Equality’.
