    लिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    I. उद्देशिका भारत को एक संपूर्ण प्रभुत्व-संपन्न समाजवादी पंथनिरपेक्ष लोकतंत्रात्मक गणराज्य के रूप में वर्णित करती है।

    II. इसमें सभी नागरिकों को न्याय, स्वतंत्रता और समता सुनिश्चित करने तथा बंधुता बढ़ाने का संकल्प व्यक्त किया गया है।

    III. ‘समाजवादी’, ‘पंथनिरपेक्ष’ और ‘अखंडता’ शब्द मूल रूप से अंगीकृत उद्देशिका का भाग नहीं थे।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों से निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष निकाले जा सकते हैं?

    1. उद्देशिका के वर्तमान पाठ में ‘समाजवादी’ शब्द ‘पंथनिरपेक्ष’ से पहले आता है, जबकि ‘न्याय’ शब्द ‘स्वतंत्रता’ और ‘समता’ से पहले आता है।

    2. उद्देशिका में बंधुता को न्याय, स्वतंत्रता और समता के बाद रखा गया है।

    3. वर्तमान उद्देशिका में भारतीय राज्य की प्रकृति का वर्णन करने वाले सभी शब्द 26 नवंबर, 1949 को अंगीकृत संविधान का भाग थे।

    नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

    (a) केवल 1 

    (b) केवल 1

    (c) केवल 2 और 3

    (d) 1, 2 और 3


    		 Polity And Governance b

    Difficult
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:50 Sec.
    • Statement 1 is correct:The Preamble describes India as a Sovereign Socialist Secular Democratic Republic (correct sequence.
      •  Although the words 'Socialist' and 'Secular' were expressly inserted in 1976, the original Constitution already contained provisions reflecting social justice, religious freedom and equality.
    • Statement 2 is correct: The Preamble resolves to secure to all citizens Justice, Liberty and Equality, and to promote Fraternity.
      • Justice — Social, Economic and Political
      • Liberty — Thought, Expression, Belief, Faith and Worship
      • Equality — Status and Opportunity
      • Fraternity — Assuring the dignity of the individual and the unity and integrity of the Nation
      • Justice addresses unfairness; Liberty protects individual freedom; Equality establishes equal standing and opportunity, and Fraternity encourages solidarity and dignity within society.
    • Statement 3 is correct: The words Socialist, Secular and Integrity were not present in the original Preamble adopted on 26 November 1949. They were added through the 42nd Constitutional Amendment Act, 1976.
    • An important constitutional distinction is that the Constitution was adopted on 26 November 1949, while most of its provisions came into force on 26 January 1950.
    • Conclusion 1 is correct: The word Socialist precedes Secular, while Justice precedes Liberty and Equality. This follows directly from the present wording of the Preamble and the order identified in Statements I and II.
    • Conclusion 2 is correct: Fraternity appears after Justice, Liberty and Equality. The Preamble first sets out the three objectives to be secured and then expresses the commitment to promote Fraternity.
    • Conclusion 3 is not correct: It claims that all expressions describing the nature of the Indian State were present in the Constitution as adopted in 1949. This contradicts Statement III, because Socialist and Secular were inserted only in 1976.
    • Hence, only conclusions 1 and 2 follow, thus the correct answer is option (b).

    Which one of the following statements regarding Panchayats is NOT correct?

    (a) The Constitution provides for reservation of not less than one-third of the total number of seats in Panchayats for women.

    (b) The minimum age for being eligible to be chosen as a member of a Panchayat is 21 years.

    (c) Elections to constitute a Panchayat must be completed before the expiry of its duration or, in case of dissolution, ordinarily within six months from the date of dissolution.

    (d) A Panchayat constituted following its premature dissolution is entitled to continue for a fresh term of five years from the date of its first meeting.

    पंचायतों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा एक सही नहीं है?

    (a) संविधान पंचायतों में कुल सीटों की संख्या के कम-से-कम एक-तिहाई भाग को महिलाओं के लिए आरक्षित करने का प्रावधान करता है।

    (b) पंचायत के सदस्य के रूप में चुने जाने के लिए पात्रता की न्यूनतम आयु 21 वर्ष है।

    (c) पंचायत का गठन करने के लिए निर्वाचन उसकी अवधि समाप्त होने से पहले अथवा विघटन की स्थिति में सामान्यतः विघटन की तारीख से छह माह के भीतर पूरे किए जाने चाहिए।

    (d) समय से पूर्व विघटन के पश्चात गठित पंचायत अपनी पहली बैठक की तारीख से पाँच वर्ष के नए कार्यकाल तक बने रहने की हकदार होती है।


    		 Polity And Governance d

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		d
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:43 Sec.
    • Under Article 243E, a Panchayat normally continues for five years. However, if it is dissolved before completing its term and a new Panchayat is constituted, the reconstituted Panchayat continues only for the remainder of the period for which the dissolved Panchayat would have continued. It does not automatically receive a fresh five-year term. Hence statement (d) is not  correct.
    • Article 243D provides that not less than one-third of the total seats to be filled by direct election in every Panchayat shall be reserved for women, including seats reserved for women belonging to SCs/STs. Hence statement (a) is correct.
    • A person cannot be disqualified for Panchayat membership merely because they are below 25 years if they have attained 21 years.  25 years is the minimum age for Lok Sabha/Legislative Assembly membership, whereas for Panchayats it is 21. Hence statement (b) is correct.
    • Article 243E requires elections to be completed before expiry of the Panchayat's term, or in case of premature dissolution, before the expiration of six months from dissolution—subject to the exception where the remainder of the original term is less than six months. Hence statement (c) is correct.

    Consider the following statements regarding the constitution of Parliamentary Committees in India:

    I. Members of Parliamentary Committees may be elected by the House on a motion moved and adopted for that purpose.

    II. Members of Parliamentary Committees may also be nominated by the Speaker of the Lok Sabha or the Chairman of the Rajya Sabha, as the case may be.

    III. All Parliamentary Committees are constituted through election by the respective Houses, and the Presiding Officers have no role in nominating their members.

    How many of the statements given above are correct?

    (a) I only

    (b) II only

    (c) I and II only

    (d) I and III only

    भारत में संसदीय समितियों के गठन के संबंध में निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    I. संसदीय समितियों के सदस्यों का निर्वाचन सदन द्वारा इस प्रयोजन के लिए प्रस्तुत और स्वीकृत प्रस्ताव के आधार पर किया जा सकता है।

    II. संसदीय समितियों के सदस्यों को, यथास्थिति, लोक सभा के अध्यक्ष अथवा राज्य सभा के सभापति द्वारा नामनिर्देशित भी किया जा सकता है।

    III. सभी संसदीय समितियों का गठन संबंधित सदनों द्वारा निर्वाचन के माध्यम से किया जाता है तथा उनके सदस्यों के नामनिर्देशन में पीठासीन अधिकारियों की कोई भूमिका नहीं होती है।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

    (a) केवल I

    (b) केवल II

    (c) केवल I और II

    (d) केवल I और III


    		 Polity And Governance c

    Medium
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		b
    Incorrect
    :-0.66 Marks
    Time:29 Sec.

    • Statement I is correct: Membership of Parliamentary Committees is not governed by a single method. For some committees, members are elected by the House on a motion moved and adopted for the purpose.

    • Statement II is correct: Members of certain committees are nominated by the Presiding Officer—the Speaker in the Lok Sabha or the Chairman in the Rajya Sabha.

    • Statement III is not correct: Method for constituting all Parliamentary Committees is not universal but it varies and depend on the committee whether the members may be elected or nominated.

    • A parliamentary committee means a committee that:
      • Is appointed or elected by the House or nominated by the Speaker / Chairman. 
      • Works under the direction of the Speaker / Chairman.
      • Presents its report to the House or to the Speaker / Chairman. 
      • Has a secretariat provided by the Lok Sabha / Rajya Sabha. 
    • Parliamentary committees are of two kinds:
      • Standing Committee: They are permanent (constituted every year or periodically) and work on a continuous basis. For example, the public accounts committee.
      • Ad Hoc Committees: They are temporary and cease to exist on completion of the task assigned to them. For example, committee on the conduct of certain members during the President's address. Ad hoc committees can be divided into two categories, that is, Inquiry Committees and Advisory Committees.

        • Inquiry Committees are constituted from time to time, either by the two Houses on a motion adopted in that behalf or by the Speaker / Chairman, to inquire into and report on specific subjects. For example, the committee on the Conduct of Certain Members during President’s Address.

        • Advisory Committees include select or joint committees on bills, which are appointed to consider and report on particular bills. These committees are distinguishable from the other ad hoc committees in as much as they are concerned with bills and the procedure to be followed by them is laid down in the Rules of Procedure and the Directions by the Speaker / Chairman.

    • A Joint Parliamentary Committee (JPC) is a committee of members of the Parliament of India, constituted jointly by the Lok Sabha and the Rajya Sabha, to inquire into a particular matter of public importance. The JPC has the power to summon and examine witnesses, take evidence on oath, and make recommendations to the government. The JPC submits its report to the Houses of Parliament, which may then take appropriate action. JPC can have members from both the ruling parties and the opposition.

      • The JPC is created by a resolution of either House of Parliament. The resolution must specify the terms of reference of the JPC, the number of members, and the duration of the JPC. The details regarding membership of the Joint Parliamentary Committee (JPC) and subjects are also decided by Parliament.

    Consider the following statements regarding the jurisdiction of the Supreme Court under Article 32 of the Constitution:

    1. The Supreme Court may refuse relief where the petitioner fails to establish that a Fundamental Right guaranteed by the Constitution has been infringed.

    2. The Supreme Court can exercise its jurisdiction under Article 32 for enforcement of any constitutional or legal right, even if no Fundamental Right is involved.

    3. The Supreme Court must dismiss a petition if the petitioner has sought an inappropriate writ, even when infringement of a Fundamental Right is established.

    How many statements given above are correct?

    (a) Only one

    (b) Only two

    (c) All three

    (d) None

    संविधान के अनुच्छेद 32 के अंतर्गत उच्चतम न्यायालय की अधिकारिता के संबंध में निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

    यदि याचिकाकर्ता यह स्थापित करने में विफल रहता है कि संविधान द्वारा प्रत्याभूत किसी मूल अधिकार का उल्लंघन हुआ है, तो उच्चतम न्यायालय अनुतोष प्रदान करने से इनकार कर सकता है।

    उच्चतम न्यायालय किसी भी संवैधानिक या विधिक अधिकार के प्रवर्तन के लिए अनुच्छेद 32 के अंतर्गत अपनी अधिकारिता का प्रयोग कर सकता है, भले ही कोई मूल अधिकार संबंधित न हो।

    यदि याचिकाकर्ता ने अनुपयुक्त रिट जारी करने की प्रार्थना की है, तो उच्चतम न्यायालय को याचिका खारिज करनी ही होगी, भले ही मूल अधिकार का उल्लंघन स्थापित हो गया हो।

    उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कितने सही हैं?

    (a) केवल एक

    (b) केवल दो

    (c) सभी तीन

    (d) कोई नहीं


    		 Polity And Governance a

    Difficult
    Fundamental(F)
    Detailed Analysis     		a
    Correct
    :2 Marks
    Time:54 Sec.
    • Article 32 is itself a Fundamental Right, but its purpose is specifically the enforcement of Fundamental Rights. Therefore, if no Fundamental Right has been infringed, the basis for relief under Article 32 is absent.
    • Availability of an alternative remedy is not by itself a bar to Article 32 when a Fundamental Right is infringed. Article 32 is also not a general remedy for enforcing every legal or constitutional right. Further, the Court's constitutional jurisdiction is not defeated merely because the petitioner has incorrectly identified the particular writ; the crucial issue is whether an enforceable Fundamental Right has been violated.

    • Statement 1 is correct: The primary prerequisite for moving the Supreme Court under Article 32 is the violation of a Fundamental Right. If the petitioner fails to establish that a Fundamental Right guaranteed under Part III of the Constitution has been infringed, the Court will refuse to grant the relief.

    • Statement 2 is incorrect: The jurisdiction of the Supreme Court under Article 32 is restricted only to the enforcement of Fundamental Rights. It cannot be exercised for the enforcement of an ordinary legal or constitutional right that falls outside Part III. (Unlike the High Court under Article 226, which can issue writs "for any other purpose").

    • Statement 3 is incorrect: The Supreme Court is not bound to dismiss a petition simply because the petitioner has prayed for an inappropriate writ. If the infringement of a Fundamental Right is established, the Court has the wide power to modify the relief and issue whichever direction, order, or writ is deemed appropriate to serve the ends of justice.

    • Although the right to approach the Supreme Court under Article 32 is a Fundamental Right, the Supreme Court has ruled in various judgments that it may refuse to entertain an Article 32 petition if an effective and adequate alternative remedy is available before the High Court under Article 226. The court frequently directs petitioners to first exhaust their remedy at the High Court level before approaching the apex court.

      Which one of the following statements regarding the special address of the Governor is correct?

      (a) The Governor addresses the State Legislature at the commencement of every session of the Legislature.

      (b) The special address is required only after a general election to the Legislative Assembly and not subsequently during its term.

      (c) The Governor addresses the Legislative Assembly, or both Houses in a bicameral State, at the first session after a general election and the first session of each year.

      (d) The special address is based on a legislative convention and is not expressly provided for in the Constitution.

      राज्यपाल के विशेष अभिभाषण के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

      (a) राज्यपाल राज्य विधानमंडल के प्रत्येक सत्र के प्रारंभ में विधानमंडल को संबोधित करता है।

      (b) विशेष अभिभाषण केवल विधान सभा के साधारण निर्वाचन के पश्चात आवश्यक होता है, उसके कार्यकाल के दौरान बाद में नहीं।

      (c) राज्यपाल साधारण निर्वाचन के पश्चात प्रथम सत्र तथा प्रत्येक वर्ष के प्रथम सत्र में विधान सभा को अथवा द्विसदनीय राज्य में दोनों सदनों को संबोधित करता है।

      (d) विशेष अभिभाषण एक विधायी परंपरा पर आधारित है और संविधान में इसका स्पष्ट प्रावधान नहीं किया गया है।


      		 Polity And Governance c

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:19 Sec.
      • Article 176 of the Constitution expressly provides for the Governor's special address. Hence statement (d) is not correct.
      • Article 176(1): the address comes at the first session after each general election to the Assembly and at the first session of each year ( and NOT at the commencement of every session of the legislature; also NOT only after a general election to the Legislative Assembly and not subsequently during its term). Hence statement (c) is correct and statements (a) and (b) are not correct.
      • Article 176(2) requires House rules to allot time to discuss the address: the Motion of Thanks.
      • Article 176 gives the Governor an address that is compulsory rather than discretionary, and it is delivered on the advice of the State Council of Ministers, so its content is the government's programme rather than the Governor's own opinion.
      • The House debates it on a Motion of Thanks, and amendments to that motion are one of the recognised ways of testing a government on the floor.

      Consider the following pairs:

      Article Special provision relating to
      1. Article 371-A Nagaland
      2. Article 371-B Assam
      3. Article 371-D Sikkim
      4. Article 371-I Goa

      How many of the pairs given above are correctly matched?

      (a) Only one

      (b) Only two

      (c) Only three

      (d) All four

      निम्नलिखित युग्मों पर विचार कीजिए:

      अनुच्छेदसंबंधित राज्य के लिए विशेष प्रावधान
      1. अनुच्छेद 371-Aनागालैंड
      2. अनुच्छेद 371-Bअसम
      3. अनुच्छेद 371-Dसिक्किम
      4. अनुच्छेद 371-Iगोवा

      उपर्युक्त युग्मों में से कितने सही सुमेलित हैं?

      (a) केवल एक

      (b) केवल दो

      (c) केवल तीन

      (d) सभी चारों


      		 Polity And Governance c

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:19 Sec.
      • Articles 371 to 371-J
        • Article 371 — Maharashtra and Gujarat: Empowers the President to set up separate development boards for Vidarbha, Marathwada, and the rest of Maharashtra, as well as Saurashtra, Kutch, and the rest of Gujarat, to ensure equitable fund allocation and job/education opportunities.
        • Article 371-A — Nagaland: Protects Naga religious and social practices, customary civil and criminal law, and ownership of land. Acts passed by Parliament do not apply to these matters unless the State Legislative Assembly passes a resolution. It also gives special law and order responsibility to the Governor and establishes a Tuensang district regional council. Hence pair 1 is correctly matched.
        • Article 371-B — Assam: Enables the creation of a committee of the State Legislative Assembly consisting of members elected from the tribal areas of Assam (Karbi Anglong and Dima Hasao). Hence pair 2 is correctly matched.
        • Article 371-C — Manipur: Provides for a Committee of the Legislative Assembly comprising members from the Hill Areas and grants the Governor a special responsibility to report annually on the administration of the hill areas.
        • Article 371-D & 371-E — Andhra Pradesh and Telangana: Contains special provisions for equitable opportunities and facilities in public employment and education for different local areas of the state, along with the provision for establishing a central university. Hence pair 3 is not correctly matched.
        • Article 371-F — Sikkim: Includes unique provisions following Sikkim's merger with India, safeguarding existing laws, customary rights, and specifying a minimum number of seats for Sikkimese of various origins in the State Legislative Assembly.
        • Article 371-G — Mizoram: Prohibits Parliamentary acts from applying to Mizo religious or social practices, customary law and procedure, administration of civil/criminal justice, and land ownership/transfer, unless the Legislative Assembly decides so.
        • Article 371-H — Arunachal Pradesh: Grants the Governor a special responsibility with respect to law and order, and the Governor's decision is final in these matters.
        • Article 371-I — Goa: Stipulates that the Legislative Assembly of Goa must consist of not less than 30 members. Hence pair 4 is correctly matched.
        • Article 371-J — Karnataka: Provides special status for the Kalyana-Karnataka (Hyderabad-Karnataka) region, authorizing a separate development board, local reservation in education and employment, and allocation of funds for regional development.

      Consider the following statements:

      I. The Government of India Act, 1935 provided for the establishment of a Federal Court in India.

      II. The jurisdiction of a High Court may extend beyond the State in which its principal seat is located, and a High Court may have benches at places other than its principal seat.

      Which of the statements given above is/are correct?

      (a) I only

      (b) II only

      (c) Both I and II

      (d) Neither I nor II

      निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      I. भारत शासन अधिनियम, 1935 ने भारत में एक संघीय न्यायालय की स्थापना का प्रावधान किया था।

      II. किसी उच्च न्यायालय की अधिकारिता उस राज्य से परे भी विस्तृत हो सकती है, जिसमें उसका प्रधान स्थान स्थित है तथा उच्च न्यायालय के प्रधान स्थान से भिन्न स्थानों पर भी उसकी न्यायपीठें हो सकती हैं।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

      (a) केवल I

      (b) केवल II

      (c) I और II दोनों

      (d) न तो I, न ही II


      		 Polity And Governance c

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:25 Sec.
      • Statement I is correct: The Federal Court of India was a judicial body, established in India in 1937 under the provisions of the Government of India Act 1935. Composed of only two puisne judges and a Chief Justice, the Federal Court was smaller than any of the Provincial High Courts and its jurisdiction was very limited. There was a right of appeal to the Judicial Committee of the Privy Council in London from the Federal Court of India. 

      • The Supreme Court of India came into being on 28 January 1950. It replaced both the Federal Court of India and the British Privy Council and had wider jurisdiction than its predecessor.

        Article 130 of the constitution provides that the Supreme Court shall sit in Delhi or in such other place or places, as the Chief Justice of India may, with the approval of the President, from time to time, appoint. Hence statement 3 is correct.

      • Statement II is correct: The jurisdiction of an Indian High Court can extend to more than one State or Union Territory, and High Courts can establish permanent or circuit benches outside their principal seat.
        • Common High Courts: Under Article 230 of the Constitution and the 7th Constitutional Amendment Act of 1956, Parliament may establish a common High Court for two or more States or for a State and a Union Territory.
        • Multi-State Examples: The Bombay High Court has jurisdiction over the states of Maharashtra and Goa, as well as the Union Territories of Dadra and Nagar Haveli and Daman and Diu, with benches in Nagpur, Aurangabad, and Panaji.
        • The Guwahati High Court and others similarly cover multiple northeastern states.
        • Benches Outside Principal Seats: Under Section 51 of the States Reorganisation Act, 1956, the President of India may consult the Governor and the Chief Justice to establish permanent benches away from the principal seat (such as the Allahabad High Court having its principal seat in Prayagraj and a bench in Lucknow).
        • The Chief Justice can also authorize judges to sit at other designated places or circuit benches within the jurisdiction.

      Which of the following statements with regard to juveniles and the juvenile justice system in India is/are correct?

      1. Under the Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Act, 2015, a child means a person who has not completed eighteen years of age.

      2. A child aged 16–18 years alleged to have committed a heinous offence is automatically tried as an adult under the Act.

      3. Mission Vatsalya provides preventive, statutory care and rehabilitation services to both children in need of care and protection and children in conflict with law. 

      Select the answer using the code given below.

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 1 and 3 only

      (c) 2 and 3 only

      (d) 3 only

      भारत में किशोरों और किशोर न्याय प्रणाली के संबंध में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

      1. किशोर न्याय (बालकों की देखरेख और संरक्षण) अधिनियम, 2015 के अंतर्गत बालक से आशय ऐसे व्यक्ति से है, जिसने अठारह वर्ष की आयु पूरी नहीं की है।

      2. किसी जघन्य अपराध का आरोप लगने पर 16–18 वर्ष की आयु के बालक का विचारण इस अधिनियम के अंतर्गत स्वतः एक वयस्क के रूप में किया जाता है।

      3. मिशन वात्सल्य देखरेख और संरक्षण की आवश्यकता वाले बालकों तथा विधि का उल्लंघन करने वाले बालकों, दोनों को निवारक, सांविधिक देखरेख तथा पुनर्वास सेवाएँ प्रदान करता है।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (b) केवल 1 और 3

      (c) केवल 2 और 3

      (d) केवल 3


      		 Polity And Governance b

      Difficult
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:44 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct: The Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Act, 2015 introduced provisions to handle cases of children in the age group of 16-18 years who commit heinous crimes. Key Provisions of the Juvenile Justice Act, 2015:

      • Under Section 2(12) of the Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Act, 2015, a "child" is defined as a person who has not completed 18 years of age.

      • Statement 2 is not correct:  A child aged 16–18 years alleged to have committed a heinous offence is not automatically tried as an adult. The Juvenile Justice Board (JJB) must first conduct a mandatory preliminary assessment under Section 15 of the Act to evaluate the child's mental and physical capacity, ability to understand the consequences, and the circumstances of the offense before deciding whether to transfer the case to a Children's Court.
      • Statement 3 is correct: Mission Vatsalya is a centrally sponsored scheme, wherein financial support is provided to the state Government/UT Administrations, for delivering services as mandated under the Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Act, 2015 (as amended in 2021) and Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Model Rules, 2016 (as amended in 2022).
        • The primary responsibility of execution of the Act, as such, lies with the States/UTs.
        • The Scheme aims to foster a sensitive, supportive, and synchronized ecosystem for children as they transit different ages and stages of their development.
        •  This is envisaged to be done by strengthening the institutional framework of child welfare and the Statutory and Service delivery structures in all districts of the country.

        • The Scheme provides preventive, statutory care and rehabilitation services to children who are in need of care and protection and those in conflict with law as defined under the Juvenile Justice (Care and Protection of Children) Act, 2015.

      Consider the following statements regarding Particularly Vulnerable Tribal Groups (PVTGs) in India:

      Statement-I: PVTGs constitute a separate constitutional category from the Scheduled Tribes and are specifically defined in the Constitution of India.

      Statement-II: PVTGs are found in 18 States and the Union Territory of Andaman and Nicobar Islands.

      Statement-III: PM-JANMAN aims at improving the socio-economic conditions of PVTGs by addressing gaps in areas such as housing, drinking water, education, health, connectivity and livelihoods.

      Which one of the following conclusions based on the above statements is correct?

      (a) There are two correct statements, that include Statement-I.

      (b) There are two correct statements, that are Statement-II and Statement-III.

      (c) There is only Statement-I that is correct.

      (d) All three statements are correct.

      भारत में विशेष रूप से कमजोर जनजातीय समूहों (PVTGs) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      कथन-I: PVTGs अनुसूचित जनजातियों से पृथक एक संवैधानिक श्रेणी हैं तथा भारत के संविधान में विशेष रूप से परिभाषित हैं।

      कथन-II: PVTGs 18 राज्यों तथा अंडमान और निकोबार द्वीपसमूह संघ राज्यक्षेत्र में पाए जाते हैं।

      कथन-III: पीएम-जनमन का उद्देश्य आवास, पेयजल, शिक्षा, स्वास्थ्य, संपर्क और आजीविका जैसे क्षेत्रों में विद्यमान कमियों को दूर करके PVTGs की सामाजिक-आर्थिक स्थितियों में सुधार करना है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों पर आधारित निम्नलिखित निष्कर्षों में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

      (a) दो कथन सही हैं, जिनमें कथन-I शामिल है।

      (b) दो कथन सही हैं, जो कथन-II और कथन-III हैं।

      (c) केवल कथन-I सही है।

      (d) ﻿सभी तीनों कथन सही हैं।


      		 Polity And Governance b

      Medium
      Applied(A)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:19 Sec.


      • Statement-I is not correct: Particularly vulnerable Tribal Groups (PVTGs) are a special category of tribal groups identified by the government as being more vulnerable than other tribal communities. They are not a separate constitutional category. They are especially vulnerable groups identified within the Scheduled Tribes.
      • The Government of India, based on the recommendations of the Debhar Commission, has established four main criteria to identify PVTGs –
        • Pre-agricultural Level of Technology - This refers to the use of primitive tools for subsistence-level agricultural practice.
        • Stagnated or declining population – PVTGs tend to have very low or negative population growth, which indicates their vulnerable demographic condition.
        • Extremely low literacy – Low literacy level, particularly among adults, is a major criterion for identifying these groups.
        • Subsistence-level economy - Many PVTGs practice a subsistence economy with limited market integration making them economically vulnerable.
      • Statement-II is correct: The Government of India has identified 52 PVTGs in 1975, which has increased to 75 in 1993, spread across 18 States and the UT of Andaman & Nicobar Islands.
      • Statement-III is correct: Pradhan Mantri Janjati Adivasi Nyaya Maha Abhiyan (PM-JANMAN) was launched on November 15, 2023 (announced on Janjatiya Gaurav Diwas) by Ministry of Tribal Affairs (MoTA) for the PVTGs. The mission aims to provide essential services like permanent housing, clean water, healthcare, and education to these 75 vulnerable communities across India, ensuring 100% saturation of basic government schemes.

      With reference to the All India Football Federation (AIFF), consider the following statements:

      I. The AIFF is the national governing body for association football in India and is affiliated with both FIFA and the Asian Football Confederation (AFC).

      II. The AIFF is a statutory body established by an Act of Parliament and functions directly under the administrative control of the Ministry of Youth Affairs and Sports.

      Which of the statements given above is/are correct?

      (a) I only

      (b) II only

      (c) Both I and II

      (d) Neither I nor II

      भारत में विशेष रूप से कमजोर जनजातीय समूहों (PVTGs) के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      कथन-I: PVTGs अनुसूचित जनजातियों से पृथक एक संवैधानिक श्रेणी हैं तथा भारत के संविधान में विशेष रूप से परिभाषित हैं।

      कथन-II: PVTGs 18 राज्यों तथा अंडमान और निकोबार द्वीपसमूह संघ राज्यक्षेत्र में पाए जाते हैं।

      कथन-III: पीएम-जनमन का उद्देश्य आवास, पेयजल, शिक्षा, स्वास्थ्य, संपर्क और आजीविका जैसे क्षेत्रों में विद्यमान कमियों को दूर करके PVTGs की सामाजिक-आर्थिक स्थितियों में सुधार करना है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों पर आधारित निम्नलिखित निष्कर्षों में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

      (a) दो कथन सही हैं, जिनमें कथन-I शामिल है।

      (b) दो कथन सही हैं, जो कथन-II और कथन-III हैं।

      (c) केवल कथन-I सही है।

      (d) सभी तीनों कथन सही हैं।


      		 Current Affairs a

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:21 Sec.
      • Statement I is correct: The All India Football Federation (AIFF) is the national governing body for association football in India (founded in 1937) and is affiliated with both FIFA and the Asian Football Confederation (AFC).
      • Statement II is not correct: The AIFF is not a statutory body established by an Act of Parliament. It is registered as a society under the Societies Registration Act, 1860, and functions as a National Sports Federation (NSF) recognized by the Ministry of Youth Affairs and Sports, rather than a direct statutory body under its administrative control.


      69

      Mr. X is a senior official in a State Health Department responsible for procuring medical equipment for government hospitals. During the evaluation of bids, he learns through an internal report that one of the lowest bidders may have supplied substandard equipment in an earlier project. However, the allegations are still under inquiry and have not been conclusively established. His superior asks him to ignore the report and proceed quickly, as delaying procurement may affect hospital services.

      What amongst the following would be the most appropriate course of action for Mr. X?

      1. Make the unverified allegations public immediately so that citizens can decide whether the contractor should be selected.

      2. Recommend rejection of the bidder solely on the basis of the allegations, even before their verification.

      3. Place the information before the competent procurement/oversight authority and seek appropriate verification while maintaining necessary confidentiality.

      Select the answer using the code given below.

      (a) 1 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      श्री X राज्य के स्वास्थ्य विभाग में एक वरिष्ठ अधिकारी हैं और सरकारी अस्पतालों के लिए चिकित्सा उपकरणों की खरीद हेतु उत्तरदायी हैं। बोलियों के मूल्यांकन के दौरान उन्हें एक आंतरिक रिपोर्ट से पता चलता है कि सबसे कम बोली लगाने वालों में से किसी एक ने संभवतः एक पूर्व परियोजना में घटिया गुणवत्ता वाले उपकरणों की आपूर्ति की थी। हालाँकि, आरोपों की अभी जाँच चल रही है और वे निर्णायक रूप से सिद्ध नहीं हुए हैं। उनके वरिष्ठ अधिकारी उनसे रिपोर्ट की अनदेखी करके शीघ्र आगे बढ़ने को कहते हैं, क्योंकि खरीद में विलंब से अस्पताल की सेवाएँ प्रभावित हो सकती हैं।

      श्री X के लिए निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सी कार्रवाई सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त होगी?

      1. अप्रमाणित आरोपों को तुरंत सार्वजनिक कर देना, ताकि नागरिक यह तय कर सकें कि ठेकेदार का चयन किया जाना चाहिए या नहीं।

      2. आरोपों के सत्यापन से पहले ही, केवल उनके आधार पर बोलीदाता की बोली अस्वीकार करने की अनुशंसा करना।

      3. आवश्यक गोपनीयता बनाए रखते हुए जानकारी को सक्षम खरीद/पर्यवेक्षण प्राधिकारी के समक्ष प्रस्तुत करना तथा समुचित सत्यापन का अनुरोध करना।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए। 

      (a) केवल 1 

      (b)  केवल 2 और 3 

      (c) केवल 3 

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Polity And Governance c

      Medium
      Applied(A)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:49 Sec.
      • The most appropriate course of action for Mr. X is to place the information before the competent procurement or oversight authority and seek appropriate verification while maintaining necessary confidentiality.
      • This question examines the ethical responsibilities of a public servant when faced with a conflict between administrative efficiency, public interest, procedural fairness, accountability and integrity.
      • Understanding the ethical dilemma
        • Mr. X faces two competing responsibilities:
          • Ensuring timely procurement: Government hospitals require medical equipment, and unnecessary delays may adversely affect healthcare delivery.
          • Ensuring integrity in procurement: The possibility that a bidder previously supplied substandard equipment raises concerns about patient safety, public expenditure and procurement quality.
          • The central difficulty is that the allegations have not yet been conclusively established.
          • Therefore, Mr. X should neither ignore the report nor treat the allegations as proven misconduct.
          • The appropriate response must balance timely procurement with a fair, evidence-based and accountable decision-making process.
      • Statement 1 is incorrect: Making the unverified allegations public immediately so that citizens can decide whether the contractor should be selected. 
        • This approach is inappropriate because the allegations remain under inquiry.
        •  Public disclosure at this stage could damage the bidder's reputation without sufficient evidence, compromise the inquiry and breach applicable confidentiality requirements.
        • Although transparency is an important principle of public administration, transparency does not mean indiscriminate disclosure of unverified or confidential information. Furthermore, the responsibility for evaluating bids rests with the legally authorised procurement authorities, not with public opinion.
        • Ethical principle violated: Procedural fairness, confidentiality and responsible exercise of official authority.
      • Statement 2 is incorrect: Recommending rejection of the bidder solely on the basis of the allegations, even before their verification.
        • This option is also inappropriate because it assumes that an allegation is sufficient proof of wrongdoing.
        • Rejecting the bidder solely on this basis, without applying the prescribed eligibility criteria and verification procedures, could result in arbitrary decision-making and unfair treatment.
        • The bidder is entitled to procedural fairness, subject to applicable procurement rules. An adverse decision should be supported by legally relevant evidence and proper authority.
        • However, this does not mean that Mr. X must ignore credible risk information. If procurement rules permit provisional safeguards, enhanced technical scrutiny or temporary withholding of an award pending urgent verification, those measures may be appropriate.
        • Ethical principle violated: Objectivity, impartiality, fairness and evidence-based decision-making.
      • Statement 3 is correct: Place the information before the competent procurement/oversight authority and seek appropriate verification while maintaining necessary confidentiality.
        • This is the most appropriate course because it combines accountability with procedural fairness.
        • Mr. X should formally bring the report to the attention of the competent authority, ensure that the concerns are properly recorded, and seek timely verification in accordance with procurement rules.
        • Necessary confidentiality protects the integrity of the inquiry and the legitimate interests of all parties.
        • At the same time, Mr. X should recommend time-bound scrutiny so that the procurement process is not unnecessarily delayed.
        • Ethical principles upheld: Integrity, accountability, due diligence, impartiality, public interest and procedural fairness.
      • Why the superior's instruction should not be followed blindly
        • The superior's concern about hospital services is legitimate. However, an instruction to ignore a potentially significant quality concern does not eliminate Mr. X's responsibility to exercise due diligence.
        • A public servant is expected to respect the administrative hierarchy while also ensuring that official decisions comply with applicable rules and safeguard the public interest.
        • Mr. X should therefore record the concern through proper channels, seek timely directions from the competent authority and, where necessary, escalate the matter in accordance with established procedures.
        • He should not independently impose a penalty or publicly disclose the allegations merely because he disagrees with his superior.
      70

      Mr. X publishes a post on a public platform severely criticising a government policy. The State directs the removal of the post on the ground that it:

      1. contains statements allegedly defamatory of certain individuals;

      2. uses material considered contrary to decency or morality; and

      3. contains words allegedly amounting to incitement to an offence.

      Mr. X argues that the direction necessarily violates his freedom of speech and expression under Article 19(1)(a).

      Which one of the following is the most appropriate constitutional position?

      (a) The direction is necessarily unconstitutional because political speech cannot be restricted by the State.

      (b) Only defamation can constitute a ground for reasonable restriction on freedom of speech and expression.

      (c) Decency or morality and incitement to an offence are permissible grounds, but defamation is not.

      (d) All three grounds may form the basis of reasonable restrictions on freedom of speech and expression, subject to the requirements of Article 19(2).

      श्री X किसी सार्वजनिक मंच पर एक सरकारी नीति की कड़ी आलोचना करते हुए एक पोस्ट प्रकाशित करते हैं। राज्य इस आधार पर पोस्ट हटाने का निर्देश देता है कि:

      1. उसमें कुछ व्यक्तियों के संबंध में कथित रूप से मानहानिकारक कथन हैं;

      2. उसमें ऐसी सामग्री का प्रयोग किया गया है, जिसे शिष्टाचार या सदाचार के प्रतिकूल माना गया है; तथा

      3. उसमें ऐसे शब्द हैं, जो कथित रूप से किसी अपराध के उद्दीपन के समान हैं।

      श्री X का तर्क है कि यह निर्देश अनुच्छेद 19(1)(a) के अंतर्गत उनकी वाक् और अभिव्यक्ति की स्वतंत्रता का अनिवार्यतः उल्लंघन करता है।

      निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सी सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त संवैधानिक स्थिति है?

      (a) यह निर्देश अनिवार्यतः असंवैधानिक है, क्योंकि राज्य राजनीतिक अभिव्यक्ति पर प्रतिबंध नहीं लगा सकता है।

      (b) केवल मानहानि ही वाक् और अभिव्यक्ति की स्वतंत्रता पर युक्तियुक्त निर्बंधन लगाने का आधार हो सकती है।

      (c) शिष्टाचार या सदाचार तथा किसी अपराध का उद्दीपन अनुमन्य आधार हैं, किंतु मानहानि नहीं।

      (d) अनुच्छेद 19(2) की अपेक्षाओं के अधीन, तीनों आधार वाक् और अभिव्यक्ति की स्वतंत्रता पर युक्तियुक्त निर्बंधन लगाने का आधार हो सकते हैं।


      		 Polity And Governance d

      Difficult
      Applied(A)
      Detailed Analysis       		d
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:83 Sec.
      • Option (a) is incorrect: The direction is necessarily unconstitutional because political speech cannot be restricted by the State. Political speech, including criticism of government policies, enjoys strong constitutional protection. However, it is not completely immune from reasonable restrictions.

        • For example, a post criticising a government policy may also contain a legally defamatory allegation against an individual or material amounting to incitement to an offence.

        • Such content may be restricted if the legal requirements under Article 19(2) are satisfied.

        • The expression “necessarily unconstitutional” makes an absolute claim that Article 19 does not support. Political speech is protected, but not unconditionally.

        • The Constitution also expressly recognises decency or morality, incitement to an offence, public order, contempt of court and other specified grounds.
      • Option (b) is incorrect: Only defamation can constitute a ground for reasonable restriction on freedom of speech and expression. Defamation is indeed a permissible ground under Article 19(2). However, it is not the only permissible ground.

        • The word “only” wrongly excludes other constitutionally recognised grounds.

        • The constitutional protection of reputation must be balanced with freedom of expression, and not every criticism or negative statement automatically constitutes defamation.
      • Option (c) is incorrect: Decency or morality and incitement to an offence are permissible grounds, but defamation is not. The first part is correct because both decency or morality and incitement to an offence are included under Article 19(2).
        •  However, the second part is incorrect because defamation is also expressly recognised as a ground for reasonable restrictions. It excludes an expressly enumerated constitutional ground.
      • Option (d) is correct: All three grounds may form the basis of reasonable restrictions on freedom of speech and expression, subject to the requirements of Article 19(2). This accurately reflects the constitutional position.
        • Defamation, decency or morality, and incitement to an offence are all permissible grounds. However, the State cannot merely label expression as defamatory, indecent or inciting and automatically justify its removal.
        • The restriction must be supported by law and satisfy constitutional standards of reasonableness.
        • Thus, the option recognises both the permissible grounds and the constitutional limitations on the State's power to restrict speech.

      • Applying Article 19(2) to Mr. X's post: The three allegations require separate legal examination.

        • First, defamation: The State must establish that the allegedly defamatory content meets the applicable legal requirements. Mere criticism, disagreement or embarrassment caused to public officials does not automatically amount to defamation.

        • Second, decency or morality: The State must identify the legally objectionable material and establish that its restriction is justified. Mere offensiveness, unpopular opinions or harsh language do not by themselves establish a constitutional ground for removal.

        • Third, incitement to an offence: The State must demonstrate more than mere advocacy or criticism. The relevant expression must satisfy the applicable legal test for incitement.

        • The Supreme Court's decision in Shreya Singhal v. Union of India (2015) is particularly relevant. The Court distinguished between discussion, advocacy and incitement, emphasising that mere discussion or advocacy, however unpopular, cannot be restricted simply because the State disapproves of it.

        • Consequently, the removal direction is neither automatically valid nor automatically unconstitutional. Its legality depends on the content, applicable law, procedure and constitutional justification.

      71

      Consider a freshwater lake receiving excessive agricultural runoff rich in nitrogen and phosphorus.

      If the nutrient inflow in a lake were suddenly reduced substantially, which of the following outcomes would be most scientifically reasonable?

      (a) The reduction in external nutrient loading would immediately suppress algal growth, leading to a corresponding improvement in dissolved oxygen levels, irrespective of sediment conditions.

      (b) The lake may continue to exhibit eutrophic conditions because nutrients accumulated in sediments can be released into the water column, sustaining primary productivity.

      (c) The reduction in nutrient inflow would initially accelerate eutrophication because nutrient limitation promotes the growth of phytoplankton adapted to nutrient-poor conditions.

      (d) The lake may undergo a gradual transition towards oligotrophic conditions as reduced nutrient availability necessarily eliminates internal nutrient recycling.

       मीठे जल की एक झील में नाइट्रोजन और फॉस्फोरस से समृद्ध कृषि अपवाह अत्यधिक मात्रा में पहुँच रहा है। यदि इस झील में पोषक तत्त्वों के अंतर्वाह को अचानक काफी कम कर दिया जाए, तो निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा परिणाम वैज्ञानिक दृष्टि से सर्वाधिक युक्तिसंगत होगा?

      (a) पोषक तत्त्वों के बाह्य भार में कमी से शैवालों की वृद्धि तुरंत दब जाएगी और अवसादों की स्थिति की परवाह किए बिना घुलित ऑक्सीजन के स्तर में तदनुरूप सुधार होगा।

      (b) झील में सुपोषी दशाएँ बनी रह सकती हैं, क्योंकि अवसादों में संचित पोषक तत्त्व जल स्तंभ में मुक्त होकर प्राथमिक उत्पादकता बनाए रख सकते हैं।

      (c) पोषक तत्त्वों के अंतर्वाह में कमी से आरंभ में सुपोषण तीव्र होगा, क्योंकि पोषक तत्त्वों की सीमित उपलब्धता अल्प-पोषक दशाओं के प्रति अनुकूलित पादपप्लवकों की वृद्धि को बढ़ावा देती है। 

      (d) झील धीरे-धीरे अल्पपोषी दशाओं की ओर संक्रमण कर सकती है, क्योंकि पोषक तत्त्वों की कम उपलब्धता आंतरिक पोषक तत्त्व पुनर्चक्रण को अनिवार्यतः समाप्त कर देती है।


      		 Environment b

      Easy
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:202 Sec.
      • Option (a) is incorrect. Although reducing external nutrient inputs can improve water quality over time, recovery is not necessarily proportional to the reduction in nutrient loading. Internal nutrient recycling, sediment oxygen demand and other ecological processes may delay the restoration of dissolved oxygen levels.
      • Option (b) is correct. Eutrophic lakes may exhibit internal nutrient loading, particularly through the release of phosphorus accumulated in bottom sediments. Under anoxic conditions, phosphorus bound to iron compounds may be released into the overlying water, sustaining algal growth even after external nutrient inputs have been substantially reduced.
      • Option (c) is incorrect. Nutrient limitation generally constrains primary productivity rather than accelerating eutrophication. Although nutrient reduction may alter phytoplankton community composition, it does not inherently promote eutrophication.
      • Option (d) is incorrect. Reduced external nutrient loading does not necessarily eliminate internal nutrient recycling. Moreover, the transition from a eutrophic to an oligotrophic state is neither automatic nor guaranteed, particularly in lakes with substantial historical nutrient accumulation.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.

      Consider the following statements regarding invasive alien species:

      1.Every species introduced outside its native geographical range becomes invasive.

      2.An invasive alien species may alter nutrient cycling, fire regimes and the structure of native communities.

      3.The ecological impact of an introduced species can depend on the characteristics of the receiving ecosystem.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 1 and 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      आक्रामक विदेशी प्रजातियों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. अपने मूल भौगोलिक विस्तार से बाहर लाई गई प्रत्येक प्रजाति आक्रामक बन जाती है।

      2. कोई आक्रामक विदेशी प्रजाति पोषक तत्त्वों के चक्रण, अग्नि की आवृत्ति एवं प्रकृति तथा देशज समुदायों की संरचना में परिवर्तन कर सकती है।

      3. किसी क्षेत्र में लाई गई प्रजाति का पारिस्थितिक प्रभाव उस पारितंत्र की विशेषताओं पर निर्भर कर सकता है, जिसमें उसे लाया गया है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

      (a)  केवल 1 और 2

      (b)  केवल 2 और 3

      (c) केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3 


      		 Environment b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:36 Sec.
      • Invasive Alien Species (IAS) are species introduced intentionally or unintentionally outside their natural geographical range, whose establishment and spread threaten native biodiversity, ecosystem functioning and associated ecosystem services. These species may outcompete native organisms, alter ecological interactions, disrupt ecosystem processes and cause significant environmental and economic damage. According to the IPBES Global Assessment Report on Invasive Alien Species and their Control (2023), invasive alien species are among the five major direct drivers of global biodiversity loss.
      • Statement 1 is not correct: Not every species introduced outside its native geographical range becomes invasive. An introduced or alien species is one that occurs outside its natural distribution due to human intervention, whether deliberate or accidental. However, only some introduced species establish self-sustaining populations, spread rapidly and cause ecological or other adverse impacts. For instance, several introduced ornamental and agricultural plant species may persist without becoming invasive. Thus, the terms alien species and invasive alien species are not interchangeable.
      • Statement 2 is correct: Invasive alien species can significantly modify ecosystem structure, composition and functioning. Certain invasive plants alter nutrient cycling by changing the rate of litter decomposition and nutrient availability in soils. Some invasive grasses increase the accumulation and continuity of flammable biomass, thereby modifying natural fire regimes and increasing the frequency or intensity of wildfires. For example, Lantana camara, an invasive shrub widely distributed in India, can suppress native vegetation, affect forest regeneration and modify habitat conditions. Similarly, Prosopis juliflora can form dense thickets and alter native plant communities in dryland ecosystems.
      • Statement 3 is correct: The ecological impacts of introduced species depend not only on their biological characteristics but also on the environmental conditions and ecological attributes of the receiving ecosystem. Factors such as climate suitability, resource availability, disturbance regimes, the presence of natural enemies and the resistance of native communities influence whether an introduced species becomes established and spreads. For instance, disturbed habitats with reduced native vegetation cover may be more vulnerable to invasion, while ecosystems with strong biotic resistance may restrict the establishment of certain introduced species. Consequently, the same alien species may exhibit different levels of invasiveness and ecological impact across different ecosystems.
      • The management of invasive alien species involves prevention, early detection, rapid response, monitoring and appropriate control or eradication measures. Internationally, the Kunming–Montreal Global Biodiversity Framework, adopted under the Convention on Biological Diversity (CBD) in 2022, includes Target 6, which seeks to reduce the introduction and establishment of priority invasive alien species and minimise their impacts on biodiversity and ecosystem services.
      • Hence, statements 2 and 3 are correct.

      Which one of the following Indian States has recently initiated India's first State-level Red List Assessment of Ecosystems, aimed at evaluating the risk of ecosystem collapse and strengthening biodiversity conservation?

      (a) Karnataka

      (b) Kerala

      (c) Tamil Nadu

      (d) Maharashtra

      निम्नलिखित भारतीय राज्यों में से किसने हाल ही में पारितंत्रों के पतन के जोखिम का मूल्यांकन करने तथा जैव विविधता संरक्षण को सुदृढ़ करने के उद्देश्य से भारत का पहला राज्य-स्तरीय ‘पारितंत्रों का लाल सूची आकलन’ आरंभ किया है?

      (a) कर्नाटक

      (b) केरल

      (c) तमिलनाडु

      (d)  महाराष्ट्र


      		 Environment b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:35 Sec.
      • Kerala has become the first Indian State to initiate a State-level Red List Assessment of Ecosystems (RLE), marking an important step towards ecosystem-based biodiversity conservation. The initiative, undertaken by the Kerala State Biodiversity Board (KSBB), aims to scientifically assess the ecological condition, degradation and risk of collapse of different ecosystems across the State. The assessment will support evidence-based conservation planning, ecological restoration and climate resilience.
      • The assessment follows the framework developed by the International Union for Conservation of Nature (IUCN) under its Red List of Ecosystems. Unlike the IUCN Red List of Threatened Species, which evaluates the extinction risk of individual species, the Red List of Ecosystems assesses the risk of collapse of entire ecosystems. It considers factors such as reduction in ecosystem distribution, restricted geographic extent, environmental degradation and disruption of ecological processes. Ecosystems are classified into risk categories such as Critically Endangered, Endangered, Vulnerable and Least Concern, among others.
      • Kerala's assessment encompasses ecologically significant habitats, including Myristica swamps, shola-grassland ecosystems of the Western Ghats, mangrove forests, wetlands and riverine ecosystems. These ecosystems face multiple threats, including habitat fragmentation, invasive alien species, land-use changes, pollution and climate change. The assessment seeks to identify vulnerable ecosystems and establish scientific baselines for prioritising conservation and restoration interventions.
      • The initiative is also significant in the context of India's commitments under the Convention on Biological Diversity (CBD) and the Kunming–Montreal Global Biodiversity Framework (2022). By integrating ecosystem-level risk assessments into biodiversity planning, it can strengthen habitat protection, ecological restoration and sustainable land-use management. The initiative also highlights the importance of shifting from exclusively species-focused conservation approaches towards comprehensive ecosystem-based conservation strategies.
      • Hence, option (B) is the correct answer.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      सतत कृषि पद्धतियों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. संरक्षण कृषि में सामान्यतः मृदा में न्यूनतम विक्षोभ, स्थायी मृदा आवरण तथा फसल विविधीकरण पर बल दिया जाता है।

      3. खेत में फसल अवशेष बनाए रखने से मृदा पर वर्षा का प्रहार बढ़ जाता है, जिसके कारण मृदा अपरदन अनिवार्यतः बढ़ता है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (c) केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Environment a

      Easy
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:35 Sec.
      • Sustainable agricultural practices refer to farming methods that aim to maintain agricultural productivity while conserving natural resources, improving soil health, enhancing water-use efficiency and minimising environmental degradation. Practices such as conservation agriculture, direct seeding of rice, crop diversification and crop residue management play an important role in promoting resource-efficient and climate-resilient agriculture. These approaches also contribute to reducing soil erosion, improving nutrient cycling and enhancing the long-term sustainability of agricultural systems.
      • Statement 1 is correct: Conservation agriculture is based on three fundamental principles: minimum mechanical soil disturbance, permanent organic soil cover and diversification of crop species through crop rotation or intercropping. Minimum tillage helps preserve soil structure and soil organisms, while permanent soil cover, maintained through crop residues or cover crops, reduces evaporation, suppresses weeds and protects the soil against erosion. Crop diversification further contributes to maintaining soil fertility, improving nutrient cycling and reducing the incidence of pests and diseases.
      • Statement 2 is correct: Direct Seeding of Rice (DSR) is a cultivation technique in which rice seeds are sown directly into the field rather than raising seedlings in a nursery and subsequently transplanting them into puddled fields. Under suitable soil, climatic and management conditions, DSR can reduce irrigation water requirements by avoiding puddling and prolonged field flooding. It may also reduce labour requirements, cultivation costs and methane emissions associated with continuously flooded rice cultivation. However, its effectiveness depends on appropriate weed management, soil characteristics, rainfall patterns and irrigation practices.
      • Statement 3 is not correct: Crop residue retention generally helps reduce soil erosion rather than increase it. When crop residues are retained on the soil surface, they act as a protective mulch, reducing the direct impact of raindrops, slowing surface runoff and limiting the detachment and transportation of soil particles. Residue retention also promotes moisture conservation, improves soil organic matter over time and supports beneficial soil microbial activity. In contrast, removing or burning crop residues can leave the soil exposed to rainfall and wind, thereby increasing its vulnerability to erosion and degradation.
      • Sustainable agricultural practices are particularly important for India in the context of groundwater depletion, declining soil fertility, agricultural greenhouse gas emissions and increasing climate variability. The adoption of conservation agriculture, improved water management and residue-based farming practices can contribute to resource conservation, climate adaptation and long-term agricultural productivity.
      • Hence, option (a) is the correct answer.

      Which one of the following best distinguishes a hydrogen fuel-cell electric vehicle (FCEV) from a battery electric vehicle (BEV)?

      (a) An FCEV uses hydrogen in an internal combustion engine, whereas a BEV uses an electric motor.

      (b) An FCEV generates electricity onboard through an electrochemical reaction, whereas a BEV draws electricity primarily from energy stored in a rechargeable battery.

      (c) An FCEV does not require an electric motor, whereas a BEV necessarily requires one.

      (d) An FCEV emits carbon dioxide during normal fuel-cell operation, whereas a BEV emits only water vapour.

       निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक हाइड्रोजन ईंधन-सेल विद्युत वाहन (FCEV) और बैटरी विद्युत वाहन (BEV) के बीच सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त अंतर स्पष्ट करता है?

      (a) FCEV में अंतर्दहन इंजन में हाइड्रोजन का उपयोग होता है, जबकि BEV में विद्युत मोटर का उपयोग होता है।

      (b) FCEV में विद्युत-रासायनिक अभिक्रिया द्वारा वाहन के भीतर ही विद्युत उत्पन्न होती है, जबकि BEV मुख्यतः पुनः आवेशित की जा सकने वाली बैटरी में संचित ऊर्जा से विद्युत प्राप्त करता है।

      (c) FCEV में विद्युत मोटर की आवश्यकता नहीं होती है, जबकि BEV में उसकी आवश्यकता अनिवार्यतः होती है।

      (d) FCEV सामान्य ईंधन-सेल परिचालन के दौरान कार्बन डाइऑक्साइड उत्सर्जित करता है, जबकि BEV केवल जलवाष्प उत्सर्जित करता है।


      		 Environment b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:7 Sec.
      • Hydrogen fuel-cell electric vehicles (FCEVs) generate electricity onboard through an electrochemical reaction between hydrogen and oxygen in a fuel-cell stack. The electricity produced powers an electric motor, often supported by a relatively small battery that assists with acceleration and regenerative braking. In a hydrogen fuel cell, water and heat are principal products of the electrochemical reaction.
      • Battery electric vehicles (BEVs) primarily store electrical energy in rechargeable batteries. This stored energy is supplied to an electric motor, which converts electrical energy into mechanical energy for propulsion. BEVs are usually charged using electricity from an external supply, whereas hydrogen FCEVs are refuelled with hydrogen.
      • Both technologies employ electric motors for propulsion, but they differ primarily in how energy is stored and supplied. FCEVs are not necessarily hydrogen internal-combustion vehicles, and normal fuel-cell operation does not involve direct carbon dioxide generation from the hydrogen-oxygen reaction. Therefore, the distinction in Option (B) correctly identifies the operating principles of the two technologies.
      • Hence, Option (B) is the correct answer.

      Consider the following statements:

      Assertion (A): Green hydrogen produced through water electrolysis can have a low life-cycle greenhouse-gas footprint.

      Reason (R): Electrolysis intrinsically produces no carbon dioxide at the point where water is split, and its overall emissions depend significantly on the electricity supply and equipment life cycle.

      Select the correct answer using the code given below.

      (a) Both A and R are true, and R correctly explains A.

      (b) Both A and R are true, but R does not explain A.

      (c) A is true, but R is false.

      (d) A is false, but R is true.

      निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      अभिकथन (A): जल के विद्युत अपघटन से उत्पादित हरित हाइड्रोजन का जीवन-चक्र ग्रीनहाउस गैस पदचिह्न कम हो सकता है।

      कारण (R): विद्युत अपघटन की प्रक्रिया में जहाँ जल का विघटन होता है, वहाँ स्वभावतः कार्बन डाइऑक्साइड उत्पन्न नहीं होती है तथा इसका समग्र उत्सर्जन विद्युत आपूर्ति और उपकरणों के जीवन-चक्र पर काफी हद तक निर्भर करता है।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या है।

      (b) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या नहीं है। 

      (c) A सही है, किंतु R गलत है। 

      (d) A गलत है, किंतु R सही है।


      		 Environment a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:17 Sec.
      • Green hydrogen is generally produced through water electrolysis using electricity obtained from renewable energy sources such as solar and wind power. Electrolysis splits water into hydrogen and oxygen, allowing hydrogen to be used subsequently as an energy carrier or industrial feedstock. When renewable electricity and low-emission supply chains are used, green hydrogen can have a relatively low life-cycle greenhouse-gas footprint. Hence, the Assertion is correct.
      • The electrochemical splitting of water does not directly release carbon dioxide because water contains hydrogen and oxygen rather than carbon. However, the overall environmental footprint depends on the electricity used, manufacturing of equipment, water treatment, hydrogen compression, storage and transport. Hydrogen produced using fossil-fuel-based electricity may therefore have substantial indirect emissions. Hence, the Reason is correct.
      • Hydrogen production pathways are commonly differentiated according to their production processes and associated emissions. For example, grey hydrogen is generally produced from fossil fuels without carbon capture, while green hydrogen relies on renewable-powered electrolysis. The absence of direct carbon emissions during electrolysis and the dependence of life-cycle emissions on the energy supply explain the potential climate benefits of green hydrogen. The Reason correctly explains the Assertion.
      • Hence, Option (A) is the correct answer.

      • The Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC) was established in 1988 by the World Meteorological Organization (WMO) and the United Nations Environment Programme (UNEP). Its primary purpose is to assess scientific knowledge relating to climate change, its impacts, risks and potential response options. Hence, Statement 1 is correct.
      • The IPCC does not primarily conduct original field experiments or independent climate research. Instead, it assesses existing scientific, technical and socio-economic literature produced by researchers and institutions worldwide. Its reports undergo extensive expert and government review processes. Therefore, describing original field experimentation as its principal function is incorrect. Hence, Statement 2 is incorrect.
      • The IPCC has three Working Groups. Working Group I examines the physical science basis of climate change; Working Group II assesses impacts, adaptation and vulnerability; and Working Group III evaluates options for mitigating climate change. The IPCC also has a Task Force on National Greenhouse Gas Inventories, which develops methodologies for estimating and reporting emissions and removals. Hence, Statement 3 is correct.
      • Hence, Option (C) is the correct answer.

      Which of the following can act as carbon sinks under suitable ecological conditions?

      1. Mangrove forests

      2. Peatlands

      3. Seagrass meadows

      4. Degraded agricultural soils under all conditions

      Select the correct answer using the code given below.

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 1, 2 and 3 only

      (c) 2, 3 and 4 only

      (d) 1, 2, 3 and 4

      उपयुक्त पारिस्थितिक परिस्थितियों में निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से कार्बन सिंक के रूप में कार्य कर सकते हैं?

      1. मैंग्रोव वन

      2. पीटभूमियाँ

      3. समुद्री घास के मैदान

      4. सभी परिस्थितियों में निम्नीकृत कृषि मृदाएँ

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (c) केवल 2, 3 और 4

      (d) 1, 2, 3 और 4


      		 Environment b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:12 Sec.
      • A carbon sink is a system that absorbs more carbon from the atmosphere than it releases over a specified period. Mangrove forests can act as important carbon sinks because they store carbon in vegetation and especially in waterlogged, carbon-rich sediments. Their capacity to accumulate and preserve organic carbon makes them important components of coastal blue-carbon ecosystems. Hence, Item 1 is correct.
      • Peatlands are ecosystems characterized by the accumulation of partially decomposed organic matter under predominantly waterlogged conditions. Oxygen limitation slows decomposition and allows substantial carbon storage over long periods. Seagrass meadows similarly capture atmospheric or dissolved inorganic carbon through photosynthesis and facilitate carbon accumulation in coastal sediments. Hence, Items 2 and 3 are correct.
      • Degraded agricultural soils do not necessarily function as carbon sinks. Soil erosion, excessive tillage, loss of organic matter and unsuitable land-management practices can cause soils to release more carbon than they absorb. Such soils may become carbon sinks under restoration and improved management, but this is not guaranteed under all conditions. Hence, Item 4 is incorrect.
      • Hence, Option (B) is the correct answer.

      A satellite is placed in a low Earth orbit rather than a geostationary orbit.

      Which of the following can reasonably be inferred?

      1. The satellite generally completes an orbit in a shorter period than a geostationary satellite.

      2. The satellite necessarily remains fixed over a particular location on Earth's surface.

      3. Maintaining continuous communication coverage of a region may require multiple satellites.

      Select the correct answer using the code given below.

      (a) 1 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 1 and 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      किसी उपग्रह को भूस्थिर कक्षा के बजाय निम्न भू-कक्षा में स्थापित किया जाता है। निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष युक्तिसंगत रूप से निकाले जा सकते हैं?

      1. यह उपग्रह सामान्यतः किसी भूस्थिर उपग्रह की तुलना में कम समय में एक परिक्रमा पूरी करता है।

      2. यह उपग्रह पृथ्वी की सतह पर किसी विशेष स्थान के ऊपर अनिवार्यतः स्थिर बना रहता है।

      3. किसी क्षेत्र में निरंतर संचार कवरेज बनाए रखने के लिए अनेक उपग्रहों की आवश्यकता हो सकती है।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a)  केवल 1

      (b) केवल 2 और 3

      (c) केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Science And Technology c

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:65 Sec.
      • Low Earth orbit (LEO) generally refers to Earth orbits at altitudes below approximately 2,000 kilometres. Satellites in these orbits travel around the Earth much more rapidly than geostationary satellites, which orbit at approximately 35,786 kilometres above the equator and have an orbital period matching Earth's rotation. Hence, Statement 1 is correct.
      • A satellite in an ordinary low Earth orbit does not remain fixed above a single geographical location. It moves relative to the Earth's surface and passes over different areas during successive revolutions. Remaining stationary relative to an equatorial ground location is a characteristic of a geostationary satellite, not a LEO satellite. Hence, Statement 2 is incorrect.
      • Because an individual LEO satellite has a limited instantaneous coverage area and moves rapidly across the sky, continuous regional or global communication services often require constellations containing multiple satellites. Such constellations can provide successive coverage as different satellites pass over a region. Hence, Statement 3 is correct.
      • Hence, Option (C) is the correct answer.

      Consider the following statements regarding gene-editing technologies:

      1. CRISPR-Cas9 can introduce targeted double-stranded breaks in DNA.

      2. Base editing necessarily requires a double-stranded DNA break.

      3. Prime editing can introduce certain targeted DNA changes without requiring a donor DNA template containing the desired edit.

      4. Somatic gene edits are ordinarily inherited by offspring.

      How many of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) One only

      (b) Only two

      (c) Three only

      (d) All four

      जीन-संपादन प्रौद्योगिकियों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. CRISPR-Cas9, DNA के दोनों रज्जुकों में लक्षित विच्छेद उत्पन्न कर सकता है।

      2. क्षारक संपादन के लिए DNA के दोनों रज्जुकों में विच्छेद अनिवार्यतः आवश्यक होता है।

      3. प्राइम संपादन, वांछित संपादन वाला दाता DNA साँचा आवश्यक हुए बिना, DNA में कुछ लक्षित परिवर्तन कर सकता है।

      4. दैहिक कोशिकाओं में किए गए जीन-संपादन सामान्यतः संतति को वंशानुगत रूप से प्राप्त होते हैं।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कितने सही हैं?

      (a) केवल एक

      (b) केवल दो

      (c) केवल तीन

      (d) सभी चारों 


      		 Science And Technology b

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:28 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct: CRISPR-Cas9 is a genome-editing technology derived from the natural defence mechanism of bacteria against invading viruses. It uses a guide RNA (gRNA) to identify a specific DNA sequence and the Cas9 enzyme to introduce a targeted double-stranded break in the DNA. Following the break, the cell activates its natural DNA repair mechanisms, primarily Non-Homologous End Joining (NHEJ) and Homology-Directed Repair (HDR). NHEJ may introduce small insertions or deletions that disrupt gene function, whereas HDR can facilitate precise genetic modifications using a suitable DNA repair template. Thus, conventional CRISPR-Cas9 enables targeted modifications in the genome by cutting DNA at specific locations.
      • Statement 2 is not correct: Base editing is an advanced gene-editing technique that enables precise changes to individual DNA bases without necessarily introducing double-stranded DNA breaks. It generally employs a modified Cas enzyme combined with a deaminase enzyme to chemically convert one DNA base into another. Two major types include Cytosine Base Editors (CBEs), which typically convert C–G base pairs into T–A base pairs, and Adenine Base Editors (ABEs), which typically convert A–T base pairs into G–C base pairs. Although some base-editing systems introduce a single-strand nick to improve editing efficiency, they do not require a double-stranded break. Therefore, the assertion that base editing necessarily requires such a break is incorrect.
      • Statement 3 is correct: Prime editing is a sophisticated genome-editing technique that enables targeted DNA substitutions, small insertions and deletions without generally requiring double-stranded DNA breaks or a separate donor DNA template. It employs a Cas9 nickase enzyme fused with a reverse transcriptase and a specialised prime-editing guide RNA (pegRNA). The pegRNA identifies the target DNA sequence and carries an RNA template encoding the desired genetic modification. The reverse transcriptase uses this RNA template to synthesise the edited DNA sequence. Unlike conventional HDR-based editing, which may require an externally supplied donor DNA template, prime editing can directly introduce specified genetic changes using the instructions contained within the pegRNA. Therefore, the statement is correct.
      • Statement 4 is not correct: Somatic gene editing involves modifying the genetic material of non-reproductive body cells, such as blood, liver or muscle cells. Since these modifications generally do not affect sperm or egg cells, they are ordinarily confined to the treated individual and are not transmitted to offspring. In contrast, germline gene editing involves genetic modifications in reproductive cells or early embryos that may become incorporated into the germline and potentially be inherited by subsequent generations. An important application of somatic gene editing is the treatment of genetic disorders such as sickle cell disease. Therefore, the assertion that somatic gene edits are ordinarily inherited by offspring is incorrect.

      :2 Marks

      • Statement 1 is correct: The BioE3 Policy stands for Biotechnology for Economy, Environment and Employment. It was approved by the Union Cabinet on 24 August 2024, under the Department of Biotechnology (DBT), Ministry of Science and Technology, to foster high-performance biomanufacturing in India. The policy aims to accelerate economic growth through biotechnology-driven industries, promote environmental sustainability through green and circular bioeconomy models, and generate skilled employment opportunities. It encourages the development of high-value bio-based products using advanced technologies such as synthetic biology, genome editing and metabolic engineering. The policy also aligns with India's Net Zero ambitions and the Lifestyle for Environment (LiFE) initiative. 
      • Statement 2 is correct: The BioE3 Policy promotes the establishment of Biofoundries, Biomanufacturing Hubs and Bio-Artificial Intelligence (Bio-AI) Hubs to strengthen India's biotechnology innovation ecosystem. Biofoundries are specialised facilities that integrate automation, synthetic biology and high-throughput experimentation for designing, constructing and testing biological systems. Biomanufacturing Hubs facilitate the scaling up of laboratory innovations into pilot-scale and commercially viable bio-based products. Bio-AI Hubs employ artificial intelligence, machine learning and data-driven approaches to accelerate biotechnology research and product development. These facilities are intended to support research institutions, start-ups, small and medium enterprises and industries through shared infrastructure and public-private partnerships.
      • Statement 3 is not correct: Futuristic marine and space research is explicitly included among the six thematic priority sectors identified under the BioE3 Policy. Marine biotechnology involves exploring marine organisms, microorganisms and biological resources for applications such as pharmaceuticals, industrial enzymes, bioactive compounds and other high-value products. Space biotechnology explores biological processes and systems relevant to space environments, including microbial behaviour, biological production systems and life-support applications. By including marine and space research, the policy seeks to encourage emerging applications of biotechnology beyond conventional agriculture, healthcare and industrial production. Therefore, the assertion that marine and space research is outside the scope of the policy is incorrect
      • Statement 4 is correct: Precision biotherapeutics and climate-resilient agriculture are two important thematic sectors covered under the BioE3 Policy. Precision biotherapeutics involves developing advanced biological treatments, including monoclonal antibodies, mRNA-based therapeutics, and cell and gene therapies, for more targeted and effective disease management. Climate-resilient agriculture focuses on biotechnology-based innovations that can improve agricultural productivity and resilience against environmental stresses such as drought, salinity, extreme temperatures and emerging pests and diseases. These interventions may include the development of climate-resilient crop varieties, microbial biofertilisers, biopesticides and other agricultural biological products. The policy thereby seeks to strengthen healthcare innovation, agricultural sustainability and food security.
