      With reference to semiconductor technology, which of the following statements is/are correct?

      1. Gallium nitride and silicon carbide are examples of wide-bandgap semiconductors.

      2. Wide-bandgap semiconductors may offer advantages in high-voltage and high-temperature applications.

      3. Silicon is a compound semiconductor containing two different elements.

      Select the correct answer using the code given below.

      (a) 1 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 1 and 2 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      अर्धचालक प्रौद्योगिकी के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

      1. गैलियम नाइट्राइड और सिलिकॉन कार्बाइड चौड़े बैंड-अंतराल वाले अर्धचालकों के उदाहरण हैं।

      2. चौड़े बैंड-अंतराल वाले अर्धचालक उच्च वोल्टता और उच्च तापमान वाले अनुप्रयोगों में लाभ प्रदान कर सकते हैं।

      3. सिलिकॉन दो भिन्न तत्त्वों से युक्त एक यौगिक अर्धचालक है।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a)  केवल 1

      (b) केवल 2 और 3 

      (c) केवल 1 और 2 

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Science And Technology c

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		c
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:34 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct: Gallium nitride (GaN) and silicon carbide (SiC) are examples of wide-bandgap semiconductors, which possess a larger  energy difference between their valence band and conduction band compared to conventional silicon. The bandgap represents the minimum energy required to excite an electron from the valence band to the conduction band, enabling electrical conduction. Silicon has a bandgap of approximately 1.1 electron volts (eV), whereas silicon carbide (4H-SiC) has a bandgap of approximately 3.3 eV and gallium nitride has a bandgap of approximately 3.4 eV. These materials exhibit properties such as high breakdown electric fields and improved performance under demanding operating conditions. Consequently, GaN and SiC are increasingly used in advanced power electronics, electric vehicles, renewable energy systems and high-frequency electronic devices.
      • Statement 2 is correct: Wide-bandgap semiconductors offer significant advantages in high-voltage, high-temperature and high-power applications because their larger bandgaps and high critical electric fields enable devices to withstand stronger electric fields before electrical breakdown. Their characteristics can also help reduce power losses and improve energy efficiency in appropriately designed electronic systems. Silicon carbide is particularly useful in high-voltage power converters, electric vehicle inverters and industrial power electronics due to its high breakdown strength and excellent thermal conductivity. Gallium nitride is widely used in high-frequency and high-efficiency power devices, including compact chargers, telecommunications infrastructure and power conversion systems. However, their performance advantages depend on device design, manufacturing quality and operating conditions.
      • Statement 3 is not correct: Silicon (Si) is an elemental semiconductor, not a compound semiconductor, because it consists of atoms of a single chemical element. It belongs to Group 14 of the periodic table and is the most widely used semiconductor material in integrated circuits, microprocessors, memory chips and solar cells. In contrast, compound semiconductors contain two or more different chemical elements. Examples include gallium arsenide (GaAs), gallium nitride (GaN), silicon carbide (SiC) and indium phosphide (InP). Compound semiconductors may offer specialised electronic and optical properties that make them suitable for high-frequency communication, optoelectronics and advanced power electronics. Therefore, the assertion that silicon contains two different elements is incorrect.
      • Recent Context: In 2026, India continued strengthening its domestic semiconductor ecosystem under the India Semiconductor Mission. In February 2026, the government reported that approved projects included a silicon carbide fabrication facility, reflecting the growing importance of wide-bandgap semiconductor materials. In May 2026, the government approved India's first commercial Mini/Micro-LED display manufacturing facility based on gallium nitride (GaN) technology. Further, in July 2026, the Union Cabinet approved Semicon 2.0, with an outlay of ₹1,27,500 crore to strengthen India's semiconductor design and manufacturing ecosystem.

      Consider the following geographical characteristics of an Indian state:

      1. यह लगभग 22° उत्तरी और 25° उत्तरी अक्षांशों के बीच स्थित है।

      निम्नलिखित में से किस राज्य का वर्णन किया जा रहा है?

      (a) मिजोरम

      (b) त्रिपुरा

      (c) मेघालय

      (d) असम

      Difficult
      Fundamental(F)

      • Clue 1 – Latitudinal location: Tripura lies approximately between 22°56′N and 24°32′N latitude and between 91°E and 92°22′E longitude. It is located in northeastern India, south of the Tropic of Cancer, which passes through neighbouring Mizoram. Its geographical location places it within a humid subtropical to tropical monsoon environment, favourable for dense vegetation and plantation crops. Hence, the first clue is consistent with Tripura.
      • Clue 2 – Forest cover: According to the India State of Forest Report 2023, Tripura has approximately 72.33% of its geographical area under forest cover. However, this clue alone is insufficient to identify the state because several northeastern states, including Mizoram, Manipur, Nagaland and Meghalaya, also have forest cover exceeding 70% of their geographical area. The distinction between absolute forest area and percentage forest cover is particularly important for UPSC. Hence, the second clue supports Tripura but does not uniquely establish its identity.
      • Clue 3 – International boundaries and rubber production: Tripura shares its international boundary with Bangladesh on three sides—north, west and south—while its northeastern and eastern margins connect with Assam and Mizoram. Approximately 84% of its total boundary is shared with Bangladesh. Tripura is also a major centre of natural rubber cultivation and has traditionally been India's second-largest natural rubber-producing state after Kerala. Its humid climate and suitable rainfall conditions support rubber plantations, which are an important component of the state's agricultural economy. This combination of geographical location and plantation agriculture clearly distinguishes Tripura from the other options. Hence, the third clue establishes Tripura as the correct answer.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.

      Consider the four locations P, Q, R and S shown on the schematic world map below.

      With reference to the locations marked above, consider the following statements:

      1. Locations P and Q experience sunrise at different times, even though they lie on the same latitude.

      2. Locations Q and S have the same local solar time but experience opposite seasons during much of the year.

      3. Locations P and R have the same duration of daylight on the June solstice because they lie on the same longitude.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) 1 only

      (b) 1 and 2 only

      (c) 2 and 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      नीचे दिए गए सांकेतिक विश्व मानचित्र में दर्शाए गए चार स्थानों P, Q, R और S पर विचार कीजिए।


      सांकेतिक मानचित्र — निर्देशांक स्थानों को निर्धारित करते हैं।

      उपर्युक्त चिह्नित स्थानों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. स्थान P और Q पर सूर्योदय अलग-अलग समय पर होता है, यद्यपि वे एक ही अक्षांश पर स्थित हैं।

      2. स्थान Q और S का स्थानीय सौर समय समान है, किंतु वर्ष के अधिकांश समय में वहाँ विपरीत ऋतुएँ होती हैं।

      3. स्थान P और R पर जून अयनांत के दिन दिनालोक की अवधि समान होती है, क्योंकि वे एक ही देशांतर पर स्थित हैं।


      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

      (a) केवल 1

      (b) केवल 1 और 2

      (c) केवल 2 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3 


      		 Geography b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:72 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct: Locations P and Q are situated at 30°N latitude but at different longitudes, 60°W and 60°E respectively. Since the Earth rotates from west to east, places located farther east experience solar events earlier than places to their west, when measured against a common time reference. The longitudinal difference between P and Q is 120°, corresponding to eight hours of difference in local solar time. Therefore, although both locations lie on the same latitude, they do not experience sunrise simultaneously. Hence, Statement 1 is correct.
      • Statement 2 is correct: Locations Q and S lie on the same longitude, 60°E, but on opposite sides of the Equator. Places on the same longitude have the same mean local solar time because solar time is primarily determined by longitude. However, seasonal conditions differ between the Northern and Southern Hemispheres due to the Earth's axial tilt and revolution around the Sun. For instance, when Q experiences the Northern Hemisphere summer, S experiences the Southern Hemisphere winter. Hence, Statement 2 is correct.
      • Statement 3 is not correct: Locations P and R lie on the same longitude, 60°W, but at 30°N and 30°S respectively. On the June solstice, the Northern Hemisphere is tilted towards the Sun, resulting in longer daylight hours at 30°N, while the Southern Hemisphere experiences shorter daylight hours at 30°S. Consequently, places on the same longitude do not necessarily have equal day lengths. Day length is influenced mainly by latitude and the Earth's seasonal position relative to the Sun. Hence, Statement 3 is incorrect.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.

      With reference to the theory of Plate Tectonics, consider the following statements:

      1. The lithospheric plates move over the underlying asthenosphere, which is relatively weak and capable of flowing over geological timescales.

      2. The formation of new oceanic crust at divergent plate boundaries is necessarily accompanied by an increase in the total surface area of the Earth.

      3. Earthquakes may occur at convergent, divergent and transform plate boundaries, whereas volcanic activity is not equally characteristic of all three types of boundaries.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) 1 only

      (b) 1 and 2 only

      (c) 2 and 3 only

      (d) 1 and 3 only

      प्लेट विवर्तनिकी के सिद्धांत के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. स्थलमंडलीय प्लेटें अपने नीचे स्थित दुर्बलतामंडल पर गति करती हैं, जो अपेक्षाकृत कमजोर है और भूवैज्ञानिक समयमान पर प्रवाहित होने में सक्षम है।

      2. अपसारी प्लेट सीमाओं पर नई महासागरीय भूपर्पटी के निर्माण के साथ पृथ्वी के कुल पृष्ठीय क्षेत्रफल में अनिवार्यतः वृद्धि होती है।

      3. अभिसारी, अपसारी और रूपांतरण प्लेट सीमाओं पर भूकंप आ सकते हैं, जबकि ज्वालामुखीय सक्रियता इन तीनों प्रकार की सीमाओं की समान रूप से विशिष्ट विशेषता नहीं है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं?

      (a) केवल 1

      (b) केवल 1 और 2

      (c)  केवल 2 और 3

      (d) केवल 1 और 3


      		 Geography d

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		d
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:52 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct: According to the theory of Plate Tectonics, Earth's lithosphere is divided into several rigid plates consisting of the crust and the uppermost solid mantle. These plates move relative to one another over the underlying asthenosphere, a mechanically weaker region of the upper mantle. Although the asthenosphere is predominantly solid, its rocks can undergo slow plastic deformation and flow over geological timescales. Plate movements are influenced by forces such as slab pull, ridge push and gravitational forces associated with mantle convection. Importantly, the asthenosphere is not a continuous ocean of molten magma, as is sometimes incorrectly assumed. Hence, Statement 1 is correct.
      • Statement 2 is incorrect: At divergent plate boundaries, lithospheric plates move away from one another, allowing mantle material to rise and undergo decompression melting. The resulting magma solidifies to form new oceanic crust, as observed along mid-ocean ridges such as the Mid-Atlantic Ridge. However, the creation of new oceanic crust does not necessarily increase Earth's total surface area because older oceanic lithosphere is recycled into the mantle at subduction zones associated with convergent boundaries. Thus, seafloor spreading and subduction are complementary processes within the global plate tectonic system. Hence, Statement 2 is incorrect.
      • Statement 3 is correct: Earthquakes can occur along all three major types of plate boundaries because tectonic stresses accumulate and are released through fault movement. At convergent boundaries, subduction or continental collision may generate powerful earthquakes, including deep-focus earthquakes in subduction zones. At divergent boundaries, earthquakes are generally shallow and associated with crustal extension. At transform boundaries, earthquakes occur as plates slide horizontally past each other. However, volcanism is not equally characteristic of all these boundaries. It is common at divergent boundaries and many subduction-related convergent boundaries, whereas typical transform boundaries do not generally produce significant volcanism because they do not inherently promote extensive mantle melting. Hence, Statement 3 is correct.
      • Hence, option (d) is the correct answer.

      The devastating flash floods in Nepal's Rasuwa region in August 2026 were primarily associated with which one of the following events?

      (a) Failure of a moraine-dammed glacial lake following the overtopping and erosion of its sedimentary barrier.

      (b) Sudden drainage of an ice-dammed glacial lake through channels formed beneath the glacier.

      (c) A massive rock–ice avalanche that mobilised debris and water, generating a destructive flood downstream.

      (d) An intense cloudburst over the upper Himalayan catchment causing rapid surface runoff and river flooding.

      अगस्त 2026 में नेपाल के रसुवा क्षेत्र में आई विनाशकारी आकस्मिक बाढ़ मुख्यतः निम्नलिखित में से किस घटना से संबद्ध थी?

      (a) हिमोढ़ से अवरुद्ध हिमनदीय झील के अवसादी अवरोध के ऊपर से जल बहने तथा उसके अपरदन के बाद बाँध का टूटना।

      (b) हिमनद के नीचे बने जलमार्गों से होकर, बर्फ द्वारा अवरुद्ध हिमनदीय झील का अचानक खाली हो जाना।

      (c) एक विशाल शैल-हिम हिमस्खलन, जिसने मलबे और जल को गतिशील कर निचले क्षेत्रों में विनाशकारी बाढ़ उत्पन्न की।

      (d) हिमालय के ऊपरी जलग्रहण क्षेत्र में तीव्र बादल फटना, जिससे तीव्र सतही अपवाह और नदी में बाढ़ उत्पन्न हुई।


      		 Geography c

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:28 Sec.
      • Option (a) is incorrect: Moraine-dammed glacial lakes are held back by naturally deposited glacial debris, including boulders, gravel, sand and other unconsolidated sediments. Their barriers may fail due to overtopping, internal erosion or slope instability. Although this is an important Himalayan GLOF mechanism, the August 2026 Nepal flood was not identified as the breach of a pre-existing moraine-dammed lake.
      • Option (b) is incorrect: Ice-dammed glacial lakes are impounded by glacier ice and may undergo rapid drainage through channels beneath or within the glacier, sometimes producing jökulhlaups. However, the reported Nepal event was not primarily attributed to the sudden drainage of such a lake.
      • Option (c) is correct: On 26 August 2026, a large mass of glacier ice and rock detached in the upper Lhende–Bhote Koshi watershed near the Nepal–China border. The avalanche descended along steep terrain, entraining sediments, debris and water, producing a destructive flood and debris flow downstream. Early assessments also examined temporary river blockage and subsequent water release, although later research suggested that the principal flood wave may have developed through rapid, largely continuous debris-flow movement. Hence, option (c) is correct.
      • Option (d) is incorrect: Cloudbursts can cause devastating flash floods in mountainous terrain because intense rainfall rapidly increases runoff. However, available investigations indicated a large rock–ice mass movement rather than an exceptionally intense local rainfall event as the primary trigger. Hence, option (d) is incorrect.
      • Hence, option (c) is the correct answer.

      Consider the following scientific observations:

      Observation I: A narrow band of moisture-laden air moves across the ocean and produces intense rainfall when it encounters a mountain range.

      Observation II: Increasing freshwater input into the North Atlantic reduces the salinity of surface seawater and may weaken the sinking of dense water.

      Based on the above observations, which one of the following conclusions is most scientifically appropriate?

      (a) Observation I describes an atmospheric river, while Observation II indicates a possible weakening of the Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation.

      (b) Observation I describes an atmospheric river, while Observation II indicates a strengthening of the Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation.

      (c) Observation I describes a jet stream, while Observation II indicates a possible weakening of the Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation.

      (d) Observation I describes a jet stream, while Observation II indicates a strengthening of the Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation.

      निम्नलिखित वैज्ञानिक प्रेक्षणों पर विचार कीजिए:

      प्रेक्षण-I: आर्द्रता से भरी वायु की एक संकीर्ण पट्टी महासागर के ऊपर से गुजरती है और पर्वत शृंखला से टकराने पर तीव्र वर्षा करती है।

      प्रेक्षण-II: उत्तरी अटलांटिक में मीठे जल के बढ़ते अंतर्वाह से सतही समुद्री जल की लवणता घटती है तथा सघन जल के नीचे धँसने की प्रक्रिया कमजोर पड़ सकती है।

      उपर्युक्त प्रेक्षणों के आधार पर, निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक निष्कर्ष वैज्ञानिक दृष्टि से सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त है?

      (a) प्रेक्षण-I एक वायुमंडलीय नदी का वर्णन करता है, जबकि प्रेक्षण-II अटलांटिक मेरिडियनल ओवरटर्निंग परिसंचरण के संभावित रूप से कमजोर पड़ने का संकेत देता है।

      (b) प्रेक्षण-I एक वायुमंडलीय नदी का वर्णन करता है, जबकि प्रेक्षण-II अटलांटिक मेरिडियनल ओवरटर्निंग परिसंचरण के सुदृढ़ होने का संकेत देता है।

      (c) प्रेक्षण-I एक जेट धारा का वर्णन करता है, जबकि प्रेक्षण-II अटलांटिक मेरिडियनल ओवरटर्निंग परिसंचरण के संभावित रूप से कमजोर पड़ने का संकेत देता है।

      (d) प्रेक्षण-I एक जेट धारा का वर्णन करता है, जबकि प्रेक्षण-II अटलांटिक मेरिडियनल ओवरटर्निंग परिसंचरण के सुदृढ़ होने का संकेत देता है।


      		 Geography a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:41 Sec.
      • Observation I describes an atmospheric river: Atmospheric rivers are long, relatively narrow corridors of concentrated water vapour transport in the atmosphere. They commonly transport substantial amounts of moisture from lower to higher latitudes. When moisture-laden air encounters a mountain range, it is forced to rise, cool and condense, resulting in orographic precipitation. Atmospheric rivers can therefore produce intense rainfall, snowfall, flooding and landslides, although weaker events can also replenish water resources. The Pineapple Express, which transports moisture from the tropical Pacific towards the western coast of North America, is a well-known example. Hence, Observation I describes an atmospheric river.
      • Observation II indicates a possible weakening of the AMOC: The Atlantic Meridional Overturning Circulation is a large-scale system of ocean circulation that transports relatively warm surface waters northwards across the Atlantic and returns colder, deeper waters southwards. In the high-latitude North Atlantic, surface seawater cools, becomes denser and may sink to form deep water. However, an increased supply of freshwater from melting glaciers, the Greenland Ice Sheet or increased precipitation can lower surface salinity and reduce seawater density. This can inhibit deep-water formation and potentially weaken the overturning circulation. Hence, Observation II indicates a possible weakening of the AMOC.

      Consider the following statements:

      Assertion (A): El Niño generally weakens both the southwest and northeast monsoons, increasing the likelihood of drought-like conditions across all parts of India.

      Reason (R): During El Niño, changes in the Walker Circulation can suppress atmospheric convection over parts of the Indian monsoon region.

      Which one of the following is correct in respect of the above statements?

      (a) Both Assertion (A) and Reason (R) are correct, and Reason (R) is the correct explanation of Assertion (A).

      (b) Both Assertion (A) and Reason (R) are correct, but Reason (R) is not the correct explanation of Assertion (A).

      (c) Assertion (A) is correct, but Reason (R) is incorrect.

      (d) Assertion (A) is incorrect, but Reason (R) is correct.

      निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      अभिकथन (A): एल नीनो सामान्यतः दक्षिण-पश्चिम और उत्तर-पूर्व, दोनों मानसूनों को कमजोर करता है, जिससे भारत के सभी भागों में सूखे जैसी स्थितियों की संभावना बढ़ जाती है।

      कारण (R): एल नीनो के दौरान वॉकर परिसंचरण में होने वाले परिवर्तन भारतीय मानसून क्षेत्र के कुछ भागों में वायुमंडलीय संवहन को दबा सकते हैं।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक सही है?

      (a) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या है।

      (b) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या नहीं है।

      (c) A सही है, किंतु R गलत है।

      (d) A गलत है, किंतु R सही है।


      		 Geography d

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:37 Sec.
      • Assertion (A) is incorrect: El Niño does not uniformly weaken both the southwest and northeast monsoons over India. It is generally associated with an increased probability of deficient southwest monsoon rainfall during June–September. However, the northeast monsoon during October–December, which contributes significantly to rainfall over Tamil Nadu and adjoining southeastern peninsular India, often exhibits a positive association with El Niño conditions. Some El Niño years may experience enhanced northeast monsoon rainfall over Tamil Nadu despite deficient southwest monsoon rainfall elsewhere. Furthermore, other factors, including the Indian Ocean Dipole, influence regional rainfall patterns. Therefore, El Niño does not necessarily produce drought-like conditions across all parts of India. Hence, Assertion (A) is incorrect.
      • Reason (R) is correct: During El Niño, weakening of the equatorial Pacific trade winds allows warm surface waters to spread towards the central and eastern equatorial Pacific. Consequently, the main region of tropical convection shifts eastwards, altering the Walker Circulation. These changes can favour anomalous descending air over parts of the western Pacific and adjoining Indo-Pacific region, suppressing convection and contributing to weaker southwest monsoon rainfall over India. However, the effects of these atmospheric changes vary geographically and seasonally. Hence, Reason (R) is correct.
      • Hence, option (d) is the correct answer.

      5. वधावन

      6. पारादीप

      उपर्युक्त में से कौन-से महापत्तन हैं अथवा भारत सरकार द्वारा महापत्तनों के रूप में अधिसूचित किए गए हैं?

      (a) केवल 1, 2, 3 और 6

      		 Geography c

      • Mundra Port (1) is incorrect. Located in Gujarat, Mundra is a major commercial port in terms of cargo handling, but it is not classified as a statutory Major Port. It is a privately operated, state-regulated non-major port. This distinction between commercial size and statutory classification is an important UPSC trap.
      • Kamarajar Port (2) is correct. Located at Ennore in Tamil Nadu, Kamarajar is a Major Port. It is notable for being India's first Major Port operating under a corporatised model.

      • Thus, ports 2, 4, 5 and 6 satisfy the conditions.
      • Hence, option (c) is the correct answer.

      Consider the following statements:

      Assertion (A): In certain high-latitude oceanic regions, cold and saline surface waters become sufficiently dense to sink and contribute to the formation of deep ocean currents.

      Reason (R): The density of seawater is influenced by its temperature and salinity, with decreasing temperature and increasing salinity generally favouring higher density.

      Which one of the following statements is correct?

      (a) Both Assertion (A) and Reason (R) are correct, and Reason (R) explains Assertion (A).

      (b) Both Assertion (A) and Reason (R) are correct, but Reason (R) does not explain Assertion (A).

      (c) Assertion (A) is correct, but Reason (R) is incorrect.

      निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      अभिकथन (A): उच्च अक्षांशों वाले कुछ महासागरीय क्षेत्रों में ठंडा और लवणीय सतही जल पर्याप्त रूप से सघन होकर नीचे धँसता है तथा गहरी महासागरीय धाराओं के निर्माण में योगदान देता है।

      कारण (R): समुद्री जल का घनत्व उसके तापमान और लवणता से प्रभावित होता है तथा सामान्यतः तापमान में कमी और लवणता में वृद्धि से घनत्व बढ़ता है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक सही है? 

      (a) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या है।

      (b) A और R दोनों सही हैं तथा R, A की सही व्याख्या नहीं है।

      (c) A सही है, किंतु R गलत है।

      (d) A गलत है, किंतु R सही है।


      		 Geography a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:38 Sec.
      • Assertion (A) is correct. Thermohaline circulation refers to large-scale ocean circulation associated with differences in seawater density arising from temperature (thermo) and salinity (haline). In certain regions of the North Atlantic and around Antarctica, surface waters become dense enough to sink and contribute to deep-water formation.
      • Reason (R) is correct. Seawater density generally increases when temperature decreases and when salinity increases. In polar environments, intense cooling makes surface water denser. During sea-ice formation, salt is largely excluded from the growing ice and can increase the salinity of surrounding seawater through a process known as brine rejection.
      • Two important deep-water masses are North Atlantic Deep Water (NADW) and Antarctic Bottom Water (AABW). These water masses contribute to the global redistribution of heat, dissolved oxygen, carbon and nutrients.
      • Therefore, the Reason directly explains the physical mechanism described in the Assertion.
      • Hence, option (a) is the correct answer.

      With reference to critical minerals and their strategic importance, consider the following statements:

      1. A mineral may be classified as critical even when it is geologically abundant, if its supply chain is highly concentrated or vulnerable to disruption.

      2. All rare-earth elements are included in India's identified critical minerals, but all critical minerals are not necessarily rare-earth elements.

      3. The classification of a mineral as critical is universal and remains unchanged irrespective of a country's industrial requirements and technological capabilities.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      महत्वपूर्ण खनिजों और उनके रणनीतिक महत्व के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. किसी खनिज को भूवैज्ञानिक दृष्टि से प्रचुर मात्रा में उपलब्ध होने पर भी महत्वपूर्ण के रूप में वर्गीकृत किया जा सकता है, यदि उसकी आपूर्ति शृंखला अत्यधिक संकेंद्रित हो अथवा उसमें व्यवधान की आशंका हो।

      2. सभी दुर्लभ मृदा तत्त्व भारत द्वारा चिह्नित महत्वपूर्ण खनिजों में शामिल हैं, किंतु सभी महत्वपूर्ण खनिजों का दुर्लभ मृदा तत्त्व होना आवश्यक नहीं है।

      3. किसी खनिज का महत्वपूर्ण के रूप में वर्गीकरण सार्वभौमिक होता है और किसी देश की औद्योगिक आवश्यकताओं तथा तकनीकी क्षमताओं की परवाह किए बिना अपरिवर्तित रहता है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं? 

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (b) केवल 2 और 3

      (c)  केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Geography a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:27 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct. Critical minerals are minerals considered essential for a country's economic development, technological advancement and national security, whose supply chains are vulnerable to disruption. The classification of a mineral as critical does not necessarily imply geological scarcity. A mineral may be relatively abundant in the Earth's crust but still be classified as critical because its economically viable extraction, refining or processing is concentrated in a few countries. For example, rare-earth elements are relatively widespread in the Earth's crust, but their extraction, separation and refining involve technological complexities, while processing capabilities are concentrated in a limited number of countries. Consequently, geopolitical tensions, export restrictions, trade disruptions or inadequate domestic processing capabilities may threaten their availability. Thus, mineral criticality is determined by a combination of economic importance and supply risk, rather than geological abundance alone. Hence, Statement 1 is correct.
      • Statement 2 is correct, with a qualification regarding terminology. In 2023, India's Ministry of Mines released a list identifying 30 critical minerals and mineral groups, including Rare Earth Elements (REEs), Platinum Group Elements (PGEs), lithium, cobalt, nickel, graphite, gallium and germanium. Rare-earth elements comprise 17 metallic elements, including the 15 lanthanides along with scandium and yttrium. The Indian list identifies REEs collectively as a critical mineral group rather than enumerating each element separately. However, critical minerals are not restricted to rare-earth elements. For example, lithium is important for rechargeable batteries, cobalt and nickel are used in various battery technologies, and graphite is used in lithium-ion battery anodes. None of these is a rare-earth element. Therefore, all rare-earth elements are covered under India's identified REE group, but all critical minerals are not necessarily rare-earth elements. Hence, Statement 2 is correct.
      • Statement 3 is not correct. The classification of minerals as critical is neither universal nor permanently fixed. It varies across countries depending on their industrial structure, strategic priorities, technological capabilities, domestic mineral resources, import dependence and supply-chain vulnerabilities. For instance, a mineral essential for a country's semiconductor or defence industry may be classified as critical because of its strategic importance, even if another country does not face the same supply risk. Moreover, criticality can change over time as new technologies emerge, alternative materials become available, recycling improves or geopolitical conditions alter supply chains. The Government of India's expert committee has accordingly recommended periodic up
      93

      The 2026 Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine was awarded to Karl Deisseroth, Peter Hegemann and Georg Nagel for discoveries concerning light-gated ion channels and optogenetics.

      1. इसमें चयनित कोशिकाओं में प्रकाश-संवेदनशील प्रोटीनों को कूटबद्ध करने वाले जीन प्रविष्ट कराए जाते हैं, जिससे उनकी सक्रियता को प्रकाश द्वारा प्रभावित किया जा सकता है।

      2. यह तकनीक उन प्रकाश-संवेदनशील आयन चैनलों पर आधारित है, जिनकी पहचान मूलतः हरे शैवाल जैसे सूक्ष्मजीवों में की गई थी।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-सा/से सही है/हैं?

      (a) केवल 1

      (b)  केवल 2

      (c) 1 और 2 दोनों

      (d) न तो 1, न ही 2 


      		 Science And Technology c

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Incorrect
      :-0.66 Marks
      Time:24 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct. Optogenetics combines genetic engineering and optics. Genes encoding light-sensitive proteins, such as channelrhodopsins, are expressed in target neurons. Exposure to an appropriate wavelength of light can then change their electrical activity.
      • Statement 2 is correct. Channelrhodopsins are naturally occurring light-sensitive proteins found in organisms including the green alga Chlamydomonas. Some operate as light-gated ion channels, allowing ions to cross membranes when illuminated.
      • Statement 3 is correct. The major breakthrough of optogenetics is its spatial and temporal precision. Researchers can investigate the functioning of specific neural circuits instead of stimulating large regions of the brain indiscriminately.
      • The technique is important for understanding memory, behaviour, epilepsy and neurodegenerative disorders. Clinical applications remain under development; it is not a universal cure for neurological diseases.
      • Hence, option (c) is the correct answer.

      The 2026 Nobel Prize in Chemistry recognised Henri B. Kagan and Kenso Soai for discoveries concerning nonlinear effects and autocatalysis in asymmetric organic synthesis.

      A research team observes that a reaction produces two mirror-image forms of a molecule. A slight initial excess of one form, under suitable reaction conditions, results in a much larger excess of the same form as the reaction progresses.

      Which one of the following best explains this observation?

      (a) The reaction necessarily converts all molecules into an achiral product with no optical activity.

      (b) An asymmetric autocatalytic pathway may amplify an initially small imbalance between the two enantiomers.

      (c) The two mirror-image forms must have entirely different molecular formulas and atomic compositions.

      (d) The observed increase establishes that every asymmetric reaction requires an enzyme catalyst.

      वर्ष 2026 का रसायन विज्ञान का नोबेल पुरस्कार हेनरी बी. कागन और केन्सो सोआई को असममित कार्बनिक संश्लेषण में अरैखिक प्रभावों तथा स्व-उत्प्रेरण से संबंधित खोजों के लिए प्रदान किया गया।

      एक अनुसंधान दल प्रेक्षण करता है कि किसी अभिक्रिया में एक अणु के दो दर्पण-प्रतिबिंब रूप उत्पन्न होते हैं। उपयुक्त अभिक्रिया परिस्थितियों में किसी एक रूप की थोड़ी-सी प्रारंभिक अधिकता, अभिक्रिया आगे बढ़ने पर उसी रूप की काफी बड़ी अधिकता में परिणत हो जाती है।

      निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक इस प्रेक्षण की सर्वाधिक उपयुक्त व्याख्या करता है

      (a) यह अभिक्रिया सभी अणुओं को अनिवार्यतः ऐसे अकाइरल उत्पाद में बदल देती है, जिसमें कोई प्रकाशिक सक्रियता नहीं होती है।

      (b) कोई असममित स्व-उत्प्रेरक मार्ग दोनों प्रतिबिंब समावयवियों के बीच प्रारंभिक रूप से विद्यमान छोटे असंतुलन को बढ़ा सकता है।

      (c) दोनों दर्पण-प्रतिबिंब रूपों के आणविक सूत्र और परमाण्विक संघटन पूर्णतः भिन्न होने ही चाहिए।

      (d) प्रेक्षित वृद्धि यह स्थापित करती है कि प्रत्येक असममित अभिक्रिया के लिए एंजाइम उत्प्रेरक आवश्यक होता है।


      		 Science And Technology b

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:58 Sec.
      • The central concept is chirality, or the property of a molecule existing in two mirror-image forms that cannot be perfectly superimposed. These forms are known as enantiomers.
      • Option (b) is correct. In asymmetric autocatalysis, a reaction product can act as a catalyst for its own formation. Under appropriate conditions, this can amplify an initially small enantiomeric imbalance, producing a substantially greater excess of one mirror-image form.
      • Option (a) is incorrect because asymmetric synthesis seeks to favour one enantiomer rather than eliminate chirality altogether. Option (c) is incorrect because enantiomers have the same molecular formula and connectivity. Option (d) is incorrect because asymmetric catalysis can involve metal complexes, small organic molecules or other catalysts; enzymes are not universally required.
      • This field has major implications for pharmaceutical manufacturing because enantiomers of the same drug may exhibit different biological activities.

      :2 Marks
      Time:45 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct. Microgravity provides an opportunity to study fluid behaviour and materials processing with greatly reduced buoyancy-driven convection and sedimentation.
      • Statement 2 is correct. Extended exposure to microgravity can result in muscle atrophy, bone mineral loss and changes in cardiovascular physiology. Understanding these changes is essential for future human space exploration.
      • Statement 3 is incorrect. A spacecraft in low Earth orbit is not outside Earth's gravitational field. It experiences substantial gravitational attraction. The apparent weightlessness arises primarily because the spacecraft and objects within it are in continuous free fall around Earth.
      • Statement 4 is correct. Microgravity can alter the growth conditions of some protein crystals, which may help researchers investigate their structures and improve crystallisation methods.
      • According to India's 2026 parliamentary updates, the Bharatiya Antariksh Station is planned as a five-module station, with the first module targeted for 2028 and the complete station targeted for 2035.
      • Hence, option (c) is the correct answer.
      96

      With reference to the working of a pressure cooker, consider the following statements:

      1. An increase in pressure inside the cooker raises the boiling point of water, allowing food to be cooked at temperatures higher than 100°C.

      2. At high altitudes, food generally takes longer to cook in an open vessel because the atmospheric pressure is lower.

      3. The cooking time in a pressure cooker is reduced primarily because water acquires a higher specific heat capacity under pressure.

      Which of the statements given above are correct?

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 1 and 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      प्रेशर कुकर की कार्यप्रणाली के संदर्भ में, निम्नलिखित कथनों पर विचार कीजिए:

      1. कुकर के भीतर दाब बढ़ने से जल का क्वथनांक बढ़ जाता है, जिससे भोजन 100°C से अधिक तापमान पर पकाया जा सकता है।

      2. अधिक ऊँचाई वाले स्थानों पर वायुमंडलीय दाब कम होने के कारण खुले पात्र में भोजन पकने में सामान्यतः अधिक समय लगता है।

      3. प्रेशर कुकर में भोजन पकने का समय मुख्यतः इसलिए कम हो जाता है, क्योंकि दाब के अंतर्गत जल की विशिष्ट ऊष्मा धारिता बढ़ जाती है।

      उपर्युक्त कथनों में से कौन-से सही हैं

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (b) केवल 2 और 3

      (c) केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Basic Science (Chemistry) a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:111 Sec.
      • Statement 1 is correct. The boiling point of a liquid is the temperature at which its vapour pressure equals the surrounding pressure. Inside a pressure cooker, the pressure increases above atmospheric pressure, raising the boiling point of water. Consequently, food can cook at temperatures exceeding 100°C, accelerating many cooking processes.
      • Statement 2 is correct. At higher altitudes, atmospheric pressure decreases, causing water to boil at a lower temperature. Since boiling water is relatively cooler, cooking processes requiring sustained heat generally take longer. Pressure cookers are particularly useful in such regions.
      • Statement 3 is incorrect. The principal reason for faster cooking is the higher cooking temperature achieved under pressure, not an increase in the specific heat capacity of water.
      • Hence, option (a) is the correct answer.
      97

      A student conducts an experiment using an induction cooktop and observes the following:

      • A flat-bottomed iron vessel becomes hot when placed on the cooktop.
      • An ordinary glass vessel does not heat directly when placed on the same cooktop.
      • The cooktop does not rely on a conventional exposed heating element to transfer heat to the vessel.

      Which one of the following most plausibly explains these observations?

      (a) The cooktop produces infrared radiation that is selectively absorbed by iron but completely reflected by glass.

      (b) The cooktop generates an alternating magnetic field that induces electrical currents in suitable cookware, resulting in heat generation.

      (c) The cooktop creates an electrostatic field that transfers heat through the movement of stationary electrical charges.

      (d) The cooktop heats vessels primarily through thermal conduction from its glass surface, irrespective of their material properties.

      एक विद्यार्थी इंडक्शन कुकटॉप का उपयोग करके एक प्रयोग करता है और निम्नलिखित प्रेक्षण करता है:

      • कुकटॉप पर रखे जाने पर समतल तली वाला लोहे का पात्र गर्म हो जाता है।

      • उसी कुकटॉप पर रखा साधारण काँच का पात्र सीधे गर्म नहीं होता है।

      • पात्र तक ऊष्मा पहुँचाने के लिए कुकटॉप किसी पारंपरिक अनावृत तापन तत्त्व पर निर्भर नहीं करता है।

      निम्नलिखित में से कौन-सा एक इन प्रेक्षणों की सर्वाधिक संभावित व्याख्या करता है?


      (a) कुकटॉप अवरक्त विकिरण उत्पन्न करता है, जिसे लोहा चयनात्मक रूप से अवशोषित करता है, किंतु काँच पूर्णतः परावर्तित कर देता है।

      (b) कुकटॉप एक प्रत्यावर्ती चुंबकीय क्षेत्र उत्पन्न करता है, जो उपयुक्त पाक-पात्रों में विद्युत धाराएँ प्रेरित करता है और फलस्वरूप ऊष्मा उत्पन्न होती है।  

      (c) कुकटॉप एक स्थिरवैद्युत क्षेत्र उत्पन्न करता है, जो स्थिर विद्युत आवेशों की गति के माध्यम से ऊष्मा का स्थानांतरण करता है। 

      (d) कुकटॉप पात्रों के पदार्थगत गुणों की परवाह किए बिना मुख्यतः अपनी काँच की सतह से ऊष्मीय चालन द्वारा उन्हें गर्म करता है। 


      		 Basic Science (Physics) b

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:29 Sec.
      • Option (b) is correct. An induction cooktop operates on the principle of electromagnetic induction. An alternating electric current flowing through a coil produces a changing magnetic field.
      • When suitable cookware is placed within this field, electric currents known as eddy currents are induced in the conducting material. The electrical resistance of the vessel converts electrical energy into heat. In ferromagnetic cookware, magnetic hysteresis may also contribute to heating.
      • Option (a) is incorrect because infrared radiation is not the principal operating mechanism. Option (c) is incorrect because electromagnetic induction involves changing magnetic fields, not stationary electrostatic charges. Option (d) is incorrect because the vessel itself is principally heated by electromagnetic induction, rather than by heat conducted from a conventional hotplate.
      • UPSC trap: Not all metallic vessels are equally suitable for induction cooking. Material composition and magnetic properties, along with cookware design, determine compatibility.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.
      98

      Consider the following observations:

      Statement I: Water droplets often appear on the outer surface of a glass containing a cold beverage, even when no liquid is spilled.

      Statement II: Water kept in a porous earthen pot may remain cooler than the surrounding air under suitable atmospheric conditions.

      Statement III: Evaporative cooling is generally more effective under conditions of high relative humidity than under conditions of low relative humidity, other factors remaining the same.

      Which of the following conclusions can be correctly drawn?

      1. Statement I can be explained by condensation of atmospheric water vapour when the glass surface is sufficiently cold.

      2. Statement II can be explained by the absorption of latent heat during evaporation of water.

      3. Statement III is consistent with the relationship between humidity and the rate of evaporation.

      Select the correct answer using the code given below:

      (a) 1 and 2 only

      (b) 2 and 3 only

      (c) 1 and 3 only

      (d) 1, 2 and 3

      निम्नलिखित प्रेक्षणों पर विचार कीजिए:

      कथन-I: ठंडा पेय रखने वाले गिलास की बाहरी सतह पर प्रायः जल की बूँदें दिखाई देती हैं, भले ही कोई द्रव बाहर न छलका हो।

      कथन-II: उपयुक्त वायुमंडलीय परिस्थितियों में सरंध्र मिट्टी के घड़े में रखा जल आसपास की वायु की तुलना में अधिक ठंडा रह सकता है।

      कथन-III: अन्य कारक समान रहने पर, वाष्पीकरणीय शीतलन सामान्यतः निम्न सापेक्ष आर्द्रता की तुलना में उच्च सापेक्ष आर्द्रता की दशाओं में अधिक प्रभावी होता है।

      निम्नलिखित में से कौन-से निष्कर्ष सही रूप से निकाले जा सकते हैं?

      1. कथन-I की व्याख्या गिलास की सतह पर्याप्त रूप से ठंडी होने पर वायुमंडलीय जलवाष्प के संघनन से की जा सकती है।

      2. कथन-II की व्याख्या जल के वाष्पीकरण के दौरान गुप्त ऊष्मा के अवशोषण से की जा सकती है।

      3. कथन-III, आर्द्रता और वाष्पीकरण की दर के बीच संबंध के अनुरूप है।

      नीचे दिए गए कूट का प्रयोग करके सही उत्तर चुनिए।

      (a) केवल 1 और 2

      (b) केवल 2 और 3

      (c) केवल 1 और 3

      (d) 1, 2 और 3


      		 Basic Science (Chemistry) a

      Medium
      Fundamental(F)
      Detailed Analysis       		a
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:35 Sec.
      • Statement I is correct; hence Conclusion 1 is correct. Atmospheric air contains water vapour. When air near the cold glass is cooled below its dew-point temperature, some of the water vapour condenses into liquid droplets on the outer surface.
      • Statement II is correct; hence Conclusion 2 is correct. Earthen pots have microscopic pores through which a small amount of water can reach the outer surface and evaporate. Evaporation requires latent heat, which is drawn from the water and its surroundings, thereby producing a cooling effect.
      • Statement III is incorrect; hence Conclusion 3 is incorrect. Evaporative cooling generally becomes less effective as relative humidity increases because the surrounding air has a reduced capacity to accept additional water vapour. This is why desert coolers generally work better in hot, dry climates than in hot, humid climates.
      • These phenomena illustrate three important concepts: condensation, latent heat and relative humidity.
      • Hence, option (a) is the correct answer.
      99

      Who among the following became the first woman officer from the Indian Army to serve as Aide-de-Camp (ADC) to the President of India in 2026?

      (a) Lieutenant Commander Yashasvi Solanki

      (b) Major Navya Shekhawat

      (c) Captain Shiva Chouhan

      (d) Lieutenant General Sadhna Saxena Nair

       निम्नलिखित में से कौन वर्ष 2026 में भारत के राष्ट्रपति की परिसहाय (ADC) के रूप में सेवा देने वाली भारतीय थल सेना की पहली महिला अधिकारी बनीं?

      (a) लेफ्टिनेंट कमांडर यशस्वी सोलंकी

      (b)  मेजर नव्या शेखावत

      (c) कैप्टन शिवा चौहान

      (d) लेफ्टिनेंट जनरल साधना सक्सेना नायर


      		 Polity And Governance b

      Easy
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:15 Sec.
      • Major Navya Shekhawat became the first woman officer from the Indian Army to serve as Aide-de-Camp (ADC) to President Droupadi Murmu in 2026. She belongs to the Army Service Corps.  An Aide-de-Camp (ADC) is a commissioned military officer who assists the President in official engagements, ceremonial functions, military protocol and coordination. 
      • - Lieutenant Commander Yashasvi Solanki: First woman officer from any of India's three armed services to become the President's ADC (2025).
      • - The President is traditionally assisted by five ADCs — three from the Army and one each from the Navy and Air Force.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.
      100

      With reference to satellite communication services in India, what does GMPCS authorisation, recently in the news in connection with Starlink, primarily signify?

      (a) Permission granted by ISRO to launch communication satellites from Indian territory.

      (b) Regulatory authorisation granted by the Department of Telecommunications to provide satellite-based communication services in India.

      (c) Allocation of satellite spectrum by TRAI for commercial broadband services.

      (d) Certification granted by IN-SPACe exclusively for operating Low Earth Orbit satellites.

      भारत में उपग्रह संचार सेवाओं के संदर्भ में, हाल ही में स्टारलिंक के संबंध में समाचारों में रहा GMPCS प्राधिकार मुख्यतः किसका द्योतक है?

      (a)  भारतीय राज्यक्षेत्र से संचार उपग्रहों का प्रक्षेपण करने के लिए इसरो द्वारा दी गई अनुमति।

      (b) भारत में उपग्रह-आधारित संचार सेवाएँ प्रदान करने के लिए दूरसंचार विभाग द्वारा दिया गया विनियामक प्राधिकार।

      (c) वाणिज्यिक ब्रॉडबैंड सेवाओं के लिए ट्राई द्वारा उपग्रह स्पेक्ट्रम का आवंटन।

      (d)  केवल निम्न भू-कक्षा के उपग्रहों के परिचालन के लिए इन-स्पेस द्वारा प्रदान किया गया प्रमाणन।


      		 Science And Technology b

      Medium
      Current Affairs(CA)
      Detailed Analysis       		b
      Correct
      :2 Marks
      Time:24 Sec.
      • Option (b) is correct. GMPCS stands for Global Mobile Personal Communication by Satellite. The Department of Telecommunications (DoT) grants GMPCS authorisation to eligible service providers to offer satellite-based communication services in India, subject to regulatory conditions.
      • Option (a) is incorrect. GMPCS authorisation does not grant permission to launch satellites. Space activities are regulated separately through the applicable Indian space-sector framework.
      • Option (c) is incorrect. TRAI provides recommendations on telecommunications regulation, including spectrum-related matters. It does not itself assign satellite spectrum. Spectrum assignment falls under the Government of India's regulatory framework.
      • Option (d) is incorrect. IN-SPACe regulates and authorises specified space activities. GMPCS authorisation is not issued by IN-SPACe and is not restricted exclusively to LEO satellite systems.
      • UPSC Prelims takeaway: GMPCS authorisation is distinct from spectrum allocation and does not, by itself, permit the immediate commencement of commercial satellite communication services.
      • Hence, option (b) is the correct answer.
